# Kod va hujjat auditi — 2026-08-26

Butun repo (backend, frontend, infra, bridge-agent, `docs/`) fayl-ma-fayl o'qib
chiqilgan. Har bir topilma ikki bosqichdan o'tgan:

1. **Adversarial tekshiruv** — boshqa agent topilmani RAD ETISHGA harakat qilgan;
   ishonchsizlari tashlangan.
2. **Mexanik dalil darvozasi** — topilmaning `dalil` matnidagi har bir iqtibos
   manba faylda so'zma-so'z borligi `grep -F` bilan tekshirilgan. Modelning
   o'z bahosi emas, skript natijasi.

Ya'ni quyidagi har bir satrda keltirilgan kod/hujjat iqtiboslari haqiqiy.
**Iqtibos haqiqiy bo'lishi — undan chiqarilgan XULOSA to'g'ri degani emas.**
Tuzatishdan oldin har birini o'qib chiqing.

## Jami

- Topilma: **804** (unikal `fayl:qator` — 785)
- Jiddiylik: **70 yuqori** · 308 o'rta · 426 past

| Tur | Soni | | Qism | Soni |
| --- | ---: | --- | --- | ---: |
| bug | 235 | | backend | 370 |
| doc-drift | 183 | | frontend | 348 |
| dead-code | 141 | | docs | 56 |
| cleanup | 98 | | infra | 22 |
| dry | 90 | | bridge-agent | 8 |
| overengineering | 57 | |  |  |

Tur ma'nolari: `bug` — haqiqiy nuqson · `doc-drift` — hujjat kodga zid ·
`dead-code` — o'lik kod · `dry` — takror mantiq · `cleanup` — yolg'on/ortiqcha
izoh · `overengineering` — ortiqcha abstraksiya.

Metod va xarajat tafsiloti: shu hujjat oxirida.

# Yuqori jiddiylik (70)


## backend

### `backend/app/api/routes/login.py:72` — BUG · 3 ta mustaqil agent tasdiqladi

The one-time-use tombstone for a rotated refresh token expires after 7 days while device refresh tokens (TERMINAL/TABLO) are valid for 90 days, so an already-rotated device refresh token can be replayed after day 7 without triggering revocation.

**Hujjat:** docs/02-backend-core.md:175 (`create_refresh_token(data, days=None)` — ... `routes/login.py` qurilma akkauntlariga ... `days=settings.DEVICE_REFRESH_TOKEN_EXPIRE_DAYS` (90 kun) uzatadi)

**Dalil:** login.py:40 `REFRESH_MAX_AGE = REFRESH_TOKEN_EXPIRE_DAYS * 24 * 60 * 60`; login.py:71-72 `# Keep the tombstone exactly as long as the token could still be valid.` / `await rate_limiter.client.set(_USED_JTI_PREFIX + jti, 1, ex=REFRESH_MAX_AGE)`. login.py:53-58 `days = settings.DEVICE_REFRESH_TOKEN_EXPIRE_DAYS if device else REFRESH_TOKEN_EXPIRE_DAYS` / `set_auth_cookie(response, REFRESH_COOKIE_NAME, refresh_token, days * 24 * 60 * 60)`. config.py:41 `DEVICE_REFRESH_TOKEN_EXPIRE_DAYS: int = 90`, config.py:36 `REFRESH_TOKEN_EXPIRE_DAYS: int = 7`.

**Tuzatish:** Set the tombstone TTL to `max(REFRESH_TOKEN_EXPIRE_DAYS, settings.DEVICE_REFRESH_TOKEN_EXPIRE_DAYS) * 24 * 60 * 60`, or pass the issuing token's own lifetime into `_mark_refresh_token_used(jti, ttl_seconds)` from `refresh_access_token`.

### `backend/app/api/routes/orders.py:623` — BUG

Enabling a post checkbox for a standard part appends the new route step after the auto-added TMO/UPAKOVKA tail, so strict stage order requires the part to pass TMO before that step and the scan is permanently rejected with E004.

**Hujjat:** docs/03-backend-api.md 325-qator ('Yangi qadam qo'shilganda `seq` = detalning mavjud maksimumidan keyingisi')

**Dalil:** Code (orders.py:623-630): `                next_seq = (\n                    await db.execute(\n                        select(func.coalesce(func.max(PartRoute.seq), 0) + 1).where(\n                            PartRoute.part_id == part.id,`
Ingest always terminates the AUTO route with the tail (backend/app/services/giblab/ingest.py:396-400): `            if packaging:\n                steps.append((15, PostCode.UPAKOVKA, 1))\n            # TMO — eng oxirgi qadam ...\n            steps.append((16, PostCode.TMO, 1))`
Strict order compares by seq (backend/app/services/scanning.py:565): `                    PartRoute.seq < route.seq,`

**Tuzatish:** Compute next_seq as the max seq of NON-tail steps (`PartRoute.process_code.not_in(TAIL_CODES)`) + 1 and shift the existing tail rows' seq by one, so a newly enabled post lands before UPAKOVKA/TMO.

### `backend/app/api/routes/orders.py:748` — BUG · 2 ta mustaqil agent tasdiqladi

_set_custom_route validates route_codes against the whole PROCESS_POSTS map (1..16) while its own error text promises 1–14, so a texnolog can inject the system-only tail codes 15/16 and get duplicated UPAKOVKA/TMO steps.

**Hujjat:** docs/03-backend-api.md 309-qator ('1–14 ni texnolog tanlaydi, 15 (qadoqlash) va 16 (TMO qabuli) — tizim qo'shadigan dum, `TAIL_CODES`')

**Dalil:** orders.py:747-749: `    for code in codes:` / `        if code not in PROCESS_POSTS:` / `            raise ForbiddenException(f"Noma'lum jarayon kodi: {code}. Ruxsat etilgan: 1–14.")`, keyin 799-802: `    if codes and part.assembly_parent_id is None:` / `        tail = [(15, PostCode.UPAKOVKA)] if packaging else []` / `        tail.append((16, PostCode.TMO))`. `models.py:52: TAIL_CODES = (15, 16)` mavjud, lekin bu tekshiruvda ishlatilmaydi. docs/10-giblab-reference.md:322-323: "Frontend tanlagichi (`Orders/CustomRoutes.tsx`) bu ikkisini ro'yxatdan chiqarib tashlaydi — aks holda ikkita manba paydo bo'lardi." — ya'ni himoya faqat frontendda.

**Tuzatish:** Shartni `if code not in PROCESS_POSTS or code in TAIL_CODES:` ga o'zgartirish — `TAIL_CODES` allaqachon shu faylga import qilingan (orders.py:72).

### `backend/app/api/routes/pachka.py:314` — BUG · 3 ta mustaqil agent tasdiqladi

Qadoqdan chiqarilgan nusxa O'SHA qadoqqa qayta skanerlansa UPAKOVKA qadami hisobi tiklanmaydi: doimiy `client_event_id` bekor qilingan skanni topib `duplicate` qaytaradi va `total_done` kamaygan holida qoladi.

**Hujjat:** docs/09-mes-domain.md §5 (qator 415-417) — aynan shu tuzoq yorliq uchun tasvirlangan

**Dalil:** Write path (`pachka.py:308-320`): `await record_scan_at(..., client_event_id=f"pack-{pachka.id}-{instance.id}",)` then `db.add(PachkaItem(pachka_id=pachka.id, instance_id=instance.id))`. Undo path (`pachka.py:352-370`): `item.is_deleted = True` … `scan.is_cancelled = True` / `scan.cancelled_at = now` / `update(PartRoute)...values(total_done=PartRoute.total_done - 1)` — the Scan ROW survives with the same `client_event_id`. Replay check (`services/scanning.py:478-479`): "# Idempotent replay (network retry): same client_event_id → same success, no recount." / `existing = (await db.execute(select(Scan).where(Scan.client_event_id == client_event_id))).scalar_one_or_none()` — no `is_cancelled == False` filter, so it returns `duplicate=True` and the UPAKOVKA step stays unfinished while the part sits in the pachka. The identical trap was already fixed for labels by adding a timestamp (docs/09 line 415-417: "Vaqt tamg'asi ataylab: doimiy kalitda chek bekor qilinib qayta chiqarilsa idempotentlik tekshiruvi eski (bekor qilingan) qatorni topib `duplicate` qaytarardi").

**Tuzatish:** Apply the label fix here: make the key unique per attempt, e.g. `client_event_id=f"pack-{pachka.id}-{instance.id}-{int(datetime.now(UTC).timestamp())}"`, and widen the undo lookup at pachka.py:358-360 to `Scan.client_event_id.like(f"pack-{pachka.id}-{instance_id}%")`; alternatively add `Scan.is_cancelled == False` to the replay query in scanning.py:479.

### `backend/app/api/routes/terminal.py:1090` — BUG

Two of the three scan-cancellation paths decrement `PartRoute.total_done` but omit the COMPLETED→PRODUCTION regression and the derived assembly-scan cancellation that `scanning.cancel_scan` performs, leaving an order marked COMPLETED with unfinished route steps.

**Dalil:** terminal.py:1090-1097 `for scan in auto_scans:` / `scan.is_cancelled = True` / `.values(total_done=PartRoute.total_done - 1)` — nothing else. pachka.py:366-372 identical shape. scanning.py:844-847 has the missing half: "# A completed order regresses if its last scan is undone." / `if order is not None and order.status == OrderStatus.COMPLETED:` / `order.status = OrderStatus.PRODUCTION` / `order.completed_at = None`; scanning.py:797-817 also cancels `Scan.parent_scan_id == scan.id` children.

**Tuzatish:** Extract a single `revoke_scan(db, scan)` helper in `services/scanning.py` that cancels the scan, cancels its `parent_scan_id` children, decrements every affected `PartRoute.total_done`, and regresses `order.status`/`completed_at`; call it from `terminal.cancel_instance_label`, `pachka.remove_from_pachka` and `scanning.cancel_scan`.

### `backend/app/api/routes/ws.py:65` — BUG

TABLO qurilma hisobi majburan ish markaziga bog'langani uchun WS scope filtri unga FAQAT bitta postning skan eventlarini yetkazadi — devor ekrani boshqa 8 postning skanlarini jonli ko'rmaydi.

**Hujjat:** docs/03-backend-api.md:543 (ws.py jadvali) va docs/09-mes-domain.md:340-341

**Dalil:** ws.py:65 `scope = _SCOPE_BY_ROLE.get(role) or ("terminal" if role in POST_ROLES else "dashboard")` (_SCOPE_BY_ROLE: `UserRole.TABLO: "tablo"`) + ws.py:50-52 `if user.machine_id is not None: machine = await db.get(Machine, user.machine_id); post_id = machine.post_id if machine else None`; core/ws.py `wants()`: `if self.scope == "dashboard": return True` … `if self.post_id is not None and target_post == self.post_id: return True; return self.machine_id is not None and target_machine == self.machine_id`; users.py:52-53 `if role in (UserRole.TERMINAL, UserRole.TABLO): if machine_id is None: raise DuplicateValueException(f"{role} hisobi uchun ish markazi tanlanishi shart.")`; scanning.py:735-737 skan eventlari `post_id=event.get("post_id"), machine_id=event.get("machine_id")` bilan nishonlanadi; frontend tablo.tsx:255 `if (event.type.startsWith("scan.") || event.type.startsWith("order.")) queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["tablo"] })`.

**Tuzatish:** ws.py:65 da TABLO rolini dashboard kabi FILTRSIZ qiling (masalan `WsClient(..., post_id=None, machine_id=None)` yoki `wants()` da `if self.scope in ("dashboard", "tablo"): return True`) — TABLO butun tsex matritsasini ko'rsatadi, bitta post emas. Hozir jonli yangilanish faqat nishonsiz `order.*` eventlarida ishlaydi, skanlar esa 60 s pollingni kutadi (queries/mes.ts `refetchInterval: 60_000`).

### `backend/app/core/config.py:99` — BUG

FORWARDED_ALLOW_IPS defaults to ["*"], so uvicorn's ProxyHeadersMiddleware trusts X-Forwarded-For from ANY caller and takes its LEFTMOST (client-supplied) value — the login rate limiter is bypassable by rotating a header, and every audit-log IP is forgeable.

**Hujjat:** docs/02-backend-core.md:204 and docs/07-infra-devops.md:466

**Dalil:** config.py:99 `FORWARDED_ALLOW_IPS: list[str] = ["*"]`; .venv/.../uvicorn/middleware/proxy_headers.py: `self.always_trust: bool = trusted_hosts in ("*", ["*"])` and `if self.always_trust: return _parse_host_port(x_forwarded_for_hosts[0])` — the first, i.e. attacker-written, hop. rate_limit.py:45 `ip = request.client.host if request.client else "unknown"` → key `f"ratelimit:{ip}:{path}:{window_start}"`; deps.py:24 `return request.client.host if request.client else None`. docs/07-infra-devops.md:160 confirms prod also hardcodes gunicorn `--forwarded-allow-ips '*'`.

**Tuzatish:** Set FORWARDED_ALLOW_IPS (and the gunicorn `--forwarded-allow-ips` flag in docker-compose) to the actual proxy address — the Caddy container IP or the compose bridge network CIDR (e.g. `172.16.0.0/12`) — never `*`. With a concrete trust list uvicorn walks XFF right-to-left and returns the first UNTRUSTED hop, which is the real client. Until that is done, `LOGIN_RATE_LIMIT_ATTEMPTS=5` provides no brute-force protection and `audit_log.ip` cannot be used as evidence.

### `backend/app/core/ws.py:95` — BUG

The Redis pub/sub listener task catches only CancelledError, so any Redis connection error kills cross-worker WS fan-out permanently and silently.

**Hujjat:** docs/02-backend-core.md:285 (`start_listener(redis)` / `stop_listener()` — lifespan boshqaradigan pub/sub listener task)

**Dalil:** ws.py:90-97 `async for message in pubsub.listen():` ... `except asyncio.CancelledError:` / `await pubsub.unsubscribe(_CHANNEL)` / `raise` — there is no `except Exception` and no restart. Meanwhile ws.py:63-69 `if self.redis is not None:` ... `await self.redis.publish(_CHANNEL, json.dumps(event, default=str))` / `return` returns early on a successful publish and never falls back to `_dispatch`.

**Tuzatish:** Wrap the `async for` body in `except Exception: logger.exception(...)` plus a reconnect/resubscribe loop (retry with backoff), so the listener survives a Redis blip; alternatively have `publish` also `_dispatch` locally when `self._listener` is done.

### `backend/app/models/base.py:17` — BUG

`updated_at` is declared nullable with `default=None` AND `server_default=current_timestamp(0)`, so SQLAlchemy omits it from the INSERT and every freshly created row gets a non-NULL `updated_at` — it is never NULL, defeating the frontend's "never edited" check.

**Hujjat:** docs/04-backend-data.md §1.1 line 22

**Dalil:** Code (base.py:17-23): `updated_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(` / `DateTime(timezone=True),` / `nullable=True,` / `default=None,` / `onupdate=lambda: datetime.now(UTC),` / `server_default=text("current_timestamp(0)"),`. Doc (04 line 22): "`updated_at` — `onupdate` orqali avtomatik yangilanadi". Reproduced with the project's own SQLAlchemy 2.0.52 using the identical mapping: emitted statement was `INSERT INTO t (created_at, is_deleted) VALUES (?, ?) RETURNING id, updated_at` and the stored row came back `('2026-08-26 05:11:04.888414', '2026-08-26 05:11:04')` — i.e. the server default fired. The DDL really has it: `1393a241b793_...py:39` `"updated_at", sa.DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=sa.text("current_timestamp(0)"), nullable=True`. Consumer: frontend/src/routes/_layout/orders/$orderId.tsx:348 `order.updated_at && order.updated_at !== order.created_at ? formatDateTime(order.updated_at) : "—"` — created_at carries Python microseconds, updated_at is DB second precision, so the two strings never match and a brand-new order always shows a bogus "Oxirgi tahrir".

**Tuzatish:** Drop `server_default=text("current_timestamp(0)")` from `TimestampMixin.updated_at` and add a migration that does `ALTER TABLE <each> ALTER COLUMN updated_at DROP DEFAULT` (plus a one-off `UPDATE ... SET updated_at = NULL WHERE updated_at = created_at`-style backfill if the historical rows matter). Keep `onupdate` — that is what actually maintains the column.

### `backend/app/models/employee.py:21` — TOZALASH

The `user_id` comment still labels the column "ARXIV" and claims shop workers never log in and that rows carrying it cannot be deleted — all three are wrong; the column is an active, admin-editable link and the delete guard was removed.

**Hujjat:** docs/04-backend-data.md §1.4 line 100

**Dalil:** Code (employee.py:21-25): "# ARXIV: eski avtomat mirror yozuvlarining post-rol hisobiga bog'lami. Yangi / # mirror yaratilmaydi (`c7e2b4f81d39`) — tsexdagi ishchi login qilmaydi, ya'ni / # HODIM va FOYDALANUVCHI kesishmaydi. Ustun qoladi: mavjud qatorlar shu orqali / # ajratiladi (o'chirish taqiqlanadi, skan tarixi ularda) va kelajakda ishchi / # ayni paytda menejer ham bo'lsa shu bog'lam ishlatiladi." Reality: `schemas/employees.py:38` `user_id: int | None = None` in EmployeeCreate/Update; `employees.py:86` `await _validate_user_link(db, body.user_id)`; `login.py:116` `Employee.user_id == user.id` opens the shift TODAY; `employees.py:186-190` "# Ilgari bu yerda POST ROLI hisobiga bog'langan yozuvni o'chirishga to'siq bor / # edi ... To'siq qolganda haqiqiy hodimni o'chirib bo'lmasdi." — the ban is gone; and `a9e14b7c2d63` hard-deleted the mirror rows the comment says are still distinguished by it. Doc (04 line 100): "Ilgari bu ustun ARXIV edi — eski avtomat mirror yozuvlarini belgilardi (`c7e2b4f81d39` nofaol qildi, `a9e14b7c2d63` o'chirdi)".

**Tuzatish:** Replace the ARXIV comment with the current meaning (already stated correctly in docs/04 line 100): links an employee to a login account — used by `login._open_shift_for` on scanner-less posts and for a worker who is also office staff; device/`is_system` accounts are rejected by `employees._validate_user_link`.

### `backend/app/models/user.py:18` — BUG

`user.name` DB column is VARCHAR(30) while `UserCreate`/`UserUpdate` accept up to 100 chars — a 31–100 char name passes validation and then blows up at INSERT/UPDATE.

**Hujjat:** docs/04-backend-data.md:79 — "| `name` | `String(30)` | NULL | — | — |"

**Dalil:** Model (backend/app/models/user.py:18): `name: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(30), default=None, kw_only=True)`. Init migration (backend/app/migrations/versions/e1c262d7e2ae_init.py:26): `sa.Column("name", sa.String(length=30), nullable=True),` — no later `alter_column` on it (grep over all of backend/app/migrations/versions returns none). Schema (backend/app/schemas/users.py:50): `_NAME_FIELD = Field(min_length=2, max_length=100, examples=["Abdurahmonov Sherzod"], default=None)`, whose own comment at backend/app/schemas/users.py:48-49 states the intent: `#: 30 belgi yetmasdi: "Abdurahmonov Abdurahmon Abdurahmonovich" — 39 belgi.` / `#: \`employee.full_name\` allaqachon 100 belgi, ikkalasi bir xil bo'lsin.` The admin path feeds it: frontend/src/components/People/PersonDialog.tsx:285 `maxLength={100}` on the F.I.Sh input, and that same value goes to `UsersService.createUser({ name: form.name.trim(), ... })` (PersonDialog.tsx:168). Every other name column already matches its schema (employee.full_name String(100)/max_length=100, machine.name String(100)/100, client.full_name String(150)/150, post.name String(50)/50) — user.name is the only one out of step.

**Tuzatish:** The widening to 100 was clearly intended (see the schemas/users.py:48-49 comment) but only landed in the Pydantic layer. Add an alembic migration `alter_column("user", "name", type_=sa.String(100))`, change backend/app/models/user.py:18 to `String(100)`, and update docs/04-backend-data.md:79 to `String(100)`.

### `backend/app/models/user.py:32` — TOZALASH

The comment block above `role` states four things that have all been false since `e7a35c19d840`: post-role accounts are "NOT people", password login is closed for them, exactly one exists per post, and user/employee tables "no longer overlap".

**Hujjat:** docs/04-backend-data.md §1.2 line 53; docs/09-mes-domain.md §3.0.2 lines 264-276

**Dalil:** Code (user.py:33-37): "# bound to a machine (TZ 8.1 variant B). The POST_ROLES accounts (arrachi..omborchi) are / # NOT people: password login is closed for them (`login.py`) and exactly one exists per / # post. ... WHO is working comes / # from the badge, i.e. from `Employee`; the two tables no longer overlap." Reality: `login.py:94` `if user.is_system:` → 401 — the gate is the ACCOUNT type, not the role; `login.py:99` `if POST_ROLES.get(UserRole(user.role)) is not None:` then `await _open_shift_for(db, user)` deliberately logs post-role humans in. Overlap exists: `login.py:116` `Employee.user_id == user.id` and `employees.py:119` `_validate_user_link(...)`. Doc (04 line 53): "Parol bilan kirish esa hisobning TURIGA bog'liq, ROLIGA emas: `is_system=True` hisob (seed yaratgani) 401 oladi, odamning SHAXSIY hisobi esa kiradi". The same file's own line 22-25 comment on `is_system` already says the correct thing.

**Tuzatish:** Rewrite user.py:32-37 to: staff/device roles as before; POST_ROLES accounts exist in two flavours — the `is_system=True` seed account (never logs in, only names the badge-opened shift via `_terminal_user`) and a person's personal account (logs in with a password and opens their own shift via `login._open_shift_for`, linked through `employee.user_id`).

### `backend/app/services/giblab/ingest.py:68` — BUG

snapshot_custom_routes only sees parts that already have a MANUAL route row, so a part marked "Nostandart" (or bound to an assembly parent) before any route was drawn silently loses is_custom and assembly_parent_id on re-upload.

**Hujjat:** docs/09-mes-domain.md:123-124

**Dalil:** ingest.py:68 `.join(PartRoute, PartRoute.part_id == Part.id)` + ingest.py:72 `PartRoute.source == RouteSource.MANUAL` — an INNER join, so parts without MANUAL rows never enter `saved`. Its own docstring promises otherwise: ingest.py:35 «ikkalasi ham fayldan tiklanmaydi. Bayroqning o'zi ham saqlanadi.»; docs/09-mes-domain.md:124 «Nostandart detallar ham, matritsadan qo'lda qadam qo'shilgan STANDART detallar ham olinadi (`is_custom` bayrog'i bilan birga)». Marking a part custom creates NO manual rows — orders.py:568-580 only soft-deletes the AUTO rows; MANUAL rows appear later, in `_set_custom_route`.

**Tuzatish:** Snapshot the flags from `part` itself (LEFT OUTER JOIN to PartRoute, or a second query over `Part.is_custom == True` / `Part.assembly_parent_id IS NOT NULL`) and merge the manual process codes into that dict, so is_custom + assembly_parent_id survive even with an empty code list.

### `backend/app/services/giblab/ingest.py:145` — BUG · 2 ta mustaqil agent tasdiqladi

restore_custom_routes appends restored MANUAL steps AFTER the freshly-ingested tail (UPAKOVKA/TMO), so TMO is no longer the last step of a standard part's route.

**Hujjat:** docs/09-mes-domain.md:13 (post jadvali) va docs/09:82 (standart marshrut tartibi)

**Dalil:** backend/app/services/giblab/ingest.py:145: `start_seq = max((r.seq for r in live), default=0) + 1` — `live` ichida `ingest_project` qo'ygan AUTO qadamlar bor va ular HAR DOIM TMO bilan tugaydi (ingest.py:400 `steps.append((16, PostCode.TMO, 1))`, keyin 401 `for seq, (...) in enumerate(steps, start=1)`). Demak tiklangan BOYOQ/STOLYARKA qadami `seq` bo'yicha TMO dan keyin tushadi. `scanning.py:557-574` (`STRICT_STAGE_ORDER`, sukut True — app/core/config.py:79) `PartRoute.seq < route.seq` bo'yicha tekshiradi, ya'ni bo'yoqchi skanida TMO tugamagani uchun E004 chiqadi. Xuddi shu naqsh `orders.py:622-628` dagi matritsa tugmasida ham bor: `select(func.coalesce(func.max(PartRoute.seq), 0) + 1)`.

**Tuzatish:** Dum qadamlarini (TAIL_CODES) ajratib, yangi MANUAL qadamlarni dumdan OLDIN qo'yish va dum `seq` larini surish — ikkala joyda (ingest.restore_custom_routes va orders.edit_order_matrix) bitta helper bilan.

### `backend/app/services/giblab/models.py:179` — HUJJAT DRIFTI · 6 ta mustaqil agent tasdiqladi

Hujjat PRISADKA qadami faqat TESHIK bo'lganda ochiladi deydi; kod esa har qanday ishlov — teshik, paz yoki freza — uchun ochadi (947df7e commit).

**Hujjat:** docs/10-giblab-reference.md §2.3, 282-283-qatorlar

**Dalil:** Hujjat, docs/09-mes-domain.md:88: "baribir SCX ga va yorliq eskiziga ketadi, faqat marshrut qadami bo'lmaydi. ROVER, STOLYARKA va". Kod: models.py:179 `            if op.bores or op.edge_bores or op.grooves or any(op.mills):` (qadam yaratiladi) va labels.py:236-241 `    # Paz (П) va freza (Ф) belgilari olib tashlandi ...` / `    if d.xnc_summary.get("bores") or d.xnc_summary.get("edge_bores"):` — eskiz faqat `bores`/`edge` ni chizadi. Buni docs/10-giblab-reference.md:284 ham tasdiqlaydi: "Paz/freza geometriyasi faqat SCX ga ketadi — yorliq eskizida ko'rinmaydi ... П/Ф belgilari ataylab olib tashlangan (`labels.py:237`)".

**Tuzatish:** Hujjat noto'g'ri. 85-88 qatorlarni qayta yozish: ROVER qadami yaratilmaydi (to'g'ri), lekin paz/freza PRISADKA qadamini beradi va geometriyasi FAQAT SCX ga ketadi — yorliq eskizida ko'rinmaydi.

### `backend/app/services/giblab/parser.py:468` — BUG

CS `<part>` ro'yxatidagi HAR bir stubga `label_num` beriladi, joylashtirilmagan (usedCount=0) detalga ham — ingest esa aynan `label_num is not None` ni "listga joylashtirilgan" belgisi deb oladi va soxta ARRA qadamini yaratadi.

**Hujjat:** docs/10-giblab-reference.md §3.1 (401-403)

**Dalil:** parser.py:463-469: `for stub in op_el.findall("part"):` / `        pid = int(stub.get("id", 0))` / `        label_counter += 1` / `        ref_part = part_by_file_id.get(pid)` / `        if ref_part is not None:` / `            ref_part.label_num = label_counter` / `            ref_part.material_name = cut.material_name`.
ingest.py:387: `sheet_part = project.generation != "processed" or pp.material_name or pp.label_num is not None` — izohi: "Optimizatsiya qilingan faylda materiali ham, yorliq raqami ham yo'q detal umuman listga joylashtirilmagan ... unga ARRA vazifasi berilsa arrachi kesa olmaydigan soxta ish paydo bo'lardi".
docs/10-giblab-reference.md:401-403: "CS operatsiyasida `<part>` ro'yxati bor, lekin **ro'yxatda bo'lish joylashtirilganini bildirmaydi**: `usedCount="0"` bo'lsa GibLab uni ataylab joylashtirmagan ... `kuxniya.project` da uchta shunday detal bor."

**Tuzatish:** `_parse_cs_operation` da stubning `usedCount` ini o'qib, `_f(stub, "usedCount") == 0` bo'lsa `label_num`/`material_name` ni BERMASLIK (label_counter esa GibSaw tartibi uchun baribir oshirilsin), yoki `ParsedPart` ga alohida `placed: bool` maydon qo'shib ingest'dagi `sheet_part` shartini o'shanga bog'lash.

### `backend/app/services/giblab/xnc.py:32` — BUG

The `_eval_expr` whitelist permits `_`, `.`, `(` and `)`, so it is not "arithmetic only": the explicit `**` DoS ban is bypassed via `__pow__`, and attribute access / method calls on live objects escape the emptied `__builtins__` namespace.

**Hujjat:** docs/10-giblab-reference.md §3.2, lines 555-558

**Dalil:** xnc.py:11 `_EXPR_RE = re.compile(r"^[\w\s+\-*/().]+$")`; xnc.py:30-33 `# `**` charset'dan o'tadi ("* *") — 9**9**9 kabi ifoda eval'da workerni / # osiltirib qo'yardi (DoS). Arifmetikada daraja ishlatilmaydi — taqiq.` / `if not _EXPR_RE.match(expr) or "**" in expr:`; xnc.py:35 `return float(eval(expr, {"__builtins__": {}}, env))  # noqa: S307 — charset-whitelisted arithmetic`. Doc line 555-557: "Ifodalar (`dz+3` kabi) faqat oq ro'yxatdagi arifmetika bilan hisoblanadi: raqam, identifikator, `+ - * / ( ) .`; `**` alohida taqiqlangan (`9**9**9` workerni osiltirib qo'yardi — DoS himoyasi)."

**Tuzatish:** Stop using `eval`. Parse the expression with `ast.parse(expr, mode="eval")` and walk the tree, accepting only `ast.Expression`, `ast.BinOp` (Add/Sub/Mult/Div), `ast.UnaryOp` (UAdd/USub), `ast.Constant` (int/float) and `ast.Name` resolved from `env`; reject every other node type (Attribute, Call, Subscript, Pow). That removes both the `__pow__` DoS path and the attribute-access escape, and makes the `"**" in expr` special case unnecessary.

### `backend/app/services/labels.py:208` — BUG

Loyiha nomi 26 belgigacha chiziladi, lekin QR bloki uni x=40 mm dan boshlab ustidan bosib o'chiradi — uzun loyiha nomining oxiri chekda umuman ko'rinmaydi.

**Hujjat:** docs/09-mes-domain.md §5 (376-qator): "loyiha nomi, kromka materiallari 2×2"

**Dalil:** labels.py:208 `text(5, 13.5, d.project_name[:26], 3.3, bold=True)` (izohi: "Loyiha (limit 26: kattaroq shriftda eskiz chizig'iga yetmasin)") va labels.py:275 `img.paste(qr.get_image().convert("RGB").resize((qr_px, qr_px)), (40 * mm, 1 * mm))` — QR maydoni x 40..56 mm, y 1..17 mm, matn qatori esa y 13.5..16.8 mm.

**Tuzatish:** Loyiha nomini belgi soni bilan emas, kenglik bilan cheklash: `draw.textlength` bo'yicha 35 mm (x=5..40) ga sig'guncha kesish, yoki qatorni QR ostidagi bo'sh y (masalan y≥17.5) ga tushirish. Hozirgi 26 belgi limiti QR'ni hisobga olmaydi.

### `backend/app/services/scanning.py:507` — BUG

QR text is not order-scoped (uniqueness is only per order), so when two live PRODUCTION orders are built from the same GibLab product/part codes, every scan is silently credited to the NEWEST order — and the printed label carries no order number to tell them apart.

**Dalil:** giblab/models.py:130 `return f"{self.product_code}_{self.code}"` and :136 `return [self.key] if self.count == 1 else [f"{self.key}_{n}" ...]` — nothing order-specific. models/order.py:123 `Index("ix_part_instance_order_qr", "order_id", "qr_text", unique=True, ...)` — uniqueness is (order_id, qr_text), NOT qr_text. scanning.py:502-507 `PartInstance.qr_text == cand, ... Order.status.in_([OrderStatus.PRODUCTION, OrderStatus.CANCELLED])` `.order_by(Order.id.desc())` then `.first()`.

**Tuzatish:** Make the collision impossible or visible: (a) prefix qr_text with the order (e.g. `{order.number}_{key}`) at ingest and regenerate SCX/labels, or (b) at minimum detect it — count matching rows in record_scan_at and raise `ScanRejected("E001", "Bu QR bir nechta buyurtmada bor — ...")` when more than one live order matches, instead of silently taking `Order.id.desc()`. Also draw `LabelData.order_number` on the label (it is already loaded at labels.py:104 and never rendered) so the operator can see which order a chek belongs to.

### `backend/app/services/scanning.py:591` — BUG · 2 ta mustaqil agent tasdiqladi

The `except IntegrityError` guard around `db.flush()` never fires — SQLAlchemy autoflushes the pending Scan INSERT inside the preceding `db.execute(update(...))`, so a `client_event_id` unique-index race escapes as an unhandled 500 instead of the intended 422 E001.

**Hujjat:** docs/09-mes-domain.md §3, line 140-141 ("Idempotency: `client_event_id` UNIQUE")

**Dalil:** scanning.py:587-597:
    db.add(scan)
...
    await db.execute(update(PartRoute).where(PartRoute.id == route.id).values(total_done=PartRoute.total_done + 1))
    await db.refresh(route, ["total_done"])
    try:
        await db.flush()
    except IntegrityError:  # client_event_id race — treat as replay
        await db.rollback()
        raise ScanRejected("E001", "Takroriy scan aniqlandi. Qayta urining.") from None

Session has autoflush on (backend/app/core/db.py:28: `local_session = async_sessionmaker(bind=async_engine, class_=AsyncSession, expire_on_commit=False)` — no `autoflush=False`). Verified empirically with the project's sqlalchemy 2.0.52: adding a row that violates a unique index and then calling `Session.execute(update(...))` raises `IntegrityError` FROM `execute()`, and `s.new` is already empty afterwards.

models/scan.py:35: `Index("ix_scan_client_event_id", "client_event_id", unique=True),`

**Tuzatish:** Move the `db.add(scan)` + flush before the counter UPDATE, or wrap the whole block (lines 587-594) in the `try`: `try: db.add(scan); await db.flush(); await db.execute(update(PartRoute)...); await db.refresh(route, ["total_done"]) except IntegrityError: ...`. Alternatively call `db.execute(..., execution_options={"autoflush": False})` — but the flush must then still be inside the try.

### `backend/pyproject.toml:58` — BUG

`[tool.ruff] fix = true` `ruff check` ni auto-fix rejimiga o'tkazadi, shuning uchun CI va `make lint` avto-tuzatiladigan buzilishlarda YIQILMAYDI — o'rniga fayllarni jimgina qayta yozadi va exit 0 beradi.

**Hujjat:** docs/07-infra-devops.md:67 ("`lint` ... `ruff check app` + `ruff format --check app` + `mypy app` (`backend/` dan, CI bilan aynan bir xil)") va docs/07-infra-devops.md:505 (ruff konfigi sanab o'tilgan, `fix` eslatilmagan)

**Dalil:** backend/pyproject.toml:55-58 — `[tool.ruff]` ... `fix = true`. .github/workflows/ci.yml:20 — `run: cd backend && uv run ruff check app && uv run ruff format --check app`. Makefile:53-54 — `lint: ## Run linting checks (ruff check + format check + mypy, CI bilan bir xil)` / `\tcd backend && uv run ruff check app && uv run ruff format --check app`. Amaliy tekshiruv (repo venv'idagi ruff 0.16.3): `app/_probe_tmp.py` ga ishlatilmagan `import os`/`import sys` qo'yib `ruff check app/_probe_tmp.py` chaqirildi -> chiqish "Found 2 errors (2 fixed, 0 remaining).", exit=0, fayl esa importlarsiz qolib ketdi.

**Tuzatish:** `fix = true` ni pyproject'dan olib tashlash (pre-commit hook allaqachon `--fix` ni o'zi uzatadi, `make format` ham `ruff check . --fix` deb chaqiradi). Muqobil: CI va `make lint` ni `ruff check --no-fix app` ga o'tkazish.


## bridge-agent

### `bridge-agent/agent.py:554` — BUG

flusher() jobni chop etish uchun `await asyncio.to_thread(...)` bilan kutadi, shu paytda uplink() navbat fayliga YANGI job qo'shadi va serverga ack yuboradi; keyin flusher `local_rewrite(remaining)` bilan faylni FAQAT o'zi o'qigan qoldiq bilan almashtiradi — oradagi yangi job jimgina o'chadi (serverdan ham, diskdan ham yo'q).

**Hujjat:** docs/07-infra-devops.md:270 va 265 — «Ishonchlilik: hech bir yorliq yo'qolmaydi»

**Dalil:** agent.py:549 `if await asyncio.to_thread(send_to_printer, job.get("png", "")):`
agent.py:554 `            local_rewrite(remaining)`
agent.py:436 `    tmp.replace(QUEUE_FILE)`
agent.py:667-668 `                        local_enqueue(msg)` / `                        await ws.send(json.dumps({"ok": True}))`
docs/07-infra-devops.md:270 «`uplink()` jobni avval `local_enqueue()` bilan diskka yozadi (`print-queue.jsonl`, JSONL append), **keyin** ack yuboradi. Ya'ni server jobni faqat u tsexdagi diskda turgani aniq bo'lgach unutadi»

**Tuzatish:** local_rewrite jobs ro'yxatini emas, ISHLANGAN qatorlarni olib tashlashi kerak: yozilgan bayt offsetini eslab qolish yoki rewrite paytida faylni qayta o'qib, drain'dan keyin qo'shilgan qatorlarni `remaining` oxiriga qo'shish. Eng sodda yechim — navbat faylining yagona yozuvchisi flusher bo'lsin: uplink jobni `asyncio.Queue` ga bersin, faylga esa faqat flusher tegsin.

### `docs/09-mes-domain.md:82` — HUJJAT DRIFTI · 2 ta mustaqil agent tasdiqladi

The automatic PRISADKA step is now created for any XNC work (bores, edge bores, grooves or mills), not only for holes — and paz/freza parts DO get a route step now.

**Hujjat:** docs/09-mes-domain.md §2 Standart marshrut (lines 80-88); same claim in docs/04-backend-data.md:129

**Dalil:** DOC (docs/09-mes-domain.md:82): "PRISADKA (kod 6, bf/bt/bb/bl/br bo'lsa; per_instance = shu element bor XNC op soni, `_2` → 2)."  DOC (docs/09-mes-domain.md:88): "baribir SCX ga va yorliq eskiziga ketadi, faqat marshrut qadami bo'lmaydi."  DOC (docs/04-backend-data.md:129): "PRISADKA (kod 6, teshik bo'lsa)"  CODE (backend/app/services/giblab/models.py, stage_sides): "for op in self.xnc_ops:\n            if op.bores or op.edge_bores or op.grooves or any(op.mills):\n                result[PostCode.PRISADKA] = result.get(PostCode.PRISADKA, 0) + 1" with docstring "Qadam HAR QANDAY ishlov uchun ochiladi: teshik, paz yoki freza."

**Tuzatish:** Change the condition in docs/09:82 and docs/04:129 to "teshik, paz yoki freza bo'lsa"; rewrite docs/09:87-88 to say paz/freza geometry now also produces a PRISADKA route step, and cite migration `a7c419e0b3d5` which backfilled it for already-loaded orders.

### `docs/10-giblab-reference.md:282` — HUJJAT DRIFTI

PRISADKA qadami faqat TESHIK uchun emas — paz (`gr`) va freza (`ms`/`ml`/`mac`) uchun ham ochiladi.

**Hujjat:** **Fayldan chiqadigan marshrut — STANDART va qisqa** (`ingest.py`): ARRA (kod 1, listga joylashtirilgan detalga) + KROMKA (kod 3, qirralar soni bo'yicha) + PRISADKA (kod 6, XNC da teshik bo'lsa).

**Dalil:** backend/app/services/giblab/models.py:179 — `if op.bores or op.edge_bores or op.grooves or any(op.mills):` (keyingi qator `result[PostCode.PRISADKA] = result.get(PostCode.PRISADKA, 0) + 1`). Shu funksiya docstringi (models.py:172-175) ochiq yozadi: "Ilgari faqat TESHIK hisobga olinardi va paz/freza-only detal marshrutsiz qolardi". Migratsiya backend/app/migrations/versions/a7c419e0b3d5_paz_freza_detaliga_prisadka_qadami.py sarlavhasi: "paz/freza bor detalga PRISADKA qadami (mavjud buyurtmalarga ham)". `ingest.py:392-393` shu `stage_sides()` natijasini kod 6 bilan marshrutga yozadi.

**Tuzatish:** "PRISADKA (kod 6, XNC da teshik bo'lsa)" -> "PRISADKA (kod 6, XNC da har qanday ishlov — teshik, paz yoki freza — bo'lsa)".

### `docs/10-giblab-reference.md:342` — HUJJAT DRIFTI

"paz va freza ... bosqich ham yaratmaydi" noto'g'ri: paz/freza ham PRISADKA bosqichini yaratadi.

**Hujjat:** | `XNC` | Обработка — CNC ishlov | ✅ teshik → PRISADKA bosqichi; paz va freza faqat SCX da (yorliq eskizida yo'q; bosqich ham yaratmaydi — §2.3) |

**Dalil:** backend/app/services/giblab/models.py:179 — `if op.bores or op.edge_bores or op.grooves or any(op.mills):`. Faqat "yorliq eskizida yo'q" qismi to'g'ri: cutmap.py:237 `if merged and (merged.bores or merged.edge_bores):` va cutmap.py:241-245 `holes` ga faqat `bores` va `edge` yozadi.

**Tuzatish:** Katakni shunday o'zgartirish: "✅ har qanday ishlov → PRISADKA bosqichi; paz va freza geometriyasi faqat SCX da (yorliq eskizida yo'q, lekin bosqich yaratadi — §2.3)".


## frontend

### `frontend/src/components/Orders/CustomRoutes.tsx:88` — BUG

codesOf() reads every saved route step, including the system tail steps (15 UPAKOVKA / 16 TMO) that the backend appends itself, so a re-edited custom route is saved back with the tail embedded in the middle and a second tail appended.

**Hujjat:** docs/06-frontend-components.md:262 — "O'ng tomonda joriy marshrut `7 / 11 / 8` ko'rinishida." / docs/09-mes-domain.md:100-106

**Dalil:** CustomRoutes.tsx:87-88:
  const codesOf = (partId: number, routes: { process_code: number }[]) =>
    draft[partId] ?? routes.map((r) => r.process_code)
backend/app/api/routes/orders.py:794-796 (`_set_custom_route`), tail is always appended:
    if codes and part.assembly_parent_id is None:
        tail = [(15, PostCode.UPAKOVKA)] if packaging else []
        tail.append((16, PostCode.TMO))
backend/app/api/routes/orders.py:681 (backend's own reader strips it):
    return [c for c in codes if c not in TAIL_CODES]
backend/app/api/routes/orders.py:489-503 — `read_order_matrix` returns ALL non-deleted PartRoute rows as `RouteCell`, tail included.

**Tuzatish:** Filter the tail out when reading: `routes.map(r => r.process_code).filter(c => !TAIL_CODES.includes(c))` with `TAIL_CODES = [15, 16]` added to `@/lib/mes` (backend `giblab/models.py:52` has the same tuple). This also fixes `mine = codes[codes.length - 1]` (line 209), which currently resolves to 16/TMO for every saved part and drives both the assembly-parent option filter and the "Marshrut … da tugaydi" hint.

          })),
OrderMatrix.tsx:252-258 — the custom checkbox writes only `setCustomEdits`, never clears `stageEdits[part.id]`.
OrderMatrix.tsx:310-312 (the claim):
  /** Tahrirdagi holat (serverdagi emas): checkbox belgilangan zahoti katakchalar
   *  qulflansin — aks holda bitta saqlashda is_custom va stages birga ketib,
   *  backend qo'lda chizilgan marshrutga tegib qo'yardi. */
backend/app/api/routes/orders.py:601-644 — after the is_custom block the stages loop runs on the SAME edit: `for step in steps: step.is_enabled = enabled`, and `if not steps and enabled:` even creates a fresh MANUAL PartRoute on the now-custom part.

**Tuzatish:** In the "Nostandart" `onCheckedChange`, also drop that part's staged toggles: `setStageEdits(prev => { const { [part.id]: _, ...rest } = prev; return rest })`.

### `frontend/src/components/People/PersonDialog.tsx:146` — BUG

`switching` fires the destructive "kirish hisobi o'chiriladi" confirmation on EVERY edit of a tsex employee who has a personal login, even when the account is kept.

**Hujjat:** docs/06-frontend-components.md:36-39 ("Tur o'zgarsa qarama-qarshi yozuv o'chiriladi va buning uchun TASDIQ so'raladi")

**Dalil:** PersonDialog.tsx:146-149 `const switching =\n    editing &&\n    ((kind === "ofis" && !!person?.employee) ||\n      (kind !== "ofis" && !!person?.user))` — it never looks at `withLogin` (line 129 `const withLogin = kind === "tsex" && shopLogin && canManageAccounts`). Line 414 then shows `${form.name} endi tsexda ishlaydi: kirish hisobi o'chiriladi va u ofis paneliga kira olmaydi.` while the mutation actually KEEPS the account: line 189 `const account = withLogin && chosen?.role` → line 201 `} else if (account && userId !== null) { await UsersService.updateUser(...)`.

**Tuzatish:** Add `&& !withLogin` to the tsex arm: `(kind !== "ofis" && !!person?.user && !withLogin)` so the confirm only appears when the account is really about to be deleted (checkbox unchecked).

### `frontend/src/components/Terminal/KromkaView.tsx:75` — BUG

Lokal skan tekshiruvi detalning BIRINCHI topilgan marshrut qadamiga qarab javob beradi — KROMKA marshrutda ikki marta bo'lsa, birinchi o'tish tugagach ikkinchi o'tishning skani "allaqachon tugagan" deb BLOKLANADI.

**Hujjat:** docs/09-mes-domain.md:19 — "Yagonalik `(part_id, seq)` bo'yicha ..., post bo'yicha EMAS: bitta post marshrutda bir necha marta uchrashi mumkin (kromka → prisadka → yana kromka)"

**Dalil:** KromkaView.tsx:70-80: `for (const qr of candidates) { for (const p of parts) { const inst = p.instances.find((i) => i.qr_text === qr); if (!inst) continue; ... if (inst.done >= p.sides) return oval ? ... : "Bu detalning kromkasi allaqachon tugagan."; return null } }` — `parts` da HAR MARSHRUT QADAMI alohida qator (backend `_post_route_rows` ... `.order_by(Part.code, PartRoute.seq)`, terminal.py:475) va har qatorning `instances[].done` faqat O'SHA qadam skanlari (`done=int(kromka_done.get((i.id, route.id), 0))`, terminal.py:583). Blok haqiqiy: useTerminalScan.ts:120-124 `if (err) { playError(); toast.error(err); return }` — so'rov serverga umuman ketmaydi.

**Tuzatish:** Qatorlarni `route_id` bo'yicha ajratib, tugallanmagan ENG KICHIK seq li qadamni tanlash: masalan `const rows = parts.filter(p => p.instances.some(i => i.qr_text === qr))` dan `rows.find(p => (p.instances.find(i=>i.qr_text===qr)!.done) < p.sides)` — topilsa `null`, topilmasa "allaqachon tugagan".

### `frontend/src/components/Terminal/useTerminalScan.ts:68` — BUG

Badge UUIDs are dispatched before any cooldown check, so a scanner double-read fires two `startTerminalSession` POSTs — the documented 2s duplicate barrier does not cover the badge path at all.

**Hujjat:** docs/05-frontend-app.md:231 ("Bir xil kod uchun 2s cooldown (duplikat yutiladi, TZ 8.4.4)") / docs/09-mes-domain.md:615 ("2 s umumiy QR cooldown (TT 8.4.4)")

**Dalil:** useTerminalScan.ts:65-71 — the badge branch is the very first thing after the empty check, above every `cooled()` call:
  return (raw: string) => {
    const value = raw.trim()
    if (!value) return
    if (BADGE_RE.test(value)) {
      onBadge(value)
      return
    }

Every other route through this function goes through `cooled(value, COOLDOWN_MS)` (lines 85, 91) or the KROMKA 5s check (97-104).

terminal.tsx:318 wires it straight to the mutation with no in-flight guard:
  onBadge: (badge) => startShift.mutate({ badge_id: badge }),

Failure scenario: the GoDEX-station scanner emits the badge UUID twice (a common double-read), or the operator waves the badge twice because the first beep was lost in shop noise → two POST /terminal/session calls with the same badge_id race each other. ShiftGate's `disabled={pending}` guards only the on-screen buttons, not this path.

**Tuzatish:** Move the badge branch below a `if (cooled(value, COOLDOWN_MS)) return` check (or add a dedicated badge cooldown), and/or guard the caller with `if (startShift.isPending) return`.

### `frontend/src/components/UserSettings/ChangePassword.tsx:23` — BUG · 2 ta mustaqil agent tasdiqladi

Full password-strength rules are applied to the EXISTING password field, so any account whose current password does not meet the new strength policy can never change its password through /settings.

**Hujjat:** docs/05-frontend-app.md:184

**Dalil:** ChangePassword.tsx:23 `current_password: passwordSchema,` — lib/validation.ts:15-26 `passwordSchema` = min 8 + `.regex(/[a-z]/)` + `.regex(/[A-Z]/)` + `.regex(/\d/)` + `.regex(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/)`. Backend does NOT validate the current password: backend/app/schemas/users.py:139-146 `class UpdatePassword(BaseModel): current_password: str; new_password: str` with `@field_validator("new_password")` only. Legacy/seed passwords can exist unvalidated: backend/app/commands/create_first_superuser.py:29 `hashed_password=await get_password_hash(settings.ADMIN_PASSWORD)` (no strength check). docs/05-frontend-app.md:184 records the OPPOSITE decision for the login form: "`password` — faqat `min(1)` (bo'sh emasligi; kuch talablari faqat parol o'rnatishda qo'llanadi, eski/seed parollar bilan kirish saqlanadi)".

**Tuzatish:** Use a plain `z.string().min(1, { message: "Joriy parol majburiy" })` for `current_password` and keep `passwordSchema` only on `new_password`, mirroring the login form's rationale in docs/05:184.

Doc claims Switch has two call sites (employees.tsx AKTIV/NOFAOL + printers.tsx can_print); employees.tsx never imports or renders Switch — it uses ConfirmDialog. Doc is wrong; Switch has exactly ONE caller.

Only importer: `routes/_layout/printers.tsx:43:import { Switch } from "@/components/ui/switch"`

**Tuzatish:** Fix docs/06:509 to say `printers.tsx` (chek chiqarish `can_print`) is the only caller; employees.tsx aktiv/nofaol o'tkazgichi `Common/ConfirmDialog` orqali (`TogglePerson`) ishlaydi.

### `frontend/src/hooks/useMesWs.ts:25` — BUG

The 30s heartbeat is write-only: the server's `pong` is explicitly discarded and no timer ever checks that a reply arrived, so a half-open WebSocket leaves `online === true` forever and every live event is silently lost.

**Hujjat:** docs/05-frontend-app.md:279 — "Ulanish uzilsa 3s dan keyin avto-reconnect, har 30s \"ping\" heartbeat (\"pong\" va JSON bo'lmagan frame'lar filtrlanadi)"

**Dalil:** useMesWs.ts:25-28 `heartbeat = setInterval(() => socket?.readyState === 1 && socket.send("ping"), 30_000,)` — nothing records the send. useMesWs.ts:33 `if (event.type && event.type !== "pong") handler.current(event)` — the reply is thrown away, never used as liveness proof. The only thing that can flip `online` to false is useMesWs.ts:38 `socket.onclose = () => { setOnline(false) ... }`. Backend confirms there is no server-side idle timeout either: backend/app/api/routes/ws.py:75-78 `await websocket.receive_text()` / `await websocket.send_json({"type": "pong"})` in an unbounded `while True`. Doc claims the heartbeat is a working liveness mechanism.

**Tuzatish:** Stamp `lastPongAt` in `onmessage` when `event.type === "pong"`, and in the same 30s interval close the socket (forcing the existing reconnect path) when no pong has arrived for ~2 intervals. Only then does `online` mean what docs/05:279, the dashboard pill and the terminal "ALOQA YO'Q" block claim it means.

### `frontend/src/hooks/useScanFlash.ts:43` — BUG

useScanFlash's auto-scroll timer is always cancelled before it fires, because every call site passes inline getKey/getCount lambdas that change identity on the re-render caused by setFlashId.

**Hujjat:** docs/05-frontend-app.md:273

**Dalil:** useScanFlash.ts:36-43:
      const t = setTimeout(() => {
        document
          .getElementById(`${rowIdPrefix}${changed}`)
          ?.scrollIntoView({ block: "center", behavior: "smooth" })
      }, 150)
      return () => clearTimeout(t)
    }
  }, [items, getKey, getCount, rowIdPrefix])

Call sites pass fresh lambdas each render —
KromkaView.tsx:90-95: `useScanFlash(parts, (p) => p.part_id, (p) => p.instances.reduce((s, i) => s + i.done, 0), "kv-row-")`
PrisadkaView.tsx:114-119 and :125-130 (the latter also on `instRows`, an unmemoized `parts.flatMap(...)`).

Sequence: effect finds `changed` -> `setFlashId(changed)` -> re-render -> getKey/getCount are new objects -> deps differ -> React runs the cleanup `clearTimeout(t)` well inside the 150 ms window -> the re-run sees prevRef already updated, so `changed` is null and no new timer is scheduled. scrollIntoView therefore only ever runs when setFlashId is a no-op (same row scanned twice in a row).

Doc claims it works: "hisobi O'ZGARGAN qatorni aniqlab qaytaradi ... va uni 150ms dan keyin markazga scroll qiladi".

**Tuzatish:** Keep the scroll out of the dep-sensitive path: store `changed` in a ref and do the scroll from a separate effect keyed on `flashId` only (`useEffect(() => {...}, [flashId, rowIdPrefix])`), or drop getKey/getCount from the dep array (they are read through a ref like `handler.current` in useMesWs/useBadgeScan). Also memoize `instRows` in PrisadkaView so the hook body does not rebuild its Map on every render.

### `frontend/src/routes/terminal.tsx:302` — BUG · 2 ta mustaqil agent tasdiqladi

`endSession` is the only terminal mutation with no `onError` — a failed "Yakunlash" produces no toast, no dialog close, and no visible feedback at all.

**Hujjat:** docs/05-frontend-app.md:237 ("`terminal` qurilma akkauntida sessiya bo'lsa \"Yakunlash\" (`endTerminalSession`)")

**Dalil:** terminal.tsx:302-309:
  const endSession = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (employeeId?: number) =>
      TerminalService.endTerminalSession({
        query: { employee_id: employeeId },
      }),
    onSettled: () =>
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["terminal", "context"] }),
  })

Compare startShift (234), scanMutation (271) and undoMutation (296), which all have `onError: ... toast.error(detailMessage(err))`. The global MutationCache handler in lib/queryClient.ts only reacts to 401:
  if (
    error.response?.status === 401 &&
    !error.config?.url?.includes("/login/access-token") &&
    window.location.pathname !== "/login"
  )

Failure scenario: shift-end returns 422/500 (e.g. session already closed on another terminal). `onSuccess` in terminal.tsx:706-711 never runs, so `setEndFor(null)` never fires; the ConfirmDialog stays open with `loading={endSession.isPending}` back to false, and the operator sees a dialog that appears to have done nothing.

**Tuzatish:** Add `onError: (err) => { toast.error(detailMessage(err)); setEndFor(null) }` to the `endSession` mutation, matching the other three mutations in the file.

### `frontend/src/routes/terminal.tsx:355` — O'LIK KOD

The whole 60s undo-window timer (`now` state, `setNow`, the 60s `setTimeout`) can never fire, because a second effect nulls `lastScan` after 15s — `canUndo` is therefore identical to `lastScan?.kind === "ok"`.

**Hujjat:** docs/06-frontend-components.md:427 / docs/05-frontend-app.md:232

**Dalil:** terminal.tsx:355-363:
  const [now, setNow] = useState(() => Date.now())
  useEffect(() => {
    if (lastScan?.kind !== "ok") return
    const left = 60_000 - (Date.now() - lastScan.at)
    if (left <= 0) return
    const t = setTimeout(() => setNow(Date.now()), left)
    return () => clearTimeout(t)
  }, [lastScan])
  const canUndo = lastScan?.kind === "ok" && now - lastScan.at < 60_000

terminal.tsx:367-371 (same dep, fires 45s earlier and clears the 60s timer via its cleanup):
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!lastScan) return
    const t = setTimeout(() => setLastScan(null), 15_000)
    return () => clearTimeout(t)
  }, [lastScan])

Both effects depend on [lastScan]. At T+15s setLastScan(null) changes lastScan, the 60s effect's cleanup runs clearTimeout(t), and the re-run returns early at `if (lastScan?.kind !== "ok") return`. setNow is never called, so `now` stays frozen at mount time and `now - lastScan.at` is always negative.

The comment above it (terminal.tsx:352-354) is therefore also false:
  // BEKOR QILISH oynasi 60 s (TZ 8.6). Vaqtga bog'liq bo'lgani uchun taymer
  // kerak: busiz tugma muddat o'tgach ham yoqilgan ko'rinib turardi va
  // bosilganda server rad etardi.

**Tuzatish:** Delete the `now` state and its effect and write `const canUndo = lastScan?.kind === "ok"` — or, if the real 60s window is wanted in the UI, stop clearing `lastScan` at 15s and instead keep the strip while hiding/disabling the undo button after `SCAN_UNDO_SECONDS`.

terminal.tsx:714-730 the dialog's own open flag is independent of lastScan, and every branch reads lastScan:
  <ConfirmDialog
    open={undoOpen}
    ...
    description={
      lastScan?.kind === "ok"
        ? `${lastScan.code} scan bekor qilinadi, qoldiq qaytariladi.`

    }
  />

Failure scenario: operator taps BEKOR QILISH at T+2s, is interrupted, taps "Scanni bekor qilish" at T+20s. lastScan is already null, so `onConfirm` short-circuits, no request is sent, no toast appears, and `undoOpen` is only ever set false inside undoMutation's onSuccess/onError — so the dialog stays on screen doing nothing.

**Tuzatish:** Close the dialog when the scan expires (e.g. add `if (undoOpen) return` to the 15s effect, or `useEffect(() => { if (!lastScan) setUndoOpen(false) }, [lastScan])`), and snapshot `scanId` into state when the dialog opens instead of reading `lastScan` at confirm time.


## infra

### `.github/workflows/ci.yml:20` — BUG

The CI "Lint (ruff)" step can never fail on any auto-fixable ruff violation: `backend/pyproject.toml` sets `fix = true`, so `ruff check app` silently rewrites the runner's checkout and exits 0. Lint is effectively unenforced in CI.

**Hujjat:** docs/07-infra-devops.md:416 — "| `backend` | ... → `ruff check app` + `ruff format --check app` → `mypy app`"; :438 — "remote'da GitHub Actions har push/PR'ni tekshiradi"

**Dalil:** ci.yml:20 — `run: cd backend && uv run ruff check app && uv run ruff format --check app`
backend/pyproject.toml:57-58 — `[tool.ruff]` ... `fix = true`
Verified by running the exact CI command in backend/ against a probe file with an I001 (unsorted-import) violation:
  `ruff check app` -> stdout "Found 1 error (1 fixed, 0 remaining).", exit=0
  `ruff format --check app` -> exit=0
The probe file was rewritten in place and the whole step reported success.

**Tuzatish:** Add `--no-fix` to the CI (and Makefile) invocation: `uv run ruff check --no-fix app`. Leave `fix = true` for the pre-commit hook / `make format` path, or drop `fix = true` from `[tool.ruff]` and keep the explicit `--fix` that `.pre-commit-config.yaml:23` already passes.

### `docker-compose.prod.yml:55` — BUG

`${TAG:-latest}` default hech qachon mavjud emas — CI faqat `:<git sha>` tegini push qiladi, ya'ni hujjatda ko'rsatilgan `make docker-prod` (TAG'siz) `manifest unknown` bilan yiqiladi.

**Hujjat:** docs/07-infra-devops.md:84 va :95

**Dalil:** docker-compose.prod.yml:55 `image: ${REGISTRY:-ghcr.io/dshukriddinov}/mebelix-mes-backend:${TAG:-latest}` va :52-54 izoh `# Default latest — lokal qo'lda ishga tushirishga.` | .github/workflows/ci.yml:83 `tags: ghcr.io/${{ github.repository_owner }}/mebelix-mes-${{ matrix.name }}:${{ github.sha }}` (repo bo'ylab `latest` teg push qiladigan boshqa joy yo'q) | Makefile:`docker-prod:` -> `docker compose -f docker-compose.prod.yml pull` (TAG berilmaydi) | docs/07:95 `Ishga tushirish: .env ni production qiymatlar bilan to'ldirish ..., so'ng make docker-prod.` | docs/07:84 `defaultlar lokal qo'lda ishga tushirishga.`

**Tuzatish:** Yo CI'da `:latest` tegini ham push qilish (`tags:` ga ikkinchi qator), yo `${TAG:?TAG majburiy}` qilib defaultni olib tashlash va docs/07:84,95 dagi "defaultlar lokal qo'lda ishga tushirishga" jumlasini `make docker-prod TAG=<sha>` ko'rinishiga yangilash.

### `scripts/backup.sh:27` — BUG

`env_get` strips everything from the first `#` onward, so any `#` inside POSTGRES_USER / POSTGRES_DB / ADMIN_PASSWORD / TG_BOT_TOKEN silently truncates the value — the DB dump gets encrypted with a password that is not the admin password, making every backup undecryptable by the documented recipe.

**Hujjat:** docs/07-infra-devops.md:224 — "`env_get` helperi faqat kerakli kalitlarni ... `sed` bilan o'qiydi, qiymatni qo'shtirnoq va satr oxiridagi izohdan tozalaydi"

**Dalil:** scripts/backup.sh:27 — `sed -n "s/^${1}=//p" "$2" | tail -1 | sed 's/[[:space:]]*#.*$//; s/^"\(.*\)"$/\1/; s/^'"'"'\(.*\)'"'"'$/\1/'`
scripts/backup.sh:32 — `export ADMIN_PASSWORD="$(env_get ADMIN_PASSWORD .env)"`
scripts/backup.sh:79 — `openssl enc -aes-256-cbc -pbkdf2 -pass env:ADMIN_PASSWORD -out "$FILE"`
Ran the exact helper against a probe .env:
  `ADMIN_PASSWORD="Str0ng#Pass!2026"` -> ["Str0ng]   (truncated AND keeps the opening quote)
  `POSTGRES_DB="mes#db"`              -> ["mes]
  `TG_BOT_TOKEN=123:AAF#xyz`          -> [123:AAF]
The `: "${ADMIN_PASSWORD:?...}"` guard on line 39 does not catch this — the value is non-empty, just wrong. The Telegram caption on line 90 still tells the operator "parol: admin parol".

**Tuzatish:** Only strip a comment that is preceded by whitespace and is outside quotes, or drop comment-stripping entirely and strip quotes only. Minimal fix: `sed 's/[[:space:]]\{1,\}#.*$//; s/^"\(.*\)"$/\1/; ...'` — and additionally strip quotes *before* comment handling so `"a#b"` survives. Safest is to read values with `grep -m1` + a quote-strip only, dropping the `#` rule.

### `backend/app/api/routes/orders.py:100` — BUG

_order_read_with_client resolves the client through crud_clients.get, which filters soft-deleted rows, so every mutation response blanks client_name while GET /orders/ and GET /orders/{id} still return the real name.

**Hujjat:** docs/03-backend-api.md 287-qator ('`OrderDetail`: mijoz ismi ...')

**Dalil:** Code (orders.py:100-101): `    client = await crud_clients.get(db, id=order.client_id)\n    return _order_read(order, client.full_name if client else "")`
crud_clients is a plain BaseCRUD with `exclude_deleted: bool = True` (backend/app/crud/base.py:17) whose `_build_query` adds `is_deleted == False`.
GET /orders/{id} deliberately does the opposite (orders.py:199): `    client = await db.get(Client, order.client_id)  # include soft-deleted: history must render`
GET /orders/ inner-joins without the filter (orders.py:149-150): `        select(Order, Client.full_name)\n        .join(Client, Client.id == Order.client_id)`

**Tuzatish:** Change _order_read_with_client to `client = await db.get(Client, order.client_id)` so PATCH/confirm/cancel/deliver/start return the same name the list and detail endpoints do.

### `backend/app/api/routes/orders.py:136` — BUG · 2 ta mustaqil agent tasdiqladi

On the number-collision retry path create_order reads client.full_name after db.rollback() has expired every identity-map object, so the documented retry ends in a lazy-load error instead of a 201.

**Hujjat:** docs/03-backend-api.md 284-qator ('ikki manager poygasida unique index hakam — `IntegrityError` bo'lsa 3 martagacha qayta uriniladi')

**Dalil:** Code (orders.py:118-121): `        except IntegrityError:\n            await db.rollback()\n            if attempt == 2:\n                raise DuplicateValueException("Buyurtma raqami band. Qayta urining.") from None`
Code (orders.py:136): `    return _order_read(order, client.full_name)`
`client` was loaded before the loop (orders.py:106): `    client = await crud_clients.get(db, id=body.client_id)`; the session is an AsyncSession (backend/app/core/db.py:28: `local_session = async_sessionmaker(bind=async_engine, class_=AsyncSession, expire_on_commit=False)`), where touching an expired attribute outside an awaited call raises MissingGreenlet.

**Tuzatish:** Capture the name before the loop (`client_name = client.full_name`) and return `_order_read(order, client_name)`, so no ORM attribute is read after a rollback.

### `backend/app/api/routes/orders.py:842` — BUG

`_resize_instances` re-mints qr_text by the same rule but skips the collision pre-check ingest performs, so a count change can hit the unique index and return 500 — exactly the failure the doc says was designed away.

**Hujjat:** docs/11-detal-id-nomlash.md:48-51 — "**ID to'qnashuvini oldindan aniqlash**: ... ingest unique-indeks 500 xatosi o'rniga do'stona `ParseError` beradi ... (`ingest.py ingest_project`)"; :59 — "Bazadagi `part_instance.qr_text` | `ingest.py` (`ParsedPart.qr_texts`), matritsada son o'zgarsa `orders.py _resize_instances`"

**Dalil:** ingest.py:276-284 pre-checks every QR (`if qr in qr_owner: raise ParseError(f"Detal ID to'qnashuvi: {qr} ...")`). The matrix path has no equivalent: orders.py:842 `qr = key if new_count == 1 else f"{key}_{n}"` writes straight into `existing[n].qr_text` / a new `PartInstance`, and the guarding index is models/order.py:123 `Index("ix_part_instance_order_qr", "order_id", "qr_text", unique=True, postgresql_where=text("NOT is_deleted"))`. Concrete failure: one order holds part A code `010` (count 1 -> QR `P_010`) and part B code `010_2` (count 1 -> QR `P_010_2`); both pass ingest's pre-check. Texnolog sets part A count 1->2 at orders.py:563 `await _resize_instances(db, part, edit.count)`; the loop then writes `P_010_1` and `P_010_2`, and `P_010_2` collides with part B -> IntegrityError -> 500, with no Uzbek message.

**Tuzatish:** Before writing, load the order's other live `qr_text` values (`select(PartInstance.qr_text).where(PartInstance.order_id == part.order_id, PartInstance.part_id != part.id, PartInstance.is_deleted == False)`) and raise the same friendly 422/`"Detal ID to'qnashuvi: ..."` message when a newly computed QR is already taken; best factored as one shared checker used by both `ingest_project` and `_resize_instances`.

### `backend/app/api/routes/orders.py:1110` — BUG

The whole .project ingest — XML parse, blob decode, cut-map build, SCX XML generation and one synchronous write per SCX file — runs inline on the event loop, freezing every terminal, TABLO and WebSocket in the worker for the duration of the upload.

**Dalil:** orders.py:1110 `project = parse_project(content)` (input up to `MAX_PROJECT_FILE_SIZE = 50 * 1024 * 1024`), orders.py:1125 `summary = await ingest_project(...)` → ingest.py:245 `(scx_dir / filename).write_text(content, encoding="utf-8")` inside `for filename, content, meta in build_scx_files(...)`, one call per instance of every CNC part, plus ingest.py:299 `project_path.write_bytes(raw_content)`. The project already knows the remedy and uses it elsewhere: security.py:48/53 `return await run_in_threadpool(...)` with `set_threadpool_tokens()` raising `THREADPOOL_TOKENS` at setup.py:91.

**Tuzatish:** Wrap the CPU/disk phases in `run_in_threadpool`: `project = await run_in_threadpool(parse_project, content)`, and move the file-writing part of `write_and_register_scx` (build the (filename, content) list first, then `await run_in_threadpool(_write_all, ...)`). The same treatment is needed for `excel.xlsx_response` (`wb.save(buf)` at excel.py:41), `orders.download_order_scx_zip` (`zipfile.ZipFile(...).write` loop) and `terminal.render_instance_label` (`render_label_png` at terminal.py:1120).

Har buyurtma uchun BUTUN `agg` lug'ati qayta skanerlanadi — kvadratik ish (buyurtmalar × buyurtmalar×postlar), o'sha faylning o'zi optimallashtirgan issiq yo'lda.

**Hujjat:** docs/03-backend-api.md:478 ("Marshrutlar ORM obyekti sifatida emas, faqat kerakli ustunlar bilan o'qiladi" — TABLO ning issiq yo'li)

**Dalil:** tablo.py:94-104 `rows = [TabloOrderRow(..., stages={str(posts_by_id[pid].code): stage for (oid, pid), stage in agg.items() if oid == o.id}) for o in orders]` — `agg` da buyurtma×post kalitlari bor, ya'ni 300 buyurtma × ~2700 kalit = ~810 000 iteratsiya har so'rovda; so'rov esa devor ekranidan har 60 s va har WS eventda keladi (frontend/src/queries/mes.ts `refetchInterval: 60_000`).

**Tuzatish:** `agg` ni bir marta buyurtma bo'yicha guruhlang: `by_order: dict[int, dict[str, StageAgg]]` ni `for row in routes` siklining ichida to'ldiring va `stages=by_order.get(o.id, {})` bering — bitta o'tish, qo'shimcha xotira yo'q.

**Tuzatish:** Add one `post_ctx(*allowed: PostCode)` dependency factory in `app/api/deps.py` that calls `resolve_terminal`, maps `ScanRejected` to the 422 body, and optionally asserts the post code; replace `_terminal_ctx`, `_pack_ctx` and `_wh_ctx` with `PostCtx = Annotated[..., Depends(post_ctx())]` / `Depends(post_ctx(PostCode.UPAKOVKA))` / `Depends(post_ctx(PostCode.TMO))`.

### `backend/app/api/routes/terminal.py:969` — HUJJAT DRIFTI

Seven WS event types are published by the backend but consumed by no frontend subscriber; the label-print chain doc claims they produce a live browser update.

**Hujjat:** docs/07-infra-devops.md:247 — "├── ws_manager.publish(\"label.printed\", …)         → brauzerlarga live yangilanish"

**Dalil:** There are exactly four `useMesWs` consumers (`grep -rn "useMesWs" frontend/src`): terminal.tsx:166-170 matches an exact list — `"scan.recorded", "scan.cancelled", "order.started", "order.cancelled"` plus `scan.rejected` at terminal.tsx:191; tablo.tsx:255 `event.type.startsWith("scan.") || event.type.startsWith("order.")`; _layout/index.tsx:72-74 `"scan." || "order." || "session."`; _layout/printers.tsx:182 `event.type.startsWith("bridge.")`. None of those prefixes or names covers the events actually published at: terminal.py:969 `ws_manager.publish("label.printed", {"instance_id": instance_id, "qr_text": qr_text})`, terminal.py:1108 `"label.cancelled"`, pachka.py:322 `"pachka.item"`, pachka.py:425 `"pachka.closed"`, tmo.py:275 `"tmo.accepted"`, tmo.py:320 `"tmo.shipped"`, tmo.py:350 `"tmo.delivered"`. Conversely, every event type the frontend subscribes to IS published, so there is no phantom-consumer half. `grep -rn "label\.printed|pachka\.item|tmo\.accepted" frontend/src` hits only generated `src/client/` docstrings.

**Tuzatish:** Either subscribe (add `label.`/`pachka.`/`tmo.` to the terminal handler so a second monoblok sees a colleague's print/pack/receipt without waiting for the 30 s poll), or correct docs/07-infra-devops.md:247, docs/01-architecture.md:268 and docs/09-mes-domain.md:391-392 to stop claiming live browser delivery for events nothing listens to.

### `backend/app/commands/seed_defaults.py:138` — BUG

Post-account phone numbers are derived from the POSITION of the role inside POST_ROLES, so inserting a tenth post role anywhere but at the end makes the seed try to insert a phone an existing account already owns — a UNIQUE violation that aborts prestart and blocks the whole deploy.

**Hujjat:** docs/04-backend-data.md:492

**Dalil:** seed_defaults.py:127 `for index, (role, post_code) in enumerate(POST_ROLES.items(), start=1):` and :138 `phone=f"+9980000000{index:02d}"`; models/user.py:15 `Index("ix_user_phone", "phone", unique=True, postgresql_where=text("NOT is_deleted"))`. Existing accounts keep the phone they got at creation time, so any reordering desynchronises index↔phone. That this happens in practice is documented in the same file: seed_defaults.py:49-50 "order_index 5 (eski PAZ) ataylab bo'sh: ... qayta raqamlash esa mavjud bazalarda bekorga siljish beradi".

**Tuzatish:** Derive the phone from something stable instead of the loop index — e.g. a literal `"phone"` key inside each POST_ROLES entry, or `f"+998000000{list(UserRole).index(role):03d}"` pinned once — or make the insert tolerant: look up by `username=str(role)` first (already unique per role) and skip/patch instead of inserting a positional phone.

Tashkilot yaratish shoxi hech qachon ishlamaydi — org qatorini `1393a241b793` migratsiyasi qo'yadi va prestart migratsiyani seed'dan OLDIN yuritadi; hujjatning o'zi ham buni tan oladi.

**Hujjat:** docs/04-backend-data.md:481 (§5.2)

Migratsiya 1393a241b793:659 `"""INSERT INTO organization (name, phones, currency, timezone, settings, is_deleted)`. Doc docs/04:481 `Kod o'zgarmagan, lekin bu shox endi **hech qachon ishlamaydi**: org qatorini `1393a241b793` migratsiyasi qo'yadi`

**Tuzatish:** `org` so'rovi va `if org is None` blokini (3 qator + `Organization` importi) o'chirish; docs/04:481 ni mos ravishda qisqartirish.

### `backend/app/commands/seed_defaults.py:162` — BUG

PATCH /posts/{id} lets an admin change order_index, but seed_defaults resets it to the hardcoded table on every container start, so the edit is silently reverted at the next deploy.

**Tuzatish:** Pick one owner. Either drop `order_index` from `PostUpdate` (schemas/posts.py:23) so the seed is authoritative, or restrict the seed's sync to newly-inserted posts (delete the `elif` branch at seed_defaults.py:162-166).

### `backend/app/core/config.py:79` — ORTIQCHA

STRICT_STAGE_ORDER is a feature flag with exactly one read and no place that ever sets it to False; turning it off would silently break the E004 contract the docs describe as mandatory.

**Hujjat:** docs/02-backend-core.md:72 (`STRICT_STAGE_ORDER=True` (E004 — oldingi qadam tugamaguncha skanni bloklash, TZ 7.2.5 …))

**Tuzatish:** Delete the setting and the `if` guard; keep the ordering check unconditional (scanning.py:557-574 de-indents). ~4 lines removed and one untested code path (flag=False) disappears.

### `backend/app/core/config.py:117` — BUG

`POSTGRES_URI` interpolates the raw `POSTGRES_PASSWORD` into a DSN without percent-encoding, so any password containing a URL-reserved character produces a malformed connection URL.

**Hujjat:** docs/02-backend-core.md:79 ("Computed field'lar: `POSTGRES_URI` (`user:pass@host:port/db`)")

**Dalil:** config.py:117-119 `        credentials = f"{self.POSTGRES_USER}:{self.POSTGRES_PASSWORD}"` / `        location = f"{self.POSTGRES_SERVER}:{self.POSTGRES_PORT}/{self.POSTGRES_DB}"` / `        return f"{credentials}@{location}"`, consumed verbatim by db.py:16 `DATABASE_URL = f"{DATABASE_PREFIX}{DATABASE_URI}"` and db.py:20 `    DATABASE_URL,`.

**Tuzatish:** Wrap the user and password in `urllib.parse.quote_plus(...)` inside the `POSTGRES_URI` computed field (or build the DSN with `sqlalchemy.engine.URL.create(...)`, which escapes components itself).

### `backend/app/core/middleware.py:23` — O'LIK KOD

`request.state.request_id` is written on every request but never read anywhere in the repo.

**Hujjat:** docs/02-backend-core.md:154 (`request.state.request_id`ga saqlaydi va javob header'iga qo'shadi)

**Dalil:** middleware.py:23 `request.state.request_id = request_id`. Repo-wide grep for `request_id` across *.py/*.ts/*.tsx/*.js/*.json (excluding node_modules) returns only middleware.py itself, logger.py's `_EXTRA_FIELDS` tuple and docs — no reader of `request.state.request_id` exists in backend or frontend/src.

**Tuzatish:** Either delete line 23, or actually use it — e.g. include `request.state.request_id` in the 500 error body / exception handler so clients can quote it in bug reports. Update docs/02-backend-core.md:154 accordingly if deleted.

### `backend/app/core/middleware.py:31` — BUG

An unhandled exception in a handler bypasses the access log and the response never carries X-Request-ID, so 500s — the requests that most need correlation — are invisible in the structured log.

**Hujjat:** docs/02-backend-core.md:156 (Boshqa so'rovlar uchun ... `logger.info("request", extra={request_id, method, path, status_code, duration_ms})` yoziladi)

**Dalil:** middleware.py:30-33 `start = time.perf_counter()` / `response = await call_next(request)` / `duration_ms = round(...)` / `response.headers[REQUEST_ID_HEADER] = request_id` — no try/except; Starlette builds the stack as `ServerErrorMiddleware` (outermost) + user middleware + `ExceptionMiddleware` (innermost), so an unhandled exception propagates past `RequestLoggingMiddleware` and lines 32-44 never run.

**Tuzatish:** Wrap `call_next` in try/except: on exception log the same `extra` dict with `status_code=500` and re-raise, so failures are logged with their request_id and duration.

### `backend/app/core/setup.py:138` — BUG

The hand-rolled /openapi.json handler rebuilds the schema with `application.version` (FastAPI's hardcoded default "0.1.0") and never passes `description`, so `APP_VERSION` and the "MebeliX API" description never reach the OpenAPI document.

**Hujjat:** docs/02-backend-core.md:202 ("`title` `settings.APP_NAME`dan, `description` (`\"MebeliX API\"`) esa literal")

**Dalil:** setup.py:138 `        return get_openapi(title=application.title, version=application.version, routes=application.routes)`; setup.py:151-152 `        title=settings.APP_NAME,` / `        description="MebeliX API",`; config.py:30 `    APP_VERSION: str = "0.1.0"  # reported by /health and /ready`. `create_application` never passes `version=` to `FastAPI(...)`, and fastapi/openapi/utils.py `get_openapi(*, title, version, openapi_version="3.1.0", summary=None, description=None, routes, ...)` defaults `description` to None.

**Tuzatish:** Pass `version=settings.APP_VERSION` into the `kwargs.update(...)` block in `create_application` so FastAPI's `application.version` is the configured one, and change line 138 to `return application.openapi()` (which honours title/description/version and caches the schema in `application.openapi_schema` instead of regenerating it on every request).

`_install_metrics` docstring'i `/metrics` ni proxy darajasida "faqat metrics tarmog'idan" cheklanadi deb yozadi, Caddy esa uni istisnosiz 403 qiladi — izoh eskirgan.

**Hujjat:** docs/07-infra-devops.md:156

In production, restrict access at the proxy layer (Caddy → only allow scrapes from the metrics network) — this endpoint is unauthenticated."""` | caddy/Caddyfile:38-47 `# Prometheus metrics tashqariga UMUMAN chiqmaydi. ... # Ilgari bu yerda `remote_ip private_ranges` matcher turardi ...` + `handle /metrics { respond "Forbidden" 403 }` | docs/07:156 `Endi matcher olib tashlangan — handle /metrics har doim 403 qaytaradi.`
