### `backend/app/core/worker.py:8` — ORTIQCHA

The arq worker imports the FastAPI application-factory module just to reuse a four-line `SELECT 1` probe, dragging the entire web stack into the worker process.

**Hujjat:** docs/02-backend-core.md:238 (**Bog'lanishlar:** `config.py`, `setup.py` (`check_database_connection`), `backend/app/tasks/__init__.py`)

**Dalil:** worker.py:8 `from app.core.setup import check_database_connection`; setup.py:11-24 imports `from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, FastAPI`, `from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware`, `from prometheus_fastapi_instrumentator import Instrumentator`, `from uvicorn.middleware.proxy_headers import ProxyHeadersMiddleware`, `from app.api.deps import get_current_superuser` (which pulls `app.crud`, `app.models.user`, `app.core.security`).

**Tuzatish:** Move `_retry_async` + `check_database_connection` (and `check_redis_connection`) into a small `app/core/probes.py` that imports only `db.engine`, `anyio` and `settings`; have both `setup.py` and `worker.py` import from there.

### `backend/app/core/worker.py:20` — BUG

Every arq log line in the worker is emitted twice — once as plain text and once as JSON — because arq's CLI applies its own dictConfig after this module has already installed the JSON root handler.

**Hujjat:** docs/02-backend-core.md:219 (`setup_logging()` chaqiriladi — worker protsessi ham app protsessidagidek JSON log yozadi)

**Dalil:** worker.py:20 `setup_logging()  # arq worker entrypoint — JSON logging, xuddi app protsessidagidek`; logger.py:34-36 `root = logging.getLogger()` / `root.handlers = [handler]`. arq/cli.py runs `worker_settings_ = cast(..., import_string(worker_settings))` and only afterwards `logging.config.dictConfig(log_config)` with `default_log_config` = `{'disable_existing_loggers': False, 'loggers': {'arq': {'handlers': ['arq.standard'], 'level': log_level}}}` — no `propagate` key, so `logging.config.DictConfigurator.configure_logger` leaves `logger.propagate` at True and arq records reach both the plain `arq.standard` handler and the JSON root handler.

**Tuzatish:** Pass arq a log config that does not double-handle: run the worker with `arq app.core.worker.WorkerSettings --custom-log-dict app.core.logger.ARQ_LOG_DICT` where that dict sets `{'loggers': {'arq': {'handlers': [], 'propagate': True}}}`, or drop `setup_logging()` from worker.py and let the custom log dict install JsonFormatter.

### `backend/app/core/ws.py:6` — TOZALASH

The module docstring claims a "Redis disabled (dev)" mode that cannot exist — there is no setting to disable Redis and the lifespan unconditionally starts the pub/sub listener.

**Hujjat:** docs/02-backend-core.md:283 ("Redis yo'q (dev) yoki yiqilgan bo'lsa **lokal yetkazishga degradatsiya** qiladi")

**Dalil:** ws.py:5-6 `"""...gunicorn workers deliver to their local sockets; with Redis disabled (dev) the / dispatch is local-only."""`. config.py contains only `REDIS_HOST`, `REDIS_PORT` and the computed `REDIS_URL` — no enable/disable flag (docs/02-backend-core.md:68 itself says "yoqish/o'chirish bayroqlari yo'q", and line 195 says "Redis ilovaning majburiy bog'liqligi"). setup.py:105-106 always runs `ws_redis = AsyncRedis.from_url(settings.REDIS_URL)` / `await ws_manager.start_listener(ws_redis)`.

**Tuzatish:** Reword the docstring to "before the lifespan starts the listener, or when a `PUBLISH` fails, dispatch degrades to local-only", and fix the matching phrase "Redis yo'q (dev)" in docs/02-backend-core.md:283.

### `backend/app/core/ws.py:105` — BUG

stop_listener re-raises a dead listener task's exception, which aborts the rest of the lifespan shutdown and leaks the Redis pools.

**Hujjat:** docs/02-backend-core.md:195 (`finally`da WS listener to'xtatiladi, WS Redis klienti, `queue.pool` va `rate_limiter.client` yopiladi)

**Dalil:** ws.py:102-105 `self._listener.cancel()` / `with contextlib.suppress(asyncio.CancelledError):` / `await self._listener` — cancelling an already-finished task is a no-op and the `await` re-raises whatever exception killed it (only `CancelledError` is suppressed). setup.py:110-117 `finally:` / `await ws_manager.stop_listener()` / `if ws_redis is not None: await ws_redis.close()` / `await queue.pool.aclose()` / `await rate_limiter.client.aclose()` — everything after line 111 is skipped.

**Tuzatish:** Suppress all exceptions when awaiting the cancelled listener: `with contextlib.suppress(Exception): await self._listener` (or catch and log), so shutdown always reaches the pool close calls.

### `backend/app/crud/base.py:17` — ORTIQCHA · 2 ta mustaqil agent tasdiqladi

`exclude_deleted` — repo bo'ylab hech qachon override qilinmagan class-knob; hujjat esa uni override qilishni yo'l sifatida taklif qiladi.

**Hujjat:** docs/04-backend-data.md:326 (§3.1)

**Dalil:** base.py:17 `exclude_deleted: bool = True` with docstring `"""When True, automatically filters out soft-deleted records (is_deleted=False).` / `Override at the class level or pass is_deleted explicitly to bypass."""`. Repo-wide grep for `exclude_deleted` returns only base.py:17 and base.py:30 (`if self.exclude_deleted and hasattr(...)`). Both subclasses (CRUDUser, CRUDOrder) leave it alone.

**Tuzatish:** Drop the attribute and the `self.exclude_deleted and` conjunct in `_build_query`; the `"is_deleted" not in kwargs` escape hatch already covers the documented bypass. ~4 lines removed.

### `backend/app/crud/base.py:71` — BUG

get_multi always runs a COUNT query, but three of its four call sites throw the count away — a wasted aggregate on every machines/agents/employees request.

**Dalil:** base.py:71-72 `count_query = select(func.count()).select_from(query.subquery())` / `total = (await db.execute(count_query)).scalar_one()`. Discarding callers: employees.py:23 `posts, _ = await crud_posts.get_multi(db, limit=100)`, bridge_agents.py:91 `agents, _total = await crud_bridge_agents.get_multi(db, limit=100)`, machines.py:93 `machines, _total = await crud_machines.get_multi(db, limit=500)`. Only users.py:112 uses the total.

**Tuzatish:** Add a plain `async def list_all(self, db, limit=..., **kwargs) -> list[ModelType]` (query without the count) and use it at the three sites, or give get_multi a `with_total: bool = True` and pass False. Saves one round-trip per call.

### `backend/app/migrations/env.py:18` — TAKROR

Alembic rebuilds the exact async DSN from the five individual Postgres settings instead of importing the `DATABASE_URL` that `core/db.py` already exposes, so the URL is constructed in two places.

**Hujjat:** docs/02-backend-core.md:95 ("`DATABASE_URL = POSTGRES_ASYNC_PREFIX + POSTGRES_URI` — asyncpg driver bilan to'liq URL")

**Dalil:** migrations/env.py:16-19 `config.set_main_option(` / `    "sqlalchemy.url",` / `    f"{settings.POSTGRES_ASYNC_PREFIX}{settings.POSTGRES_USER}:{settings.POSTGRES_PASSWORD}@{settings.POSTGRES_SERVER}:{settings.POSTGRES_PORT}/{settings.POSTGRES_DB}",` vs db.py:14-16 `DATABASE_URI = settings.POSTGRES_URI` / `DATABASE_PREFIX = settings.POSTGRES_ASYNC_PREFIX` / `DATABASE_URL = f"{DATABASE_PREFIX}{DATABASE_URI}"`. env.py already imports from that module: env.py:10 `from app.core.db import Base`.

**Tuzatish:** In migrations/env.py replace the f-string with `from app.core.db import DATABASE_URL, Base` and `config.set_main_option("sqlalchemy.url", DATABASE_URL)`; then any escaping/driver fix in `POSTGRES_URI` applies to migrations too.

### `backend/app/migrations/env.py:18` — BUG

The Alembic DB URL is built by raw f-string interpolation, so a POSTGRES_PASSWORD (or user/db) containing `@`, `/`, `:`, `#` or `?` silently produces a wrong host/user/database instead of failing loudly.

**Hujjat:** docs/04-backend-data.md §4.2, line 389

**Dalil:** env.py:16-19: `config.set_main_option(\n    "sqlalchemy.url",\n    f"{settings.POSTGRES_ASYNC_PREFIX}{settings.POSTGRES_USER}:{settings.POSTGRES_PASSWORD}@{settings.POSTGRES_SERVER}:{settings.POSTGRES_PORT}/{settings.POSTGRES_DB}",\n)`. Verified with the project's own SQLAlchemy: `make_url('postgresql+asyncpg://postgres:p@ss/w0rd@db-host:5432/mes')` → host='ss', username='postgres', password='p', database='w0rd@db-host:5432/mes'. `scripts/prestart.sh:11` runs `python -m alembic upgrade head` on every deploy, so a rotated password with a special character breaks the deploy.

**Tuzatish:** Percent-encode the credentials before interpolation, e.g. `from urllib.parse import quote_plus` and use `quote_plus(settings.POSTGRES_USER)` / `quote_plus(settings.POSTGRES_PASSWORD)`, or build the URL with `sqlalchemy.engine.URL.create(...)`. (`backend/app/core/config.py:116-119` `POSTGRES_URI` has the identical defect and should be fixed the same way.)

### `backend/app/migrations/env.py:31` — O'LIK KOD

Offline mode (`run_migrations_offline`) can never complete: two data migrations dereference `op.get_bind()`, which is None offline, so `alembic upgrade head --sql` crashes.

**Hujjat:** docs/04-backend-data.md §4.2, line 392 ("Offline rejim: `literal_binds` bilan SQL chiqaradi")

**Dalil:** env.py:77-78 `if context.is_offline_mode():` / `    run_migrations_offline()`. Running `alembic upgrade head --sql` against this chain aborts with: `File ".../versions/b2c7e40f9a13_part_instance_cut_meters.py", line 42, in upgrade` / `    ).fetchall()` / `AttributeError: 'NoneType' object has no attribute 'fetchall'` — from b2c7e40f9a13:39-42 `bind = op.get_bind()` … `orders = bind.execute(sa.text('SELECT id, cutmap FROM "order" ...')).fetchall()`. a7c419e0b3d5:77 `conn = op.get_bind()` has the same problem. No Makefile target, script or doc uses `--sql` (`grep -rn -- '--sql' Makefile scripts/ docs/` → nothing; only `alembic upgrade head` in Makefile:64, scripts/setup.sh:28, scripts/prestart.sh:11).

**Tuzatish:** Either delete `run_migrations_offline` and the `is_offline_mode()` branch (env.py:31-48, 77-78) and drop the "Offline rejim" sentence from docs line 392, or guard the two data migrations with `if op.get_context().as_sql: return`. Do not leave the doc claiming offline mode works.

### `backend/app/migrations/script.py.mako:8` — BUG

The revision template emits `from typing import Sequence, Union`, which fails the project's own ruff config (UP035 + I001), so every autogenerated migration must be hand-edited before it can be committed.

**Hujjat:** docs/04-backend-data.md §4.3, line 396

**Dalil:** script.py.mako:8 `from typing import Sequence, Union`. Running the project's ruff (`.venv/bin/ruff check --config pyproject.toml`) on a file generated from this template reports: `UP035 [*] Import from \`collections.abc\` instead: \`Sequence\`` and `I001 [*] Import block is un-sorted or un-formatted`. `UP` is selected in backend/pyproject.toml:71 and only UP006/UP007 are ignored (lines 79-80), so UP035 fires. Every hand-written revision already carries the corrected form, e.g. e1c262d7e2ae_init.py:9-10 `from collections.abc import Sequence` / `from typing import Union`.

**Tuzatish:** Change script.py.mako line 8 to `from collections.abc import Sequence` plus `from typing import Union` in isort order (alembic import after sqlalchemy), matching e1c262d7e2ae_init.py:9-13. Optionally enable the already-present ruff post_write_hook in alembic.ini:77-81 so generated files are fixed automatically.

### `backend/app/migrations/versions/f3fad5351d8a_oee_toxtalishlar_va_ideal_tezlik.py:40` — O'LIK KOD

Migratsiya head'ida ORM modeli yo'q 6 ta jadval va 3 ta ustun qolgan — hujjatdagi 18 jadval bazadagi holatni to'liq ifodalamaydi.

**Hujjat:** 09-mes-domain.md:8 — "18 jadval, hammasi `BaseModel` (ID + timestamps + soft-delete)"

**Dalil:** Zanjir head'i `a7c419e0b3d5` (42 migratsiya, tarmoqsiz). `f3fad5351d8a` `stop_reason` (40-qator) va `machine_stop` (62-qator) jadvallarini, `machine.planned_minutes_per_day` (82) va `machine.ideal_rate` (85) ustunlarini yaratadi; `b2c7e40f9a13:37` `part_instance.cut_meters` qo'shadi; `b4d1e79c53a8:29` `stock_item`, `c9a3e15d84b6:44,67,86` `sale`/`payment`/`stock_move` yaratadi. `grep -rn drop_table` bo'yicha bulardan HECH BIRI keyingi migratsiyada tashlanmagan (faqat `defect` — `a1c9d4f2b60e:33`, `material` — `d2b71f4c86e9:32`). `backend/app/models/` da bu jadvallarga model yo'q (`__init__.py:22-43` — 18 ta model). Ustiga-ustak `f3fad5351d8a` docstring'i "Sabablar ro'yxati ... `seed_defaults` da to'ldiriladi" deydi, lekin `app/commands/seed_defaults.py` da stop_reason seed'i umuman yo'q.

**Tuzatish:** Yo modelsiz jadvallarni/ustunlarni drop qiluvchi migratsiya yozish, yo 09-mes-domain.md §1 ga "modelsiz meros jadvallar" qatorini qo'shib holatni ochiq yozish.

### `backend/app/models/enums.py:55` — TAKROR

The operator-role → post mapping lives in three hand-maintained lists (backend `POST_ROLES`, frontend `POSITIONS`, frontend `OPERATOR_ROLES`) and is never served over the API.

**Hujjat:** docs/04-backend-data.md §1.2 line 53

**Dalil:** Backend (enums.py:55-67): `POST_ROLES: dict[UserRole, PostCode] = { UserRole.ARRACHI: PostCode.ARRA, ... UserRole.OMBORCHI: PostCode.TMO, }`. Frontend copy 1 (`frontend/src/lib/mes.ts:174-186`): `export const POSITIONS: {...}[] = [ { label: "Arrachi", kind: "tsex", post: "ARRA", role: "arrachi" }, ... { label: "TMO omborchisi", kind: "tsex", post: "TMO", role: "omborchi" }, ...]` with the comment "Rol backend `POST_ROLES` bilan bir xil / jadval". Frontend copy 2 (`frontend/src/lib/mes.ts:197-207`): `export const OPERATOR_ROLES = ["arrachi", "kromkachi", "ovalchi", "prisadkachi", "roverchi", "stolyar", "boyoqchi", "upakovkachi", "omborchi"] as const`.

**Tuzatish:** Derive `OPERATOR_ROLES` from `POSITIONS` (`POSITIONS.filter(p => p.kind === "tsex").map(p => p.role)`) so the frontend holds one list, and expose `POST_ROLES` on an existing endpoint (e.g. the posts list) so a tenth post never needs three edits.

### `backend/app/models/order.py:20` — ORTIQCHA

`organization_id` is copy-pasted onto six models with a hardcoded `default=1` and is never assigned, filtered, joined or exposed anywhere — pure multi-tenant scaffolding with zero users.

**Hujjat:** docs/04-backend-data.md §1.4 line 92

**Dalil:** Identical line in six files: `order.py:20`, `client.py:12`, `employee.py:19`, `machine.py:27`, `post.py:15`, `bridge_agent.py:20` — all `organization_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("organization.id"), default=1, kw_only=True)`. `grep -rn "organization_id" backend/app frontend/src` (minus migrations) returns ONLY those six model declarations — no query filters by it, no schema returns it, no code ever passes a value. The `Organization` table itself has 3 references total (`seed_defaults.py:15,150,152`). Doc (04 line 92): "Ko'p jadvalda `organization_id` FK (`default=1`) — sxema multi-tenant'ga tayyor (TZ 4.1), hozircha bitta \"Mebelix\" tashkiloti."

**Tuzatish:** Either extract the repeated column into an `OrgMixin` in `models/base.py` (the file already holds the mixin pattern) so it is declared once, or drop `organization_id` from the six models and the DB until a second tenant actually exists.

### `backend/app/models/scan.py:16` — O'LIK KOD

`WorkSession.user_id` is write-only: it is populated on every session open but no query, schema or UI ever reads it back.

**Hujjat:** docs/04-backend-data.md:100 — Employee row describes the user↔employee link; no doc claims `WorkSession.user_id` is read

Only write: scanning.py:191 `session = WorkSession(employee_id=employee.id, user_id=user.id, machine_id=machine.id, post_id=post.id)`. `grep -rn "WorkSession.user_id\|session.user_id" backend/app frontend/src` returns only the prose comment at models/user.py:36. Reports join `WorkSession` on `machine_id`/`employee_id` only (reports.py:89-96), and `logout.py:37` joins through `Employee.user_id`, not `WorkSession.user_id`.

**Tuzatish:** Drop the column, or use it where its intent is already documented — e.g. `logout.logout` could close sessions by `WorkSession.user_id == current_user.id` instead of the `Employee.user_id` join, which would also close sessions opened for users without an Employee row.

### `backend/app/models/scan.py:17` — BUG

work_session.machine_id has no index, yet it is the filter of the hottest query in the system — crew_sessions() runs on every scan, every session open/close and every terminal poll.

**Dalil:** models/scan.py:17 `machine_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("machine.id"), default=None, kw_only=True)` — no `index=True`, while the neighbouring `employee_id` (:15) and `status` (:19) both have one; migration 1393a241b793 creates only `ix_work_session_employee_id`, `ix_work_session_is_deleted`, `ix_work_session_status`. scanning.py:249-253 `select(WorkSession).where(WorkSession.machine_id == machine_id, WorkSession.status == SessionStatus.ACTIVE, ...)` is called from `active_session` (every scan), `open_badge_session` (every login) and the terminal endpoints; reports.py:88 also joins `ws.machine_id == Scan.machine_id` over the whole scan table.

**Tuzatish:** Add a composite index matching the query shape: `Index("ix_work_session_machine_status", "machine_id", "status")` on `WorkSession.__table_args__` plus a migration `op.create_index(...)`. The single-column `ix_work_session_status` becomes redundant and can be dropped in the same revision.

### `backend/app/schemas/users.py:50` — BUG · 2 ta mustaqil agent tasdiqladi

`name` is validated up to 100 chars but the `user.name` column is VARCHAR(30) and was never widened, so any 31+ char name returns 500 from POST /users/ and PATCH /users/me.

**Dalil:** schemas/users.py:48-50: `#: 30 belgi yetmasdi: "Abdurahmonov Abdurahmon Abdurahmonovich" — 39 belgi.` / `#: \`employee.full_name\` allaqachon 100 belgi, ikkalasi bir xil bo'lsin.` / `_NAME_FIELD = Field(min_length=2, max_length=100, examples=["Abdurahmonov Sherzod"], default=None)`  ||  models/user.py:18: `name: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(30), default=None, kw_only=True)`  ||  migrations/versions/e1c262d7e2ae_init.py:26: `sa.Column("name", sa.String(length=30), nullable=True),`  ||  `grep -rn alter_column app/migrations/versions/*.py | grep -i "'user'"` → hech narsa (ustunni kengaytiruvchi migratsiya YO'Q)  ||  docs/04:79: `| \`name\` | \`String(30)\` | NULL | — | — |`

**Tuzatish:** Yangi alembic migratsiya qo'shing: `op.alter_column('user', 'name', type_=sa.String(100))` va `models/user.py:18` da `String(30)` → `String(100)`. Muqobil: `_NAME_FIELD` da `max_length=100` → `max_length=30` ga qaytaring va 48-49 dagi izohni olib tashlang. Ikkalasi ham qilinmasa `POST /users/` va `PATCH /users/{id}` uzun ismda asyncpg `value too long for type character varying(30)` bilan 500 beradi.

docs `_NAME_FIELD` ni 2–30 deb yozadi, kod 2–100 qiladi — hujjat eskirgan.

### `backend/app/services/excel.py:52` — BUG

`_cell` `datetime` ni `TASHKENT_TZ` ga o'tkazmasdan formatlaydi, shuning uchun buyurtmalar hisobotining .xlsx eksporti UTC soatlarini (Toshkentdan 5 soat orqada) chiqaradi.

**Hujjat:** docs/02-backend-core.md:80 — "`TASHKENT_TZ = ZoneInfo("Asia/Tashkent")` — tsex mahalliy vaqti, hisobot/audit kunlari va chek timestamp'lari shu zonada (TZ 10.4; `audit.py`, `reports.py` route'lari ... ishlatadi)"

**Dalil:** excel.py:50-54 — `def _cell(v: Any) -> Any:` / `    if isinstance(v, datetime):` / `        return v.strftime("%d.%m.%Y %H:%M")`. Formatlanayotgan qiymatlar tz-aware UTC: backend/app/models/base.py:14-16 `created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(UTC), server_default=text("current_timestamp(0)"))`, backend/app/models/order.py:49-51 `production_started_at`/`completed_at`/`delivered_at` ham `DateTime(timezone=True)`. reports.py:191-219 ular to'g'ridan-to'g'ri uzatiladi: `r.created_at,` / `r.production_started_at,` / `r.completed_at,` / `r.delivered_at,`. Xuddi shu route'ning qolgan hamma joyi Toshkent bo'yicha ishlaydi (reports.py:152 `today = datetime.now(TASHKENT_TZ).date()`, :136 `func.date_trunc("day", func.timezone(TASHKENT_TZ.key, member.c.at))`).

**Tuzatish:** `_cell` da aware datetime'ni avval zonaga o'tkazish: `if isinstance(v, datetime): return (v.astimezone(TASHKENT_TZ) if v.tzinfo else v).strftime("%d.%m.%Y %H:%M")` (`from app.core.config import TASHKENT_TZ`).

### `backend/app/services/giblab/cutmap.py:33` — O'LIK KOD

`CutmapResult.problems` is always empty on every returned object — any non-empty value raises before the return — and no caller reads it.

**Dalil:** cutmap.py:33 `problems: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)`; cutmap.py:254-256 `if result.problems:` / `raise ParseError("Kesish xaritasi mos kelmadi: " + " · ".join(result.problems[:5]))` / `return result`. Repo-wide grep for `.problems` matches only cutmap.py itself (lines 144, 192, 229, 252, 254, 255); ingest.py:293/317-319/437/443 uses only `cutmap.bindings` and `cutmap.model`.

**Tuzatish:** Make `problems` a plain local list inside `build_cutmap` and remove the field from `CutmapResult`.

### `backend/app/services/giblab/cutmap.py:113` — O'LIK KOD

The horizontal branch of the leading-trim cut is unreachable: `leading_trim` is only ever set when `root_axis_vertical` is True.

**Dalil:** cutmap.py:109-114 `if pattern.leading_trim:` / `cuts.append(\n    {"x1": cursor, "y1": 0.0, "x2": cursor, "y2": pattern.width}\n    if pattern.root_axis_vertical\n    else {"x1": 0.0, "y1": cursor, "x2": pattern.length, "y2": cursor}\n)`. In csblob.py:120-129 the only branch that assigns a non-None `leading_trim` is `elif slot_depth == 1 and slot_type == 1:` which sets `root_vertical = True` / `leading_trim = slot_value`; the other two branches set `leading_trim = None` (and the `slot_type == 1 and slot_value == 0` branch is checked first, so `slot_value` there is non-zero, i.e. truthy).

**Tuzatish:** Drop the ternary and emit the vertical cut unconditionally inside `if pattern.leading_trim:`, since `BlobPattern.leading_trim` implies `root_axis_vertical`.

### `backend/app/services/giblab/cutmap.py:197` — BUG

`rot` is computed only from the rotated-match test, so a near-square part placed UNROTATED is flagged as rotated and its kromka symbols are swapped.

**Dalil:** cutmap.py:197-203 — `rot = (\n    part.length is not None\n    and part.width is not None\n    and part.length != part.width\n    and abs(cell["w"] - part.width) < 1.5\n    and abs(cell["h"] - part.length) < 1.5\n)`. There is no check that the UNROTATED interpretation (`abs(cell["w"] - part.length) < 1.5 and abs(cell["h"] - part.width) < 1.5`) does not also fit. The result feeds `_edge_markers(part, rot)` (cutmap.py:204), whose docstring says "eW = [left, right] and eH = [top, bottom] symbols as placed (rotation swaps l/r ↔ t/b)".

**Tuzatish:** Test the unrotated fit first and only set `rot=True` when the placed size matches the swapped pair AND not the direct pair, e.g. `fits_direct = abs(cell["w"]-part.length)<1.5 and abs(cell["h"]-part.width)<1.5; rot = not fits_direct and abs(cell["w"]-part.width)<1.5 and abs(cell["h"]-part.length)<1.5`.

### `backend/app/services/giblab/ingest.py:147` — TAKROR

The whole custom-route build (KROMKA first-pass per_instance rule + UPAKOVKA/TMO tail, comments included) is duplicated verbatim between restore_custom_routes and orders._set_custom_route.

**Dalil:** ingest.py:147-193 `edge_count = len(part.edges) or 1` … `first_edge_pass = post_code == PostCode.KROMKA and post_code not in seen` … `tail = [(15, PostCode.UPAKOVKA)] if packaging else []` / `tail.append((16, PostCode.TMO))` is byte-for-byte the same logic (and the same Uzbek comments «OVAL bu yerda YO'Q: oval kromkada detal bitta o'tishda ishlanadi — bitta skan.») as orders.py:769-812. ingest.py:174 even points at it: «orders._set_custom_route bilan bir xil qoida».

**Tuzatish:** Extract one helper (e.g. `giblab/ingest.build_route_rows(part, codes, posts, packaging, start_seq)` returning the PartRoute rows incl. the tail) and call it from both `_set_custom_route` and `restore_custom_routes`; today a fix to the KROMKA/tail rule has to be made twice.

### `backend/app/services/giblab/ingest.py:148` — TAKROR

Nostandart marshrutni qadam-qadam qurish mantig'i ikki joyda deyarli aynan takrorlanadi.

**Hujjat:** docs/10-giblab-reference.md:320-321 — "qadoqlashni anketadagi \"Upakovka qilish\" bayrog'i qo'shadi, TMO esa har marshrutga majburiy oxirgi qadam bo'lib tushadi (`ingest.py`, `orders._set_custom_route`)"

**Dalil:** ingest.py:147-193 (`restore_custom_routes`) va orders.py:769-810 (`_set_custom_route`) bir xil qadamlarni yozadi: `edge_count = len(part.edges) or 1` (ingest.py:147 / orders.py:769), `first_edge_pass = post_code == PostCode.KROMKA and post_code not in seen` (ingest.py:156 / orders.py:779), `tail = [(15, PostCode.UPAKOVKA)] if packaging else []` (ingest.py:176 / orders.py:800), va ikkalasida ham `if ... part.assembly_parent_id is None` sharti (ingest.py:175 / orders.py:799). ingest.py:174 izohi buni tan oladi: "(orders._set_custom_route bilan bir xil qoida)".

**Tuzatish:** Qadam ro'yxatini (`codes` + dum) `PartRoute` qatorlariga aylantiradigan bitta yordamchini `giblab/ingest.py` da ajratib, `orders._set_custom_route` shuni chaqirsin — qoida bitta joyda qolsin.

### `backend/app/services/giblab/ingest.py:218` — BUG

wipe_previous_parse unlinks the old .project/SCX files from disk inside an uncommitted transaction; if ingest/restore later raises ParseError the DB is rolled back but the files are already gone.

**Dalil:** ingest.py:216-220 `for path in paths: try: (upload_root / path).unlink(missing_ok=True)`. Caller: orders.py:1128 `await wipe_previous_parse(db, order, root)` then orders.py:1130-1134 `summary = await ingest_project(...)` / `restore_custom_routes(...)` … `except ParseError as exc: await db.rollback()`. ingest_project can still raise after that point — ingest.py:404 `raise ParseError(f"Post sozlanmagan: {post_code}…")` — and by then it has also written new files (ingest.py:299 `project_path.write_bytes(raw_content)`, ingest.py:245 `(scx_dir / filename).write_text(...)`), which stay on disk unreferenced.

**Tuzatish:** Collect the paths to delete and perform the unlink only after the caller commits (return them from wipe_previous_parse and delete in upload_order_project after `await db.commit()`); likewise write the new .project/SCX bytes to disk only after the commit, or delete them in the except branch.

### `backend/app/services/giblab/ingest.py:387` — BUG

A part that GibLab listed in the CS operation but never placed (usedCount=0) still gets an ARRA route step, so its ARRA counter can never be closed and the order can never reach COMPLETED.

**Hujjat:** docs/09-mes-domain.md:80-81; docs/10-giblab-reference.md:280-281

**Dalil:** ingest.py:387 `sheet_part = project.generation != "processed" or pp.material_name or pp.label_num is not None` — but parser.py:463-469 sets `ref_part.label_num`/`ref_part.material_name` for EVERY `<part>` stub of a CS operation, placed or not. ingest.py itself warns about exactly these parts a few lines later: ingest.py:449-450 «Kesish xaritasida {len(ghosts)} ta detalning joyi yo'q: … Ular arrachi ro'yxatiga tushmaydi.» Docs: docs/09:80-81 «ARRA (kod 1, listda joyi bor detalga, per_instance=1)», docs/10:280-281 «ARRA (kod 1, listga joylashtirilgan detalga)». ARRA has no scanner — the label print from a map cell is the scan (docs/09:411-414) — and a cell-less part has no cell to click, so scanning.py:672-683 (`all_left == 0` → OrderStatus.COMPLETED) never fires.

**Tuzatish:** Gate the ARRA step on actual placement: `sheet_part = pp.key in bindings_by_key` when a cutmap was built (fall back to the material_name/label_num heuristic only when `cutmap is None`), so ghost parts (usedCount=0, CL profiles) get no unreachable ARRA step.

### `backend/app/services/giblab/models.py:156` — BUG

`merged_machining` da qirra teshiklari imzosi `z` ni hisobga olmaydi — bir tomonda bir xil pozitsiya/diametr/chuqurlikdagi, lekin BOSHQA balandlikdagi teshik dublikat deb tashlanadi.

**Dalil:** models.py:155-159: `            for b in op.edge_bores:` / `                sig = (b["side"], round(b["pos"], 2), b["d"], round(b["dp"], 2))` / `                if sig not in seen_edge:` — `b["z"]` imzoda yo'q, holbuki xnc.py:99 uni yozadi: `op.edge_bores.append({"side": side, "pos": pos + step * i, "z": z, "d": d, "dp": dp, "thru": False})`. Natija: `cutmap.py:238` dagi `holes` overlayi va `ingest.py` dagi `xnc_summary["edge_bores"]` sanog'i ikki jismoniy teshikni bitta deb ko'rsatadi (masalan 32 mm qo'shqavat plitada har qatlam markazida bittadan teshik: side="t", pos=100, d=8, dp=30, z=8 va z=24).

**Tuzatish:** `sig` ga `round(b["z"], 2)` ni qo'shish — yuza teshiklari imzosida `thru` bo'lgani kabi qirra teshiklarida ham balandlik farqlovchi bo'lishi kerak.

### `backend/app/services/giblab/models.py:177` — ORTIQCHA

stage_sides() returns a dict keyed by PostCode that can only ever contain PRISADKA, and its single caller ignores the key anyway by hardcoding process code 6.

**Dalil:** models.py:177-187 `result: dict[PostCode, int] = {}` / `for op in self.xnc_ops:` / `if op.bores or op.edge_bores or op.grooves or any(op.mills):` / `result[PostCode.PRISADKA] = result.get(PostCode.PRISADKA, 0) + 1` / `return result` — PostCode.PRISADKA is the only key ever written, as the docstring states: 'FAQAT PRISADKA — ROVER bosqichi ataylab yaratilmaydi'. Sole caller ingest.py:392-393 `for post_code, per_instance in pp.stage_sides().items():  # faqat PRISADKA` / `steps.append((6, post_code, per_instance))  # 6 = Prisadka`.

**Tuzatish:** Replace with `def prisadka_passes(self) -> int:` returning the count, and change the caller to `if (n := pp.prisadka_passes()): steps.append((6, PostCode.PRISADKA, n))`. Removes the dict, the loop over one key, and the misleading generality.

### `backend/app/services/giblab/parser.py:100` — BUG · 2 ta mustaqil agent tasdiqladi

Kodsiz detal ogohlantirishi faqat mahsulotning BIRINCHI detali kodsiz bo'lganda yoziladi — qisman kodsiz fayllarda (korpusdagi `kupe` 38/70, `kuxniya` 22/191) avtomatik kod jimgina beriladi.

**Hujjat:** docs/10-giblab-reference.md §1 (37-43) va §2.1.2 (184-186)

**Dalil:** parser.py:99 `code = f"{len(product.parts) + 1:03d}"` runs for every code-less part, but the warning is guarded one line later: parser.py:100 `if len(product.parts) == 0:`. So for a product whose parts 1-5 carry codes and part 6 does not, `len(product.parts) == 5` and no warning is appended, yet that part still receives the generated code `006` — and per parser.py:109 `code = _dedupe_part_code(code, seen, product.code, warnings)` a generated `006` that clashes with a real `006` silently becomes `006-2`. The doc states the warning unconditionally.

**Tuzatish:** Track auto-numbering per product instead of testing `len(product.parts) == 0` — e.g. pass a per-product `auto_coded: set[str]`/bool from `parse_project` (parser.py:350-354, where `seen` is already created per product) and append the warning the first time a code is generated for that product, regardless of position.

### `backend/app/services/giblab/parser.py:342` — BUG

The documented name-as-product-code fallback can produce a value longer than the `Product.code` column, crashing ingest instead of failing with a parse message.

**Hujjat:** docs/11-detal-id-nomlash.md:20 — "`100701` | .project dagi mahsulot `code` (bo'lmasa `name`, u ham bo'lmasa `PRODUCT`)"

**Dalil:** parser.py:342 `product_code = g.get("code") or g.get("name") or "PRODUCT"` — the only validation that follows is the path-character check at parser.py:343-344 `if re.search(r"[\\/]|\.\.", product_code): raise ParseError(...)`; there is no length check. The value is stored via ingest.py:326 `code=parsed_product.code` into models/order.py:71 `code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(50), kw_only=True)  # e.g. 301401`, while the same `<good name>` also feeds `name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(255))` (models/order.py:72) — i.e. names up to 255 chars are expected, but as a code fallback anything over 50 raises StringDataRightTruncation (500) rather than a texnolog-readable error.

**Tuzatish:** In `parse_project`, right after the path-character check, validate the product code length against the column (50) — either `raise ParseError(f"Mahsulot kodi juda uzun: {product_code!r}")` or trim to 50 and append a warning, mirroring how the part-code fallback already warns.

### `backend/app/services/giblab/parser.py:373` — BUG

`<part>` havolasi yoki `program` i yo'q XNC operatsiyasi butunlay JIM tashlab yuboriladi — butun ishlov (teshik, paz, freza), PRISADKA qadami va SCX geometriyasi ogohlantirishsiz yo'qoladi.

Taqqoslash uchun xuddi shu funksiyada noma'lum detalga ishora qilgan XNC ogohlantiriladi — parser.py:395: `project.warnings.append(f"XNC operatsiya noma'lum detalga ishora qiladi: part id={pid}")`, va docs/10-giblab-reference.md:669 shu qatorni sanaydi: "| XNC operatsiya noma'lum detalga ishora qiladi | shu dasturning BUTUN ishlovi (teshik, paz, freza) detalga biriktirilmaydi va yo'qoladi |".

### `backend/app/services/giblab/scx.py:196` — BUG

A mill contour that contains only its `ms` head crashes `_offset_contour` with IndexError; the guard at line 169 only covers the empty-list case, which xnc.py can never produce.

**Dalil:** scx.py:169-170 `if not contour:` / `continue`; scx.py:196 `hx, hy, segments = _offset_contour(contour, width, radius, comp)`; scx.py:350-351 `out_pts: list[tuple[float, float]] = [(0.0, 0.0)] * len(pts)` / `out_pts[0] = seg_pts[0][0]` — `seg_pts` is empty when `contour` has one element (`segs` is built by skipping `contour[0]`, scx.py:306-309). xnc.py:190-201 always appends the contour on `<ms>` (`op.mills.append(current_contour)`) whether or not any `ml`/`mac` follows, and xnc.py:174 `if not xml_bool(el.get("enable"), True): continue` drops disabled `ml` elements before they reach the contour.

**Tuzatish:** Replace `if not contour: continue` with `if len(contour) < 2: continue` (a single point produces no `<Line>` elements anyway), or make `_offset_contour` return `(head_x, head_y, [])` when `segs` is empty.

### `backend/app/services/giblab/xnc.py:99` — O'LIK KOD

The `thru` flag on edge bores is hardcoded `False`, propagated through the cut-map model into the public API schema, and never read by any consumer.

**Dalil:** xnc.py:99 `op.edge_bores.append({"side": side, "pos": pos + step * i, "z": z, "d": d, "dp": dp, "thru": False})`; carried on at cutmap.py:243 `{"side": b["side"], "pos": b["pos"], "d": b["d"], "dp": b["dp"], "thru": b["thru"]}` and declared required at terminal.py:303 `thru: bool` in `class CutmapEdgeBore`. **Tuzatish:** Drop `"thru": False` from the dict built in xnc.py:99, remove `"thru": b["thru"]` from the edge entry in cutmap.py:243, and remove the `thru: bool` field from `CutmapEdgeBore` in backend/app/schemas/terminal.py:303 (regenerate the frontend client). Update the `edge_bores` comment in models.py:106 to `# {side, pos, z, d, dp}`.

### `backend/app/services/labels.py:155` — ORTIQCHA · 3 ta mustaqil agent tasdiqladi

### `backend/app/services/labels.py:420` — HUJJAT DRIFTI

docs/07 labels.py da faqat ikkita chiqish (`load_label_data` + `render_label_png`) bor deb jadval beradi, aslida uchta render funksiyasi bor va uchalasi ham o'sha bridge/GoDEX zanjiridan o'tadi.

**Dalil:** Hujjat: "`backend/app/services/labels.py` da yorliqning yagona chiqish formati bor" + faqat `load_label_data` va `render_label_png` qatorli jadval. `ponytail:` izohi endi mavjud bo'lmagan PAZ postiga ishora qiladi — PAZ `PostCode` dan olib tashlangan, haqiqiy approksimatsiya esa OVAL (va UPAKOVKA) postida.

**Tuzatish:** Izohni haqiqiy holatga ko'chirish: `ponytail: OVAL (va UPAKOVKA) birligi dona bilan approksimatsiya qilinadi — egri qirra uzunligi/kvadratura detal bo'yicha saqlanmaydi`. Aks holda `/ponytail-debt` ledgeri mavjud bo'lmagan post uchun qarz ko'rsatadi.

### `backend/app/services/metrics.py:26` — BUG

`part_volume` `Post.unit` ni o'qimaydi: UPAKOVKA posti birligi `m2` bo'lsa ham dona qaytaradi, natijada TABLO va dashboard "m²" yorlig'i ostida DONA sonini ko'rsatadi.

**Hujjat:** docs/09-mes-domain.md:525-527 ("ARRA m², KROMKA metr, qolganlari dona; OVAL birligi metr bo'lsa ham dona bilan approksimatsiya") — UPAKOVKA holati umuman yozilmagan

**Dalil:** metrics.py:11 docstring `"""Full-part volume in the post's unit.` bilan boshlanadi, lekin kod faqat post KODiga qaraydi: metrics.py:19 `if post_code == PostCode.ARRA:`, :21 `if post_code == PostCode.KROMKA:`, :26 `    return float(count)`. seed_defaults.py:72-78 esa UPAKOVKA ni m2 qilib seed qiladi va sababini yozadi: `# Qadoqlash — bo'yoqdan keyingi oxirgi bosqich. Birligi m2: ish haqi` / `# qadoqlangan detallar kvadraturasi bo'yicha hisoblanadi.` ... `"unit": PostUnit.M2,`. Iste'molchilar birlikni `Post.unit` dan oladi: reports.py:358 `unit=unit_labels.get(str(post.unit), "") if post else "",` (reports.py:344 `unit_labels = {"m2": "m²", "meter": "metr", "piece": "dona"}`) va tablo.py:161 `unit=str(p.unit),` -> frontend/src/routes/tablo.tsx:408 `{UNIT_LABELS[p.unit] ?? p.unit}`.

**Tuzatish:** UPAKOVKA uchun ARRA bilan bir xil m² formulasini qo'shish (`((length or 0) * (width or 0) / 1_000_000) * count`) — seed'dagi ish haqi izohi aynan shuni talab qiladi. Agar approksimatsiya ataylab bo'lsa, `seed_defaults` da UPAKOVKA birligini `PostUnit.PIECE` ga tushirish, aks holda ekranda "m²" yozib dona ko'rsatiladi.

### `backend/app/services/scanning.py:595` — BUG

`record_scan_at` dagi `except IntegrityError` bloki erishib bo'lmaydigan — poyga 422 emas, 500 beradi.

**Dalil:** backend/app/services/scanning.py:587-597 tartibi: `db.add(scan)` → 591 `await db.execute(update(PartRoute)...)` → 592 `await db.refresh(route, ["total_done"])` → 593 `try: await db.flush()` / 595 `except IntegrityError`. `local_session = async_sessionmaker(bind=async_engine, class_=AsyncSession, expire_on_commit=False)` (app/core/db.py:28) — `autoflush` sukut bo'yicha True, ya'ni 591-qatordagi `execute()` va 592-qatordagi `refresh()` kutayotgan `Scan` INSERT'ini o'zi flush qiladi. `ix_scan_client_event_id` UNIQUE buzilsa IntegrityError AYNAN 591-qatorda ko'tariladi, `try` blokidan tashqarida — 593-qatordagi `flush()` ga yetib borilmaydi.

**Tuzatish:** `try` blokini `db.add(scan)` dan boshlab 591-592 qatorlarni ham ichiga olish, yoki `db.execute(...).execution_options(synchronize_session=False)` o'rniga avval `await db.flush()` ni chaqirib, keyin UPDATE yuborish.

agent.py:270 `    root.mainloop()`

    shart emas, ish oqimini to'smaydi. Alohida threadda: asyncio loop
    (flusher/uplink) bloklanmasin.`

### `bridge-agent/agent.py:787` — BUG

Navbat fayli bilan ishlashda (local_drain/local_rewrite/local_enqueue) hech qanday xato ushlanmaydi; bitta OSError (Windows'da antivirus/backup faylni qulflaganda odatiy) `asyncio.gather` orqali butun agentni o'ldiradi, `__main__` esa faqat KeyboardInterrupt va SystemExit ni ushlaydi — oynasiz exe hech qanday xabar bermay jimgina o'ladi va chop etish to'xtaydi.

**Hujjat:** docs/07-infra-devops.md:354 — «Exe `--noconsole` bilan qurilgani uchun doimiy oyna yo'q»

**Dalil:** agent.py:787 `    await asyncio.gather(uplink(), flusher(), log_shipper())`
agent.py:421 `    for line in QUEUE_FILE.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():`
agent.py:433-436 `    with tmp.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as f:` ... `    tmp.replace(QUEUE_FILE)`
agent.py:793-798 `    except KeyboardInterrupt:` / `    except SystemExit as exc:` — umumiy `except Exception` yo'q

**Tuzatish:** flusher/uplink sikllarini `try/except Exception: log.exception(...); await asyncio.sleep(5)` bilan o'rash (vazifa o'lmasin) va `__main__` ga `except Exception` qo'shib `_alert(..., error=True)` chiqarish — aks holda nosozlik faqat agent.log da qoladi va log_shipper ham o'lgani uchun serverga yetmaydi.

**Dalil:** DOC (docs/03-backend-api.md:201): "| DELETE | `/api/v1/users/{user_id}` | Login (o'zi yoki superuser) | path: `user_id: int` | `{\"message\": ...}` | Soft-delete; superuser o'z hisobini o'chira olmaydi; oddiy user faqat o'zini o'chira oladi |"  CODE (backend/app/api/routes/users.py, delete_user): "role = UserRole(db_user.role)\n    if role in (UserRole.TERMINAL, UserRole.TABLO) or role in POST_ROLES:\n        raise ForbiddenException(f\"{db_user.username} — qurilma yoki post hisobi, odam emas. ...\")".

**Tuzatish:** Add the device/post-account 403 block to the DELETE /{user_id} row (and note it also applies to DELETE /users/me, which delegates to `delete_user`).

### `docs/03-backend-api.md:380` — HUJJAT DRIFTI

The described work-center fallback for operator roles is wrong: it is the `post_machine` status-preference rule, not "the post's first work center", and a bound-but-inactive machine also falls back.

**Hujjat:** docs/03-backend-api.md §terminal.py intro paragraph, line 380

**Dalil:** DOC (docs/03-backend-api.md:380): "`resolve_terminal` operator rollarida postni rolning o'zidan oladi (ish markazi — `user.machine_id`, bo'lmasa postning birinchi ish markazi)"  CODE (backend/app/services/scanning.py, resolve_terminal): "if machine is not None and (machine.is_deleted or machine.status == MachineStatus.INACTIVE):\n                machine = None  # biriktirilgani ishlamaydi — postdan boshqasini olamiz\n        if machine is None:\n            machine = await post_machine(db, post)"; `post_machine` filters `Machine.status != MachineStatus.INACTIVE` and orders by `_STATUS_PREFERENCE, Machine.id`.

**Tuzatish:** Reword line 380 to: work center is `user.machine_id`, but if that machine is deleted or INACTIVE (or absent) it falls back to `scanning.post_machine` — INACTIVE never chosen, ACTIVE preferred over REPAIR, tie broken by id (same rule as `/login/badge`, docs/09 §3.0.1 step 3).

CODE (backend/app/api/routes/terminal.py, cancel_instance_label): "if caller_post.code not in (PostCode.ARRA, PostCode.ROVER):\n            raise ForbiddenException(\"Chekni faqat kesish posti (ARRA yoki ROVER) bekor qila oladi.\")" and "if instance.label_post_id is not None and instance.label_post_id != caller_post.id: ... raise ForbiddenException(f\"Bu chekni {owner.name ...} chiqargan — bekor qilishni o'sha post bajaradi.\")".

**Tuzatish:** Add to the row: non-manager/texnolog/admin callers must be on ARRA or ROVER (403 otherwise), any foreign auto-scan `post_id` gives 403, and `instance.label_post_id` mismatch gives 403; `label_post_id` is also reset to None on cancel.

`stage_sides()` teshigi bor operatsiyaga emas, ISHLOVI bor har operatsiyaga (teshik/paz/freza) bitta PRISADKA skani beradi.

**Hujjat:** (`stage_sides()` teshigi bor har operatsiyaga bitta PRISADKA skani beradi — paz/freza esa marshrut qadamini umuman yaratmaydi, §2.3).

**Dalil:** backend/app/services/giblab/models.py:178-181 — `for op in self.xnc_ops:` / `if op.bores or op.edge_bores or op.grooves or any(op.mills):` / `result[PostCode.PRISADKA] = result.get(PostCode.PRISADKA, 0) + 1`.

**Tuzatish:** "teshigi bor har operatsiyaga" -> "ishlovi (teshik, paz yoki freza) bor har operatsiyaga", va "paz/freza esa marshrut qadamini umuman yaratmaydi" jumlasini olib tashlash.

### `docs/10-giblab-reference.md:764` — HUJJAT DRIFTI

orders.py `ingest` submodulidan uchtasini emas, BESHTA funksiyani import qiladi — marshrut snapshot/restore juftligi ro'yxatdan tushib qolgan.

**Hujjat:** `ingest` funksiyalari (`ingest_project`, `wipe_previous_parse`, `write_and_register_scx`) submoduldan to'g'ridan-to'g'ri olinadi (`orders.py`)

**Dalil:** backend/app/api/routes/orders.py:65-71 — `from app.services.giblab.ingest import (` `ingest_project,` `restore_custom_routes,` `snapshot_custom_routes,` `wipe_previous_parse,` `write_and_register_scx,` `)`.

**Tuzatish:** Ro'yxatga `snapshot_custom_routes` va `restore_custom_routes` ni qo'shish (ular §7 da allaqachon tilga olingan).


## frontend

### `frontend/nginx/index.conf:21` — BUG

`try_files` oxirida `=404` turgani uchun `/index.html` ichki redirect emas, FAYL tekshiruvi bo'ladi — SPA chuqur havolalari (`/orders/123`) `location = /index.html` blokidan o'tmaydi va `Cache-Control: no-cache` header'ini OLMAYDI

**Hujjat:** docs/07-infra-devops.md:167 — "`location = /index.html` — `Cache-Control: no-cache`: `index.html` hech qachon keshlanmaydi, aks holda deploy'dan keyin brauzer eski hash'li assetlarga ishora qiluvchi eski SPA'ni ushlab qolib oq ekran berardi"

**Dalil:** index.conf:12-15:
```
  location = /index.html {
    root /usr/share/nginx/html;
    add_header Cache-Control "no-cache";
  }
```
index.conf:21: `    try_files $uri $uri/ /index.html =404;`
nginx semantikasi: `try_files` ning oxirgi parametri `=code` bo'lsa, undan oldingi HAMMA parametr (jumladan `/index.html`) fayl sifatida tekshiriladi va so'rov JORIY kontekstda (`location /`) qayta ishlanadi — ichki redirect bo'lmagani uchun `location = /index.html` ning `add_header` i qo'llanmaydi. `=404` ning o'zi ham hech qachon ishlamaydi, chunki `/index.html` doim mavjud.

**Tuzatish:** `try_files $uri $uri/ /index.html;` ga o'zgartirish (oxirgi parametr URI bo'lsa nginx ichki redirect qiladi va `location = /index.html` bloki, ya'ni no-cache, chuqur havolalarga ham qo'llanadi). Zaruriy bo'lsa `root /usr/share/nginx/html;` ni `server` darajasiga ko'tarib ikki blokdagi takrorni ham yo'q qilish.

### `frontend/src/components/Anketa/RoomPhotos.tsx:70` — BUG

Klient MIME tekshiruvi serverdan kengroq: `image/*` o'tadi, lekin server faqat jpeg/png/webp qabul qiladi — iPhone'ning HEIC surati (yoki GIF/SVG) klientdan o'tib serverda 422 bo'ladi.

**Hujjat:** docs/09-mes-domain.md:507-508 — "`POST /orders/{id}/survey/photos` → `upload_room_photo` ... Faqat `image/jpeg|png|webp` (`_ROOM_PHOTO_TYPES`)"; docs/06-frontend-components.md:76 — "Klient chegaralari: xonaga 6 rasm, har biri 5 MB gacha, faqat `image/*`"

**Dalil:** Kod (RoomPhotos.tsx:70-72): `if (!f.type.startsWith("image/")) { toast.error(`${f.name}: faqat rasm qo'shish mumkin`); continue }` va :166 `accept="image/*"`. Backend (backend/app/api/routes/orders.py:314): `_ROOM_PHOTO_TYPES = {"image/jpeg": ".jpg", "image/png": ".png", "image/webp": ".webp"}` + :336-337 `ext = _ROOM_PHOTO_TYPES.get(file.content_type or ""); if ext is None: raise HTTPException(status_code=422, ...)`. Komponentning o'z yordam matni ham (:197) `JPG, PNG, WEBP` deb va'da qiladi: `Sudrab tashlang yoki tanlang · JPG, PNG, WEBP · ${MAX_PHOTO_MB} MB gacha`.

**Tuzatish:** Klientda ham aniq ro'yxat: `const ACCEPTED = ["image/jpeg", "image/png", "image/webp"]`, `accept={ACCEPTED.join(",")}` va `if (!ACCEPTED.includes(f.type)) toast.error(`${f.name}: faqat JPG, PNG yoki WEBP`)` — serverga bormasdan tushunarli xabar (komponent izohi :65 aynan shuni va'da qiladi).

### `frontend/src/components/Anketa/RoomSummary.tsx:191` — TOZALASH

Fayl oxirida yana bitta yetim JSDoc izohi — u `Facts` ni tasvirlaydi, `Facts` esa `fields.tsx` da turadi.

Bu aynan `fields.tsx:13-16` dagi xulqning izohi: `const filled = items.filter(([, v]) => v?.trim())` / `if (!filled.length) return <p ...>To'ldirilmagan.</p>` — u yerda esa izoh yo'q.

### `frontend/src/components/Anketa/fields.tsx:39` — ORTIQCHA

`NumInput`, `Num`, `CheckGrid`, `ShapeChoice`, `Choice` primitivlaridagi `disabled?` propiga hech qachon `true` uzatilmaydi — yagona chaqiruvchi (anketa formasi) uni doim `!canEdit` = `false` bilan beradi.

**Hujjat:** docs/06-frontend-components.md:295-299 — `Field` / `Num` / `NumInput` / `CheckGrid` / `Choice` jadvali (`disabled` proplari hujjatda ham eslatilmagan)

**Dalil:** Kod: `fields.tsx:39` `disabled?: boolean` (NumInput), :131 (Num), :151 (CheckGrid), :194 (ShapeChoice), :267 (Choice) — beshtasida ham. Yagona chaqiruvchi `anketa.$orderId.tsx` bo'lib, u `{room && canEdit && (` (:341) bloki ichida hammasiga `disabled={!canEdit}` beradi (yuqoridagi topilma). Repo bo'ylab boshqa import yo'q: `grep -rn "NumInput|CheckGrid|ShapeChoice|Choice" frontend/src` faqat `fields.tsx` va `anketa.$orderId.tsx` ni beradi. Uslub klasslari ham shu bilan o'lik: `${disabled ? "cursor-default opacity-70" : ""}` (:166), `${disabled ? "opacity-70" : ""}` (:216, :285).

**Tuzatish:** Beshta primitivdan `disabled` propini va u bilan bog'liq `opacity-70` / `cursor-default` shoxlarini olib tashlash. Kelajakda haqiqiy "faqat o'qish" holati kerak bo'lsa — u allaqachon `RoomSummary` ko'rinishida bor.

### `frontend/src/components/Clients/AddClient.tsx:158` — BUG

`address` has a 255-char zod limit but no `FormMessage` and no `maxLength`, so an over-long address silently blocks submit with no visible reason.

**Hujjat:** docs/06-frontend-components.md:51 ("zod: full_name (min 2 belgi), phone/phone_alt ..., address, notes")

**Dalil:** AddClient.tsx:35 `address: z.string().max(255).optional(),` versus the field markup AddClient.tsx:158-169 which renders `<FormItem><FormLabel>Manzil</FormLabel><FormControl><Input {...field} /></FormControl></FormItem>` — no `<FormMessage />` (the `full_name`/`phone`/`phone_alt` fields all have one, lines 108, 131, 153). `form.handleSubmit((d) => mutation.mutate(d))` (line 96) never runs, so "Saqlash" appears dead.

**Tuzatish:** Add `<FormMessage />` to the address (and notes) FormItem and put `maxLength={255}` on the address Input.

### `frontend/src/components/Clients/ClientCombobox.tsx:37` — BUG

The visible label is never derived from `value`, so the trigger can show "Mijozni tanlang" while the form still holds a selected client id.

**Hujjat:** docs/06-frontend-components.md:61 ("Props: value/onChange, initialLabel? (tanlangan mijoz nomi tashqaridan kelganda...)")

**Dalil:** ClientCombobox.tsx:37 `const [label, setLabel] = useState(initialLabel ?? "")` — the only other writer is `setLabel(name)` in `select()` (line 53); `value` is used solely for the check mark (line 144 `{String(c.id) === value && (`). AddOrder.tsx:99-101 renders `<ClientCombobox value={field.value} onChange={field.onChange} />` with no `initialLabel`, and AddOrder only resets on success (AddOrder.tsx:61 `form.reset()` inside `onSuccess`). Open AddOrder → pick a client → close the dialog (DialogContent unmounts, react-hook-form state survives) → reopen: `client_id` is still set but the combobox remounts with an empty label, so the manager submits an order for a client they cannot see.

**Tuzatish:** Derive the label from `value`: keep a `selected` map/lookup (or reset `label` when `value` is empty via an effect), and have AddOrder call `form.reset()` in `onOpenChange` when the dialog closes.

### `frontend/src/components/Common/ErrorComponent.tsx:3` — BUG

The router's global errorComponent throws away TanStack Router's `error` and `reset` props, so every uncaught render/loader error shows one identical generic sentence with no detail and no retry path.

**Hujjat:** docs/06-frontend-components.md:149 — "**Maqsadi:** routerning global xato ekrani — `ErrorScreen` ga delegatsiya (`code="Xato"`, "Xatolik yuz berdi..." matni)."

**Dalil:** ErrorComponent.tsx:3-8: `export const ErrorComponent = () => (\n  <ErrorScreen\n    code="Xato"\n    message="Xatolik yuz berdi. Iltimos, qaytadan urinib ko'ring."\n  />\n)` — the component takes NO props at all. __root.tsx:79 wires it as `errorComponent: () => <ErrorComponent />`, explicitly discarding the `{ error, reset }` argument the router passes. Contrast QueryError.tsx:26-30, which does offer recovery: `{onRetry && (<Button variant="outline" onClick={onRetry}>Qayta urinish</Button>)}`.

**Tuzatish:** Give ErrorComponent an `({ error, reset })` signature; render `extractErrorMessage(error)` (already used in routes/login.tsx:140, 256) under the generic line and add a "Qayta urinish" button calling `reset()`. Wire it as `errorComponent: ErrorComponent` so the router's props actually arrive.

CustomRoutes.tsx:69-72 (the success path does clear both):

OrderMatrix and CustomRoutes destructure only `data`/`isLoading` from the matrix query, so a failed request renders a misleading "no parts yet" empty state instead of an error — there is no global query error toast to fall back on.

  const { data: matrix, isLoading } = useQuery(
    getOrderMatrixQueryOptions(orderId),
  )
OrderMatrix.tsx:104-109 (what the user sees on a 500):
  if (!matrix?.parts.length) {
    return (
      <p className=…>
        Detallar yo'q — avval .project fayl yuklanishi kerak.
CustomRoutes.tsx:32-34 — same destructuring; CustomRoutes.tsx:78-84 shows "Nostandart detal yo'q…" on error.
frontend/src/lib/queryClient.ts:6-17 — the only global `onError` handler redirects on 401 and does nothing else.

The "Nostandart · N qadam" badge counts raw `part.routes`, which includes the system tail steps (UPAKOVKA/TMO) the technologist never drew and disabled steps, so N is inflated by 1-2 relative to the route shown in the CustomRoutes tab.

                        : "Nostandart · marshrut yo'q"}

backend/app/api/routes/orders.py:489-503 — `read_order_matrix` puts every non-deleted PartRoute into `MatrixPart.routes` (`RouteCell` carries `is_enabled`, which is not filtered here).

### `frontend/src/components/People/PersonDialog.tsx:76` — HUJJAT DRIFTI

Docs claim post assignment "isn't locked, one person can work two posts" — the dialog has no post picker at all and derives exactly one post from the position.

Code: the whole rendered form (PersonDialog.tsx:280-389) contains only F.I.Sh, Telefon, Lavozim, "Lavozim nomi", the shopLogin Checkbox and Login/Parol — there is no posts control anywhere, and `postsFor` (lines 76-87) returns `[post.id]` or `[]`. Docs are wrong (or the picker was removed and the doc not updated).

### `frontend/src/components/People/PersonDialog.tsx:141` — BUG

Phone is turned into a payload by stripping non-digits with no `PHONE_PATTERN` check, so a half-typed number is sent as e.g. `+99890` and the server 422s.

**Hujjat:** docs/05-frontend-app.md:137 (`optionalFormattedPhoneSchema` / `formattedPhoneSchema` are the shared phone contract)

**Dalil:** PersonDialog.tsx:141-142 `const digits = form.phone.replace(/\D/g, "")` / `const phone = digits ? `+${digits}` : null`; the only gate is PersonDialog.tsx:255-256 `(kind === "ofis" || withLogin) && !phone ? "Kirish uchun telefon kerak — login o'shandan olinadi"`. With "+998 90" in the field, `digits` = "99890" → `phone` = "+99890", `valid` is true, and `UsersService.createUser({ body: { ... phone: phone ?? "" ... } })` (line 195) hits the server's `PHONE_PATTERN`. lib/validation.ts:46-50 already exports `formattedPhoneSchema` for exactly this.

**Tuzatish:** Validate before enabling Saqlash: `const parsed = formattedPhoneSchema.safeParse(form.phone)` and use `parsed.data` as the canonical phone, adding a "Telefon +998XXXXXXXXX formatida" entry to `missing`.

### `frontend/src/components/People/PersonDialog.tsx:191` — BUG

User account and employee row are created in one non-atomic mutationFn; if the employee step fails, the created account is orphaned, invisible in the list, and a retry fails on duplicate phone.

**Hujjat:** docs/06-frontend-components.md:25-28 ("Belgilansa hisob HODIMDAN OLDIN yaratiladi va employee.user_id darhol unga bog'lanadi")

**Dalil:** PersonDialog.tsx:191-200 `const created = await UsersService.createUser({ body: {... role: chosen.role as UserRole } })` … `userId = Number(created.data.id)` followed at line 226 by `await EmployeesService.createEmployee({ body })` with no rollback; the only compensating delete is line 231 `if (!account && userId !== null && canManageAccounts)`. The orphan is unreachable in the UI: employees.tsx:100-101 `const DEVICE_ROLES: string[] = [...MACHINE_BOUND_ROLES, ...OPERATOR_ROLES]` / `const isDeviceAccount = (u: UserRead) => DEVICE_ROLES.includes(u.role)` and employees.tsx:187 `if (isDeviceAccount(u) || linked.has(Number(u.id))) continue` hide every operator-role account, and `person` is still `null` on retry so line 190 `if (account && userId === null)` creates the user again → 409 duplicate phone.

**Tuzatish:** Either create the employee first and patch `user_id` afterwards, or wrap the failure path in a compensating `UsersService.deleteUser(created.id)` before rethrowing; a backend endpoint that does both in one transaction is the real fix.

### `frontend/src/components/Sidebar/AppSidebar.tsx:76` — HUJJAT DRIFTI

The docs (and the code comment they mirror) claim the badge query adds no request because the registry page dedupes it. That only holds ON `/orders`. The sidebar is mounted by `_layout` on EVERY admin page, and the query carries `refetchInterval: 30_000`, so on /clients, /employees, /work-centers, /reports, /logs, /printers, /settings, /anketa/:id, /cutmap/:id and `/` the sidebar is the sole subscriber and drives a 30-second poll of `/orders/status-counts` all on its own. The docs are wrong.

**Hujjat:** docs/06-frontend-components.md:319 — "`getOrderStatusCountsQueryOptions()` reyestr sahifasi bilan AYNAN bitta query key'da, TanStack dedupe qiladi — sidebar qo'shimcha so'rov qo'shmaydi"

**Dalil:** Doc claim (docs/06:319): "...TanStack dedupe qiladi — sidebar qo'shimcha so'rov qo'shmaydi". Code comment repeating it, AppSidebar.tsx:76-77: `  // Reyestr sahifasi ham AYNAN shu kalitni ishlatadi — TanStack dedupe qiladi,\n  // ya'ni sidebar qo'shimcha so'rov qo'shmaydi.` followed by :79-82 `  const { data: counts } = useQuery({\n    ...getOrderStatusCountsQueryOptions(),\n    enabled: canSeeOrders,\n  })`. The factory itself, queries/mes.ts:119-124: `queryKey: ["orders", "status-counts"],\n    queryFn: async () => (await OrdersService.readOrderStatusCounts()).data,\n    refetchInterval: 30_000,`. AppSidebar is rendered unconditionally by routes/_layout.tsx:41 `      <AppSidebar />`, and the second subscriber exists only at routes/_layout/orders/index.tsx:111 `  const { data: counts } = useQuery(getOrderStatusCountsQueryOptions())`. docs/05-frontend-app.md:310 already documents the poll honestly: "refetchInterval 30s — reestr tab'lari + sidebar badge", contradicting docs/06:319.

**Tuzatish:** Correct docs/06:319 to "reestr sahifasida so'rov dedupe qilinadi; qolgan sahifalarda badge'ni sidebar o'zi 30s da bir so'raydi" and rewrite the AppSidebar.tsx:76-77 comment the same way. If the app-wide poll is unwanted, spread `refetchInterval: false` (or a longer interval) into the sidebar's `useQuery` and leave the 30s poll to the registry page that actually shows live tab counts.

### `frontend/src/components/Sidebar/Main.tsx:29` — BUG

`useRouterState()` is called without a `select`, so the sidebar menu subscribes to the ENTIRE router state and re-renders on every router state transition, not only on pathname changes — `__root.tsx` in the same repo already uses the correct selector form.

**Hujjat:** docs/06-frontend-components.md:330 "`useRouterState()` dan `currentPath` olinib `isActive = currentPath === item.path` aniqlanadi"

**Dalil:** Main.tsx:29-30 `  const router = useRouterState()\n  const currentPath = router.location.pathname` versus routes/__root.tsx:17 `  const path = useRouterState({ select: (s) => s.location.pathname })`. routes/_layout.tsx:36 repeats the unselected form: `  const pathname = useRouterState().location.pathname`. TanStack Router re-runs subscribers on every state mutation (status transitions pending→idle, `isLoading`, `resolvedLocation`, matches array identity), so Main re-renders — rebuilding 9 `SidebarMenuItem`s, each wrapped in a Radix `Tooltip` — on every navigation phase change rather than once per path change.

**Tuzatish:** `const currentPath = useRouterState({ select: (s) => s.location.pathname })` in Main.tsx, and the same in routes/_layout.tsx:36 so all three call sites agree.

### `frontend/src/components/Sidebar/Main.tsx:43` — BUG

Active-item detection is a strict equality on the full pathname, so on any child route the sidebar shows NO highlighted item — the user loses their place in the navigation.

**Hujjat:** docs/06-frontend-components.md:330 "`isActive = currentPath === item.path`"

**Dalil:** Main.tsx:43 `            const isActive = currentPath === item.path`. `/orders` is a NAV_ITEMS entry (AppSidebar.tsx:41-46 `path: "/orders"`), but routes/_layout/orders/$orderId.tsx serves `/orders/123`; `"/orders/123" === "/orders"` is false, so neither the gold 3px `before:` bar (Main.tsx:55) nor the gold icon (`data-[active=true]:[&>svg]:text-sidebar-primary`, :56) is applied on the order detail page — every menu row renders inactive.

**Tuzatish:** Special-case root and prefix-match the rest: `const isActive = item.path === "/" ? currentPath === "/" : currentPath === item.path || currentPath.startsWith(`${item.path}/`)`. Plain `startsWith` alone is wrong here because `/` would then match every route.

### `frontend/src/components/Terminal/CutmapView.tsx:27` — HUJJAT DRIFTI

Hujjat `PartPreview` va `PartCard` ni CutmapView.tsx bo'limi ichida sanaydi, `PartCard` cancel tugmasi shartini ham noto'g'ri yozadi — ikkalasi ALOHIDA `Terminal/PartCard.tsx` faylida.

**Dalil:** Kod: `import { PartCard } from "@/components/Terminal/PartCard"` (CutmapView L27); PartCard.tsx da `function PartPreview({...})` (L26) va `export function PartCard({...})` (L183), tugma sharti esa faqat `{inst.label_printed_at && (<LoadingButton variant="destructive" ...>Chekni bekor qilish</LoadingButton>)}` (PartCard L283-294) — `admin` shartisiz. Hujjat: "- `PartPreview` — tanlangan detal mini-chizmasi ..." (06:359), "- `PartCard` dialogi: ... Chek chiqarilgan HAR QANDAY nusxa yonida qizil \"Chekni bekor qilish\" (sharti faqat `admin && inst.label_printed_at`)" (06:360) — ikkalasi ham `### frontend/src/components/Terminal/CutmapView.tsx` sarlavhasi ostida; 06:370 da ham "`CutmapView/PartPreview` ham ishlatadi" deyiladi.

**Tuzatish:** `PartCard.tsx` uchun alohida bo'lim ochib `PartPreview`/`PartCard` bandlarini o'sha yerga ko'chirish va cancel tugmasi shartini `inst.label_printed_at` deb tuzatish; 06:370 dagi havolani `Terminal/PartCard.tsx` ga o'zgartirish.

Hujjat `TBtn` ni CutmapView eksporti deb yozadi — u alohida `Terminal/TBtn.tsx` moduli, CutmapView uni faqat IMPORT qiladi.

**Dalil:** Kod: `import { TBtn } from "@/components/Terminal/TBtn"` (CutmapView L33); CutmapView.tsx dagi yagona eksport — `export function CutmapView({...})` (L171); `TBtn.tsx` (37 qator) `export function TBtn({...})` va `TBTN_TONES` ni o'zi saqlaydi. ### `frontend/src/components/Terminal/CutmapView.tsx:380` — BUG

`registerPrint` is called from the render body (a side effect during render) — the sibling SheetSvg explicitly documents why this is wrong and uses an effect instead.

**Hujjat:** docs/06-frontend-components.md:361 — "sahifadagi \"PDF / Chop etish\" tugmasiga registerPrint prop orqali ulanadi"

**Dalil:** CutmapView.tsx:380-382 (render body, after the early returns):
  registerPrint?.(() =>
    printAllMaps(data.order_number, materials, holes, cellInsts),
  )
SheetSvg.tsx:369-378 states the opposite rule and follows it:
  // Render paytida emas, effektda: render tanasida tashqi ref'ga yozish yon
  // ta'sir bo'lib, StrictMode va kelajakdagi concurrent render'da ikki marta
  // ishlaydi.
  useEffect(() => { registerZoom?.({ ... }) }, [registerZoom])

**Tuzatish:** Move the registerPrint call into a `useEffect` (keeping the printable data in a ref), matching SheetSvg's registerZoom pattern.

### `frontend/src/components/Terminal/CutmapView.tsx:403` — O'LIK KOD

Backend hisoblab yuboradigan `CutmapMaterial.kk` / `kd` / `thick` maydonlarini frontendda HECH KIM o'qimaydi — Listlar/Detallar statistikasi olib tashlangach o'lik yuk bo'lib qolgan.

**Hujjat:** docs/06-frontend-components.md:353

**Dalil:** Kod: `planPairs` faqat `ko`, `dr`, `kr`, `fill`, `kerf`, `trimL`, `trimW` ni o'qiydi (L403-414); `kk`/`kd`/`thick` ga murojaat yo'q. Backend hamon yozadi: `"kd": cut.stats.get("cPartCount"), "kk": cut.stats.get("cMaterialCount"),` (backend/app/services/giblab/cutmap.py:153-154) va `"thick": cut.thickness,` (:148); sxemada `thick: float | None = None`, `kd: float | None = None`, `kk: float | None = None` (backend/app/schemas/terminal.py:272-280). Tekshiruv: `grep -rn "\.kk\b|\.kd\b|\.thick\b" frontend/src --include=*.ts --include=*.tsx` (client/ dan tashqari) — 0 natija.

**Tuzatish:** Yo `kk`/`kd` ni admin statistikasiga qaytarish (hujjat aynan shuni va'da qiladi), yo sxemadan va `cutmap.py` dan olib tashlab modelni yengillashtirish.

### `frontend/src/components/Terminal/CutmapView.tsx:422` — TOZALASH

Statistika gridi ustidagi izoh yo'q bo'lib ketgan to'qqiz katakni (KK/KD/PZ) sanab beradi — kodda 7 ta band bor va KK/KD/PZ hech qachon yo'q.

**Hujjat:** docs/06-frontend-components.md:353

**Dalil:** Izoh: `// GibSaw uslubi: 3 qator × 3 ustun qat'iy blok (KK/KD/KO | DR/KR/PZ | TP/TD/TSh)` (L422-423) — lekin `planPairs` faqat `["Atxot", ko], ["Rez, m", dr], ["Rezlar", kr], ["To'ldirish", fill], ["Arra", kerf], ["Tirim uz.", trimL], ["Tirim en", trimW]` (L403-414) beradi va o'sha faylning L399-401 izohi "Listlar 4-0=4 / Detallar 20-0=20 olib tashlandi" deb yozadi; `kk`/`kd` ga umuman murojaat yo'q, PZ hech qachon bo'lmagan.

**Tuzatish:** Izohni haqiqiy 7 bandga moslash (grid `grid-rows-3` bo'lgani uchun 3×3 emas, 3 qator × 3 ustun bo'lib oxirgi ustunda bitta band qoladi).

Farqi faqat o'ram (`Button size="icon-sm"` vs `TBtn`) va "Chop etish"/"Orqaga" bandlari.

Hujjat uzoq bosishni to'g'ridan-to'g'ri bekor qilish dialogiga bog'laydi va "PartCard faqat admin rejimida ochiladi" deydi — kodda uzoq bosish/ikki marta bosish PartCard ni TERMINALDA ochadi, bekor qilish esa kartochka ichidan nusxa bo'yicha so'raladi.

Hujjat: "Xato chek: katakni long press (~600ms) — tasdiq dialogi (\"Ha, bekor qilinsin\" / \"Yo'q\"), oxirgi CHEK CHIQARILGAN nusxa bekor qilinadi ... ### `frontend/src/components/Terminal/PartCard.tsx:288` — HUJJAT DRIFTI

Docs say PartCard only opens in admin mode and that its cancel button is gated on `admin`; the code opens it on the terminal too (double-tap / long press) and gates cancel on `label_printed_at` alone. The docs are wrong.

**Hujjat:** docs/06-frontend-components.md:360

**Dalil:** docs/06-frontend-components.md:360: "Chek chiqarilgan HAR QANDAY nusxa yonida qizil \"Chekni bekor qilish\" (sharti faqat `admin && inst.label_printed_at`) ... **PartCard faqat admin rejimida ochiladi**"
PartCard.tsx:288: `{inst.label_printed_at && (` — no admin term.
CutmapView.tsx:742: `onOpenPart={admin ? undefined : setSelected}` — i.e. the gesture-opened card is the TERMINAL path; CutmapView.tsx:770-783 passes `onCancelRequest` only when `!admin` so the terminal routes cancel through a confirm dialog.

**Tuzatish:** Rewrite docs/06-frontend-components.md:360: PartCard opens in admin (single click) and terminal (double-tap/long press); cancel is shown for any printed instance, with terminal going through the confirm dialog.

### `frontend/src/components/Terminal/PrisadkaView.tsx:140` — BUG

`downloadAll` da `try { ... } finally { ... }` bor, lekin `catch` yo'q — papka ochishda (`getDirectoryHandle`) xato bo'lsa unhandled rejection bo'lib ketadi va operator hech qanday xabar ko'rmaydi.

**Hujjat:** docs/06-frontend-components.md:387 — "SCX oqimi (`showScx`): `lib/scxDir.ts` bilan — File System Access API ... papka ichida proyekt nomli subpapka (`project_name`, taqiqlangan belgilar `_` ga) ochilib fayllar shu yerga yoziladi"

**Dalil:** PrisadkaView.tsx:139-184: `try { if (dirSupported()) { ... const folder = projectFolder(data?.project_name || data?.order_number || "scx"); const sub = await dir.getDirectoryHandle(folder, { create: true }) ... } } finally { setProgress(null) }` — ichkarida faqat FAYL sikli o'ralgan (`catch { /* bittasi yiqilsa qolganlari davom etadi */ }`), papka bosqichi emas. `projectFolder` faqat `[\\/:*?"<>|]` ni almashtiradi va `.trim()` qiladi (scxDir.ts:53-55), ya'ni bo'sh yoki "."/".." nomda `getDirectoryHandle` TypeError beradi.

**Tuzatish:** `catch (e) { toast.error("SCX papkasini ochib bo'lmadi — Papka tugmasi bilan qayta tanlang") }` qo'shish va `projectFolder` natijasi bo'sh bo'lsa `"scx"` ga tushish.

**Hujjat:** docs/06-frontend-components.md:389 — "ROVER rejimida (`showScx=false`) qator (nusxa) tanlanadi"

**Dalil:** PrisadkaView.tsx:122-124 `const instRows = parts.flatMap((p) => (p.instances ?? []).map((i) => ({ p, i })))`, 254-257: `<tr key={i.instance_id} id={`pv-inst-${i.instance_id}`} onClick={() => setSelectedId(i.instance_id)}` va 279 `selectedId === i.instance_id && "bg-primary/10 ring-2 ..."`. Backend har MARSHRUT QADAMI uchun alohida `PrisadkaPartRow` beradi va har qatorga butun `insts_by_part.get(part.id, [])` ni soladi (terminal.py:676-684), ya'ni ikki qadamda AYNI `instance_id` lar takrorlanadi.

**Tuzatish:** Kalitni qadam bilan birlashtirish: `key={`${p.route_id}:${i.instance_id}`}` va `selectedId` ni ham shu juftlik qilish (yoki `route_id`+`instance_id` obyekti).

### `frontend/src/components/Terminal/PrisadkaView.tsx:350` — TAKROR · 2 ta mustaqil agent tasdiqladi

"Chekni bekor qilasizmi?" tasdiq dialogi ikki komponentda belgima-belgi takrorlangan — faqat o'rtadagi bitta jumla farq qiladi.

**Hujjat:** docs/06-frontend-components.md:390 — "ARRA xaritasidagi long press bilan bir xil imo-ishora" va docs/06-frontend-components.md:355 — "Xato chek: katakni long press (~600ms) — tasdiq dialogi (\"Ha, bekor qilinsin\" / \"Yo'q\")"

**Dalil:** PrisadkaView.tsx:350-391 and CutmapView.tsx:787-828 both contain:
  <Dialog open={cancelTarget !== null} onOpenChange={(v) => !v && setCancelTarget(null)}>
    <DialogContent className="max-w-md">
      <DialogHeader><DialogTitle>Chekni bekor qilasizmi?</DialogTitle></DialogHeader>
      ...
      <Button variant="outline" className="h-14 flex-1 text-base font-bold" ...>Yo'q</Button>
      <LoadingButton variant="destructive" className="h-14 flex-1 text-base font-bold" loading={cancelLabel.isPending} ...>Ha, bekor qilinsin</LoadingButton>
Only the body sentence differs ("Detal chop etilmagan holatga qaytadi..." vs "Katak chop etilmagan holatga qaytadi."). `ConfirmDialog` (Common/ConfirmDialog.tsx:31-67) already renders exactly this shape with `title`/`description`/`confirmText`/`cancelText`/`destructive`/`loading`.

### `frontend/src/components/Terminal/UpakovkaView.tsx:47` — TAKROR

**Hujjat:** docs/05-frontend-app.md:74 — "handleError(err: unknown) — TanStack Query onError uchun to'g'ridan-to'g'ri handler"; docs/06-frontend-components.md:3 — "xatoliklar handleError utiliti orqali"

**Dalil:** `onError: handleError` — RoomPhotos.tsx:51,62; AddOrder.tsx:68; usePrintLabel.ts:21,41; ChangePassword.tsx:53; UserInformation.tsx:62; anketa.$orderId.tsx:140; orders/$orderId.tsx:155; printers.tsx:489 (plus every `crudCallbacks` user via queries/mes.ts:149).
`toast.error(detailMessage(e))` — PersonDialog.tsx:239; TmoView.tsx:96,111,120; UpakovkaView.tsx:47,58,68,77; terminal.tsx:236,281,297; employees.tsx:402,455,499.
The two produce different text for the same server payload: extractErrorMessage returns `"E003: message"` only when `code` is present and falls back to holatXabari(status) (utils.ts:59-67), while detailMessage always prefixes a code, defaulting to `"XATO: ..."` (utils.ts:92-108).

**Tuzatish:** State the rule in one place (terminal/kiosk flows use detailMessage because the code is spoken aloud; admin pages use handleError) and add a `handleScanError` helper so callers stop inlining `(e) => toast.error(detailMessage(e))`.

### `frontend/src/components/Terminal/UpakovkaView.tsx:57` — BUG

A successful UPAKOVKA scan produces no sound, no toast and no ScanStrip banner — the exact defect TmoView documents having fixed for itself is still present on the packing screen.

TmoView compensates and says so at frontend/src/components/Terminal/TmoView.tsx:64-67: `// Muvaffaqiyat XABARI shu yerda tug'iladi: TMO skani \`viewScanRef\` orqali` / `// ketadi va terminalning umumiy oqimini (ovoz + yashil banner) chetlab` / `// o'tadi. Ilgari bu yerda faqat \`put\` bor edi — operator hech qanday` / `// javob olmasdi.` — followed by `playSuccess()`/`playWarning()`/`playError()` (TmoView.tsx:82-96). UpakovkaView's scan mutation still has exactly the old shape: frontend/src/components/Terminal/UpakovkaView.tsx:49-58, `onSuccess: put,` (line 57) with no audio import in the file at all (its only feedback is `onError: (e) => toast.error(detailMessage(e))`).

[]` (101), 137-139: `Ochiq qadoq yo'q — "Qadoq yaratish" bilan boshlang.` va 236-238: `Hammasi qadoqlangan.`

### `frontend/src/components/Terminal/audio.ts:6` — BUG

The lazily created `AudioContext` is never `resume()`d, so on the WS-driven (stanok agent) scan path — which by design involves no interaction with that browser — the context stays suspended and the mandatory audio signal is silent.

**Hujjat:** docs/06-frontend-components.md:452-454 ("skan ovoz signallari (TZ 8.4.3, tsex shovqinida majburiy)")

**Dalil:** audio.ts:3-17:
  let ctx: AudioContext | null = null

  function beep(freq: number, durationMs: number, delayMs = 0) {
    ctx ??= new AudioContext()
    ...
    osc.start(start)
    osc.stop(start + durationMs / 1000)
  }

No `ctx.resume()` and no check of `ctx.state`. Browsers create an AudioContext in the "suspended" state until the page has user activation.

The path where that activation never happens is documented by terminal.tsx:153-160 itself:
  // STANOK SKANERIDAN kelgan skanni ham ekranda ko'rsatamiz.
  // ... agent esa to'g'ridan-to'g'ri serverga yozadi — brauzer bu yo'lda umuman
  // qatnashmaydi va agent oynasiz fon jarayoni.
and terminal.tsx:182 / 196-197 call playSuccess()/playWarning()/playError() from that WS handler.

Failure scenario: a Nanxing/prisadka terminal auto-reloads after a deploy (useKiosk.ts:68 `window.location.reload()`), the station is running `chrome --kiosk` so KioskOverlays never shows its "To'liq ekranga o'tish" button, and the operator scans only at the machine. The page has zero user activation, `new AudioContext()` starts suspended, and every scan.recorded/scan.rejected beep is silent — exactly the case the WS-echo feature was built for.

**Tuzatish:** In `beep`, after `ctx ??= new AudioContext()`, add `if (ctx.state === "suspended") ctx.resume()` (and optionally create/resume the context once on the first `keydown`/`pointerdown` in `useKiosk`).

### `frontend/src/components/Terminal/useTerminalScan.ts:105` — BUG

On KROMKA/OVAL the cooldown map is only written on the success paths, so a rejected QR bypasses the 5s barrier entirely and every repeat read fires another `resolveTerminalQr` request plus another toast.

**Hujjat:** docs/06-frontend-components.md:446 ("KROMKA/OVAL lokal tekshiruvi (`kromkaScanRef`, 5 s to'siq...)") / docs/09-mes-domain.md:588 ("bir QR uchun 5 s to'siq")

**Dalil:** useTerminalScan.ts:96-133 — note the map is read at 97 but only written at 130 and 139, never on an error path:
    const last = cooldownRef.current.get(value)
    if (last && Date.now() - last < KROMKA_COOLDOWN_MS) { ... return }
    if (mapOrderId !== null && kromkaScanRef.current) {
      const err = kromkaScanRef.current(value)
      if (err === "NOTFOUND") {
        playError()
        TerminalService.resolveTerminalQr({ path: { qr_text: value } })
          .then(...)
          .catch(...)
        return                       // <- no cooldownRef.current.set
      }
      if (err) {
        playError()
        toast.error(err)
        return                       // <- no cooldownRef.current.set
      }
      cooldownRef.current.set(value, Date.now())

and the picker branch at 136-145 only records the cooldown inside `.then`, never in `.catch`.

Failure scenario: operator scans a detail belonging to another project on a KROMKA terminal. `kromkaScanRef` returns "NOTFOUND"; the handler fires GET /terminal/qr/{qr}/order and toasts. The scanner's second read (or the operator re-scanning) finds no cooldown entry and repeats the whole thing — N network round-trips and N stacked 6-second top-center toasts for one physical scan.

**Tuzatish:** Record `cooldownRef.current.set(value, Date.now())` immediately after the 5s barrier check passes (before calling `kromkaScanRef.current`), so every attempt — accepted or rejected — starts the barrier, and drop the now-redundant writes at 130/139.

### `frontend/src/components/UserSettings/UserInformation.tsx:71` — BUG

Clearing the name field silently does nothing: the cleared value becomes `undefined`, is dropped from the JSON body, yet a success toast fires and the old name reappears after invalidation.

**Hujjat:** docs/06-frontend-components.md:469

**Dalil:** UserInformation.tsx:70-73 `const updateData: UserUpdate = {}` / `if (data.name !== currentUser?.name) { updateData.name = data.name }`. lib/validation.ts:97 `.transform((v) => (v === "" ? undefined : v))` — an emptied field yields `undefined`, so `updateData.name = undefined`, which `JSON.stringify` drops from the PATCH body. The generated contract accepts an explicit clear: client/types.gen.ts:3203-3207 `export type UserUpdate = { name?: string | null; ... }`. Meanwhile UserInformation.tsx:58 still runs `toast.success("Foydalanuvchi muvaffaqiyatli yangilandi")`. docs/06:469 claims "`onSubmit` faqat o'zgargan maydonlarni `UserUpdate` payloadga qo'shadi" — the cleared field is changed but never reaches the server.

**Tuzatish:** Send `updateData.name = data.name ?? null` so an emptied name is transmitted as an explicit `null` (the type already allows it), or block submit when the transform produced `undefined` and the server value was non-empty.

### `frontend/src/hooks/useMesWs.ts:41` — BUG

Reconnect is a flat 3-second retry with no backoff, cap or jitter, so a backend restart or outage makes every mounted client hammer the WS endpoint 20×/minute indefinitely — and four separate pages each hold their own socket.

**Hujjat:** docs/05-frontend-app.md:279 — "Ulanish uzilsa 3s dan keyin avto-reconnect"

**Dalil:** useMesWs.ts:38-42 `socket.onclose = () => { setOnline(false); if (heartbeat) clearInterval(heartbeat); if (!closed) reconnect = setTimeout(connect, 3000) }` — the delay is a literal, never grown. A failed `new WebSocket(url)` also lands here via useMesWs.ts:43 `socket.onerror = () => socket?.close()`. Four independent mounts: routes/tablo.tsx:252, routes/terminal.tsx:161, routes/_layout/index.tsx:68, routes/_layout/printers.tsx:179. Compare the generated SDK, which does the right thing — frontend/src/client/core/serverSentEvents.gen.ts:232-233 `// exponential backoff: double retry each attempt, cap at 30s`.

**Tuzatish:** Track an attempt counter and use `Math.min(3000 * 2 ** attempt, 30_000)` plus a small random jitter, resetting the counter in `onopen` — the same policy the bundled SSE client already implements.
