### `frontend/src/lib/mes.ts:198` — BUG

`OPERATOR_ROLES` is an `as const` tuple, which forces every call site to cast the argument with `as never` — silently disabling exactly the rename-safety the file's own comment promises.

**Hujjat:** docs/05-frontend-app.md:91

**Dalil:** lib/mes.ts:142-143 states the rule:
// Rol nomi UserRole (backend enum'i) dan: rol qayta nomlansa solishtirish
// jimgina "false" bo'lib qolmasin — ROLE_LABELS kabi build yiqilsin

But lib/mes.ts:198 `export const OPERATOR_ROLES = [ "arrachi", ... ] as const` narrows `.includes()` to the literal union, so all three consumers erase the check:
routes/terminal.tsx:60 `if (me.role !== "terminal" && !OPERATOR_ROLES.includes(me.role as never)) {`
routes/terminal.tsx:75 `const isOperator = OPERATOR_ROLES.includes(currentUser?.role as never)`
routes/_layout.tsx:29 `if (me.role === "terminal" || OPERATOR_ROLES.includes(me.role as never))`

Same pattern for the other tuples: `TERMINAL_STATUSES` (mes.ts:24) needs `(TERMINAL_STATUSES as readonly string[])` at AppSidebar.tsx:85, orders/$orderId.tsx:206 and :268; `MACHINE_BOUND_ROLES` (mes.ts:159) is widened at employees.tsx:100 `const DEVICE_ROLES: string[] = [...MACHINE_BOUND_ROLES, ...OPERATOR_ROLES]`.

If a backend role is renamed, `me.role as never` compiles fine and the guard quietly evaluates false — an operator would be routed into the admin shell.

**Tuzatish:** Declare `export const OPERATOR_ROLES: readonly UserRole[] = [...]` (and `TERMINAL_STATUSES: readonly OrderStatus[]`, `MACHINE_BOUND_ROLES: readonly UserRole[]`), then delete the `as never` / `as readonly string[]` casts at all six call sites so a rename breaks the build as intended.

### `frontend/src/lib/queryClient.ts:23` — BUG

The single QueryClient declares no `defaultOptions`, so every query in the app inherits TanStack v5 defaults `staleTime: 0`, `refetchOnWindowFocus: true` and `retry: 3` — the heaviest endpoints refetch on every alt-tab, and every real error takes ~7s of silent retries before `isError` turns true.

**Hujjat:** docs/05-frontend-app.md:99-101 — the queryClient section documents only `queryCache`/`mutationCache`; no `defaultOptions` are mentioned or set

**Dalil:** queryClient.ts:23-26 `export const queryClient = new QueryClient({ queryCache: new QueryCache({ onError: handleApiError }), mutationCache: new MutationCache({ onError: handleApiError }), })` — no `defaultOptions`. Only 4 of the 12 factories in queries/mes.ts set `staleTime` (lines 23, 30); `getOrdersQueryOptions`, `getOrderQueryOptions`, `getOrderMatrixQueryOptions`, `getClientsQueryOptions`, `getEmployeesQueryOptions` set none. `retry: false` exists in exactly one place — hooks/useCurrentUser.ts:9 — proving the default was considered per-consumer and never globally.

**Tuzatish:** Add `defaultOptions: { queries: { staleTime: 30_000, retry: 1, refetchOnWindowFocus: false } }` to the QueryClient and drop the now-redundant per-consumer overrides. The retry default matters most: it silently multiplies the latency of every already-reported "error is swallowed" finding by ~7s.

### `frontend/src/lib/scxDir.ts:87` — BUG

`pickDir` swallows every error as "user cancelled", so a real failure (SecurityError outside a user gesture, NotAllowedError) makes the "Hammasini yuklab olish" button do nothing at all — no toast, no error.

**Hujjat:** docs/05-frontend-app.md:110

**Dalil:** lib/scxDir.ts:76-90:
export async function pickDir(): Promise<FileSystemDirectoryHandle | null> {
  try {
    ... showDirectoryPicker({ mode: "readwrite" })
    await kvSet(KEY, h)
    return h
  } catch {
    return null // foydalanuvchi bekor qildi
  }
}

Consumers treat null as a silent no-op:
PrisadkaView.tsx:144-146 `let dir = await getSavedDir(true); if (!dir) dir = await pickDir(); if (!dir) return // bekor qilindi`
PrisadkaView.tsx:187-189 `const dir = await pickDir(); if (dir) toast.success(...)` — the failure branch shows nothing.
The comment "foydalanuvchi bekor qildi" is also inaccurate: only `AbortError` means cancellation.

**Tuzatish:** Catch the error object and re-throw / return a discriminated result for anything whose `name !== "AbortError"`, then surface it with `toast.error` in PrisadkaView so the operator knows why nothing happened.

### `frontend/src/lib/scxDir.ts:98` — BUG

SCX downloads use raw `fetch`, bypassing the axios silent-refresh interceptor, so an expired access token turns into a hard "HTTP 401" failure on a kiosk terminal that never reloads.

**Hujjat:** docs/05-frontend-app.md:60

**Dalil:** lib/scxDir.ts:98-101:
  const resp = await fetch(scxFileUrl(fileId), {
    credentials: "include",
  })
  if (!resp.ok) throw new Error(`Fayl ${fileId}: HTTP ${resp.status}`)

The 401 -> refresh -> retry logic is installed only on the generated client's own axios instance — main.tsx:25 `client.instance.interceptors.response.use(...)`, documented at docs/05:60 ("Silent refresh interceptor").
PrisadkaView.tsx:155-166 swallows each failure and only reports a count: `toast.error(\`${ok}/${allFiles.length} saqlandi — qolganlarini qayta urining.\`)`, so the operator sees a partial save with no hint that the session expired.

**Tuzatish:** Either route the download through the generated client (a blob-response service method) or, on a 401 from this fetch, call `LoginService.refreshAccessToken()` once and retry — matching the interceptor's behaviour.

### `frontend/src/lib/scxDir.ts:103` — O'LIK KOD

`saveScxToDir`'s optional `filename` parameter is never omitted by its single caller, so the content-disposition parsing and the `scx_{id}.scx` fallback are unreachable — and that fallback would break the "file name == QR text" rule if it ever ran.

**Hujjat:** docs/11-detal-id-nomlash.md:61

**Dalil:** lib/scxDir.ts:93-106:
export async function saveScxToDir(
  dir: FileSystemDirectoryHandle,
  fileId: number,
  filename?: string,
): Promise<string> {
  ...
  let name = filename
  if (!name) {
    const cd = resp.headers.get("content-disposition") ?? ""
    name = /filename="?([^";]+)"?/.exec(cd)?.[1] ?? `scx_${fileId}.scx`
  }

The only caller always supplies it — PrisadkaView.tsx:155 `await saveScxToDir(sub, f.id, f.filename)` — and `PrisadkaFile.filename` is a required `string` (client/types.gen.ts:2086-2095).
docs/11:61 requires the SCX file name to be the QR text verbatim ("SCX fayl nomi va ichidagi Panel ID | services/giblab/scx.py"), which `scx_{id}.scx` would violate for Nanxing scan matching.

**Tuzatish:** Make `filename: string` required and delete lines 103-106; the server-side name is the only one that satisfies the doc-11 naming contract.

### `frontend/src/lib/validation.ts:6` — BUG · 2 ta mustaqil agent tasdiqladi

NAME_MAX_LENGTH is 30 while the backend allows 100, even though the file's own comment claims the limits mirror the backend — full Uzbek names with a patronymic are rejected client-side.

**Hujjat:** docs/05-frontend-app.md:136

**Dalil:** frontend/src/lib/validation.ts:6 `const NAME_MAX_LENGTH = 30` feeding validation.ts:91-93 `.max(NAME_MAX_LENGTH, { message: \`Ism ko'pi bilan ${NAME_MAX_LENGTH} ta belgi bo'lishi mumkin\` })`. Backend counterpart backend/app/schemas/users.py:50 `_NAME_FIELD = Field(min_length=2, max_length=100, ...)`. Inside the frontend the two paths disagree with each other too: frontend/src/components/UserSettings/UserInformation.tsx:30 `name: optionalNameSchema,` (30-char cap on "my profile") vs frontend/src/components/People/PersonDialog.tsx:285 `maxLength={100}` (100-char cap on the admin dialog for the SAME `user.name` field). So a person with a 39-char full name can be created by an admin but cannot edit their own profile without shortening it.

**Tuzatish:** Pick one number and propagate it. Once user.name is widened to 100 (see the model finding), set `NAME_MAX_LENGTH = 100` in validation.ts:6 and update docs/05-frontend-app.md:136 ("max 30 belgi" → "max 100 belgi").

### `frontend/src/lib/validation.ts:24` — BUG

The "special character" rule differs between the two sides: `ʻ` and `ʼ` (the Uzbek oʻ/gʻ apostrophes) satisfy zod but not the server, so a password the form accepts is rejected with 422.

**Hujjat:** docs/05-frontend-app.md:133 — "| `passwordSchema` | Majburiy parol ... maxsus belgi (alohida regex'lar, o'zbekcha xabarlar; server `validate_password_strength` bilan mos) |"

**Dalil:** Frontend (frontend/src/lib/validation.ts:24-26): `.regex(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/, { message: "Parolda kamida bitta maxsus belgi bo'lishi kerak" })` — anything outside ASCII alphanumerics counts as special. Backend (backend/app/schemas/users.py:32-33): `if not any(not c.isalnum() for c in v): raise ValueError("Parolda kamida bitta belgi bo'lsin (masalan ! yoki *)")` — Python's `isalnum()` is Unicode-aware. Verified: `'ʻ'.isalnum()` → True, `'ʼ'.isalnum()` → True, `'ā'.isalnum()` → True, `'ж'.isalnum()` → True. So `Parolim1ʻ` passes `passwordSchema` and is rejected by the server; the other four password rules (min 8, lower, upper, digit) do match.

**Tuzatish:** Tighten the server side to the same ASCII definition — `if not any(not c.isascii() or not c.isalnum() for c in v)` is wrong for the reverse reason, so prefer an explicit class: `if not re.search(r"[^A-Za-z0-9]", v)` in `validate_password_strength` — which makes both sides use one rule.

### `frontend/src/routes/_layout/orders/index.tsx:149` — BUG

The registry sort comparator returns NaN whenever two rows share a missing value, because the null sentinels are ±Infinity and `Infinity - Infinity` is NaN — the sort of the 100-row registry becomes undefined for those pairs.

**Hujjat:** docs/05-frontend-app.md:207 — "saralash client-side (`created_at`, `deadline`, `priority`, `amount`, qoldiq to'lov...)"

**Dalil:** orders/index.tsx:136-146 `return o.deadline ? new Date(o.deadline).getTime() : Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY` and `return o.amount != null ? Number(o.amount) : Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY`; orders/index.tsx:149-151 `return [...list].sort((a, b) => sort.desc ? val(b) - val(a) : val(a) - val(b),)`. Verified in node: two rows with `deadline: null` give `vd(d[0])-vd(d[1]) === NaN`; two rows with `amount: null` give `NaN` likewise. A comparator returning NaN is not a valid ordering, so V8 may place those rows arbitrarily and re-sorting the same data can reorder them.

**Tuzatish:** Compare with a null-aware comparator instead of arithmetic on sentinels, e.g. `const cmp = (x, y) => (x === y ? 0 : x < y ? -1 : 1)` over nullable values with an explicit "nulls last" branch, so equal-and-missing pairs return 0 rather than NaN.

### `frontend/src/routes/_layout/printers.tsx:149` — BUG

The agent-log dialog swallows API errors: a failed `readBridgeLogs` request renders the 'no logs yet' hint instead of an error, so the admin blames the agent for a server fault.

**Hujjat:** docs/06-frontend-components.md:96

**Dalil:** printers.tsx:118 `const { data, isFetching } = useQuery({` — `isError` is never destructured. printers.tsx:149-154 `{!data?.length ? (<p ...>{isFetching ? "Yuklanmoqda..." : "Hali log yo'q — agent ulangach 30 soniya ichida keladi."}</p>)`. On a 500/network failure `data` is `undefined` and `isFetching` is `false`, so the user is told the agent has not sent logs. docs/06-frontend-components.md:96 states the mandated pattern: "So'rov xatosi | `Common/QueryError` | `useQuery` dan `isError, refetch` olinadi; naqsh: `isLoading ? Skeleton : isError ? QueryError : Table`".

**Tuzatish:** Destructure `isError, refetch` and render `<QueryError onRetry={() => refetch()} />` before falling through to the empty-state text.

### `frontend/src/routes/_layout/printers.tsx:356` — TAKROR

The work-center dropdown hand-writes the five-part Radix Select block that `Common/SelectField` exists to encapsulate, including its own '__empty__'-style sentinel.

**Hujjat:** docs/06-frontend-components.md:102 and docs/06-frontend-components.md:193

**Dalil:** printers.tsx:356-374 writes the whole stack inline: `<Select value={agent.machine_id ? String(agent.machine_id) : "none"} onValueChange={...}><SelectTrigger className="h-9 w-44"><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger><SelectContent><SelectItem value="none">— bog'lanmagan</SelectItem>{machines?.map((m) => (<SelectItem key={m.id} value={String(m.id)}>{m.name}</SelectItem>))}</SelectContent></Select>` — the `"none"` sentinel duplicates SelectField.tsx:29 `const EMPTY = "__empty__"` / line 65 `{emptyLabel && <SelectItem value={EMPTY}>{emptyLabel}</SelectItem>}`, and SelectField already takes `className` (line 61) and `options[{value,label,hint}]`. docs/06:102 lists "Tanlov ro'yxati | `Common/SelectField`" among the things you must not re-write by hand ("Yangi sahifa yoki dialog yozganda quyidagilarni **qo'lda takrorlamang**", line 88), and docs/06:193 enumerates the callers as "`Orders/CustomRoutes.tsx`, `Orders/MachineAssign.tsx`, `People/PersonDialog.tsx`, `routes/_layout/work-centers.tsx`" — printers.tsx is absent. `work-centers.tsx:263` uses `SelectField` correctly for the same kind of choice.

**Tuzatish:** Replace the inline block with `<SelectField value={agent.machine_id ? String(agent.machine_id) : ""} emptyLabel="— bog'lanmagan" className="h-9 w-44" options={machines?.map(...) ?? []} onChange={...} />`, then add printers.tsx to the caller list in docs/06:193.

### `frontend/src/routes/_layout/printers.tsx:427` — HUJJAT DRIFTI

Both docs say the printers table has exactly two row actions; the code has three, and the whole remote agent-log dialog (a real backend endpoint) is undocumented.

**Hujjat:** docs/05-frontend-app.md:222 and docs/07-infra-devops.md:301

**Dalil:** printers.tsx:424-437 renders THREE actions in the row: `<AgentLogs agent={agent} />`, `<RowAction icon={KeyRound} title="Yangi ulanish kodi (eski token bekor bo'ladi)" ...>` and `<RowAction icon={Trash2} title="Agentni bekor qilish" danger ...>`. docs/05-frontend-app.md:222 states "...va har qatorda ikki amal: `KeyRound` — yangi kod (`regenerateBridgeCode`, eski token bekor bo'ladi) va `Trash2` — agentni bekor qilish (`deleteBridgeAgent`)". docs/07-infra-devops.md:301 states "Amallar: **yangi kod** (`POST /{id}/code` ...) va **bekor qilish** (`DELETE /{id}` ...)". The missing third action calls a real endpoint — printers.tsx:122 `await BridgeAgentsService.readBridgeLogs({ path: { agent_id: agent.id } })` → backend/app/api/routes/bridge_agents.py:310 `@router.get("/{agent_id}/logs", ... operation_id="read_bridge_logs")`. docs/07:356 still tells the admin to read `%APPDATA%\MebelixAgent\agent.log` on the shop-floor PC, which the dialog exists specifically to avoid.

**Tuzatish:** Docs are wrong. Add the `Loglar` (FileText) row action and the log dialog to docs/05:222 and docs/07:292/301, and note in docs/07:356 that the last 200 lines are also readable remotely from the admin panel.

Sxemada oraliq qaytariladi (backend/app/schemas/reports.py:22-24 `class ProductivityReport(BaseModel): date_from: date; date_to: date`), lekin reports.tsx `data?.employees` / `data?.rows` dan boshqa hech narsani o'qimaydi.

### `frontend/src/routes/_layout/work-centers.tsx:65` — BUG

A failed `/posts` request is silently swallowed — only the machines query drives the error branch, so the page renders with a blank Post column and a broken sort order and no error is shown.

**Hujjat:** docs/06-frontend-components.md:96

**Dalil:** work-centers.tsx:59-65 destructures `isError`/`refetch` only from the machines query: `const { data: machines, isLoading, isError, refetch } = useQuery(getMachinesQueryOptions())` followed by `const { data: posts } = useQuery(getPostsQueryOptions())`. work-centers.tsx:96-98 `isError ? (<QueryError onRetry={() => refetch()} />)` therefore never fires for a posts failure, while work-centers.tsx:71 `const postById = new Map((posts ?? []).map((p) => [p.id, p]))` and work-centers.tsx:74-79 `(postById.get(a.post_id)?.order_index ?? 0) - ...` degrade to an unsorted list with `{post && <PostBadge code={post.code} />}` (line 117) rendering nothing. The Add button also goes permanently dead: line 88 `<Button onClick={() => setAdding(true)} disabled={!posts}>`. docs/06:96 mandates "`useQuery` dan `isError, refetch` olinadi; naqsh: `isLoading ? Skeleton : isError ? QueryError : Table`".

**Tuzatish:** Combine both queries into the guard, e.g. `const isError = machinesQ.isError || postsQ.isError` and retry both in `onRetry`, so a posts outage shows `QueryError` instead of a silently degraded table.

### `frontend/src/routes/_layout/work-centers.tsx:150` — BUG · 3 ta mustaqil agent tasdiqladi

The empty-state row spans one column more than the table has, so the 'Ish markazlari yo'q' cell overflows the table grid.

**Hujjat:** docs/06-frontend-components.md:95

**Dalil:** work-centers.tsx:150 `<EmptyRow colSpan={admin ? 5 : 4}>` but the header at work-centers.tsx:102-107 declares at most four columns: `<TableHead>Ish markazi</TableHead><TableHead>Post</TableHead><TableHead>Holat</TableHead>{admin && <TableHead className="w-20" />}` — i.e. 4 for admin, 3 otherwise. `Common/EmptyRow.tsx:16-18` passes the value straight through: `<TableCell colSpan={colSpan} className="h-24 text-center text-muted-foreground">`. By contrast printers.tsx:236 `<EmptyRow colSpan={7}>` matches its 7 `<TableHead>` elements exactly. docs/06:95 documents the prop as "`colSpan` beriladi" with no allowance for over-spanning.

**Tuzatish:** Change to `colSpan={admin ? 4 : 3}`.

### `frontend/src/routes/_layout/work-centers.tsx:208` — BUG

The auto-suggested work-center name counts existing machines on the post, so after any deletion or manual rename it proposes a name that already exists and the server rejects the pre-filled default.

**Hujjat:** docs/05-frontend-app.md:171

**Dalil:** work-centers.tsx:207-209 `const used = machines.filter((m) => m.post_id === post.id).length` / `return { ...f, post_id: postId, name: `${post.name}-${used + 1}` }`. The name is globally unique in the DB: backend/app/models/machine.py `Index("ix_machine_name", "name", unique=True, postgresql_where=text("NOT is_deleted"))`, enforced at backend/app/api/routes/machines.py:101 `await _check_name(db, body.name)`. Concretely: post ARRA holds "Arra-1" and "Arra-2"; "Arra-1" is deleted; `used` is now 1, so the dialog pre-fills "Arra-2", which already exists — Saqlash is enabled (`valid` at line 240 only checks length and post) and the request comes back as an error toast. docs/05-frontend-app.md:171 describes this dialog as "**Admin uchun to'liq CRUD**: `MachineDialog` (qo'shish va tahrirlash bitta dialog — nom, post, holat)" with no mention of the suggestion being unreliable.

**Tuzatish:** Pick the first free suffix instead of the count, e.g. increment `n` while `machines.some(m => m.name === `${post.name}-${n}`)`.

""}</span>` va `{data?.name ?? ""}`; 843: `{data?.parts.map((p) => (` — xatoda ham, yuklanishda ham devor ekranida sarlavhasiz, qatorsiz bo'sh jadval turadi (matritsada esa `isLoading ? <p>Yuklanmoqda...</p>` bor). Doc 244: "Qator bosilsa buyurtma drill-down'i — detal × post jadvali (`readTabloOrder`, 30s poll)".

**Tuzatish:** Drill'da ham matritsadagi kabi `isLoading` holatini va `isError` da aniq xato matnini chizish (yoki xatoda avtomatik `onBack()` bilan matritsaga qaytish).

### `frontend/src/routes/terminal.tsx:320` — BUG

The offline (E007) scan path is the only scan outcome that does not stamp `lastScanAtRef`, so the kiosk's "quiet time" gate can decide it is safe to auto-reload while the operator is actively scanning through a WS outage.

**Hujjat:** docs/05-frontend-app.md:230 ("Reload faqat xavfsiz paytda — xarita yopiq va oxirgi skandan 2 daqiqa o'tgan bo'lsa")

**Dalil:** terminal.tsx:78-80 states the invariant:
  // Oxirgi skan URINISHI vaqti (muvaffaqiyat ham, xato ham) — avto-reload
  // "tinchlik" o'lchovi; lastScan holatidan farqli, 15s da tozalanmaydi
  const lastScanAtRef = useRef(0)

Every other outcome honours it — terminal.tsx:183, 198, 251, 273:
  lastScanAtRef.current = Date.now()

But the offline branch (terminal.tsx:320-328) does not:
  onOffline: () => {
    playError()
    setLastScan({
      kind: "error",
      code: "E007",
      message: "Aloqa yo'q, qaytadan urining",
      at: Date.now(),
    })
  },

and it is consumed by terminal.tsx:124-126:
  const fullscreen = useKioskShell(
    () => mapOrderId === null && Date.now() - lastScanAtRef.current > QUIET_MS,
  )

Failure scenario: the shop Wi-Fi drops, `online` goes false, and the operator keeps scanning for three minutes getting E007 each time. Because none of those attempts touch `lastScanAtRef`, `canReload()` returns true and `useKiosk`'s 30s retry loop reloads the page mid-work, wiping the on-screen state the operator was reading.

**Tuzatish:** Add `lastScanAtRef.current = Date.now()` to the `onOffline` callback (and consider doing the same in `onBadge`, since a badge scan is also a scan attempt).

### `frontend/src/routes/terminal.tsx:508` — HUJJAT DRIFTI

docs/05 §Smena says the "Yakunlash" button swaps to "Chiqish" when the crew is empty; the code swaps on the operator-role check, and a device account with an empty crew renders neither button.

**Hujjat:** docs/05-frontend-app.md:96

**Dalil:** docs/05-frontend-app.md:96:
  "`Yakunlash` tugmasi brigada bo'sh bo'lganda `Chiqish` ga almashadi: beyjik bilan kirgan operator hisobi qulf ostida qolib ketmasin."

terminal.tsx:508-524:
  {isOperator ? (
    <TBtn label="Chiqish" tone="danger" onClick={() => setLogoutOpen(true)}>
      <LogOut />
    </TBtn>
  ) : crew.length ? (
    <TBtn label="Yakunlash" tone="danger" onClick={() => setEndFor(crew[0] ?? null)}>
      <LogOut />
    </TBtn>
  ) : null}

with `isOperator` from terminal.tsx:75:
  const isOperator = OPERATOR_ROLES.includes(currentUser?.role as never)

So the discriminator is the ROLE, not crew emptiness; for role `terminal` with `crew.length === 0` the branch yields `null` — no button at all, not "Chiqish". The same doc file describes the real rule correctly at docs/05:237 ("operator rollar uchun 'Chiqish' (logout), `terminal` qurilma akkauntida sessiya bo'lsa 'Yakunlash'"), so docs/05:96 is the stale copy.

**Tuzatish:** Rewrite docs/05:96 to match docs/05:237: the button is `Chiqish` for operator-role accounts and `Yakunlash` for the TERMINAL device account while a crew exists; with an empty crew on a device account no button is drawn (the ShiftGate is up instead).

### `frontend/src/routes/terminal.tsx:687` — BUG

The terminal logout ConfirmDialog is the only mutation-backed caller that omits `loading`, so its confirm button never disables and a double-tap on the touch terminal fires the logout request twice.

**Hujjat:** docs/06-frontend-components.md:126-127 — "Props: `open`, `onOpenChange`, `title`, `description?`, `confirmText?` ..., `loading?`, `onConfirm`. Tasdiqlash tugmasi `LoadingButton`"

**Dalil:** terminal.tsx:687-695: `<ConfirmDialog open={logoutOpen} onOpenChange={setLogoutOpen} title="Tizimdan chiqish" ... confirmText="Chiqish" destructive onConfirm={() => logout()} />` — no `loading` prop. ConfirmDialog.tsx:39 defaults it: `loading = false`, and ui/loading-button.tsx only disables via `disabled={loading || disabled}`. Every other mutation caller passes it: terminal.tsx:703 `loading={endSession.isPending}`, terminal.tsx:726 `loading={undoMutation.isPending}`, printers.tsx:389/415/445/456, employees.tsx:422/478/565, work-centers.tsx:344, PersonDialog.tsx:420, RoomPhotos.tsx:236, orders/$orderId.tsx:526. Root cause: hooks/useAuth.ts:30-35 returns only `const logout = () => logoutMutation.mutate()` and `return { loginMutation, logout }` — `logoutMutation.isPending` is never exposed.

**Tuzatish:** Return `logoutMutation` (or `isLoggingOut: logoutMutation.isPending`) from `useAuth` and pass `loading={...}` at terminal.tsx:687.

### `frontend/src/utils.ts:39` — TAKROR

`extractErrorMessage` and `detailParts` implement the same six-branch error ladder in the same file, duplicating two user-facing strings verbatim and giving the same axios error two different Uzbek texts.

**Hujjat:** docs/05-frontend-app.md:72, docs/05-frontend-app.md:73

**Dalil:** utils.ts:39-68 (extractErrorMessage) and utils.ts:72-104 (detailParts) both branch on ERR_NETWORK -> ECONNABORTED -> array detail -> object detail -> string detail -> holatXabari(status), in that order.

Duplicated verbatim:
:42 `return "Tarmoq xatosi — internet aloqasini tekshiring"` vs :75 `message: "Tarmoq xatosi — internet aloqasini tekshiring",`
:55 `return "Kiritilgan ma'lumot noto'g'ri — tekshirib qayta yuboring"` vs :90 `message: "Kiritilgan ma'lumot noto'g'ri — tekshirib qayta yuboring",`

Diverged for the same condition (ECONNABORTED):
:45 `return "So'rov vaqti tugadi — qayta urinib ko'ring"`
:82 `return { code: "KUTISH", message: "Server javob bermadi — qayta urining" }`
So a timeout reads one way in a dialog toast (`handleError`) and another on the terminal scan strip (`detailMessage`).

**Tuzatish:** Implement `extractErrorMessage` on top of `detailParts` (e.g. return `message || code`, prefixing the code only for MES `{code,message}` envelopes) so there is one ladder and one string per condition.

### `frontend/src/utils.ts:66` — HUJJAT DRIFTI · 2 ta mustaqil agent tasdiqladi

docs/05 says extractErrorMessage falls back to `err.message` and then to "Xatolik yuz berdi. Qayta urinib ko'ring."; the code instead uses an undocumented HTTP-status -> Uzbek message table (`holatXabari`) and a different final string.

**Hujjat:** docs/05-frontend-app.md:72

**Dalil:** docs/05:72: "... tanada `detail` bo'lmasa (5xx, HTML sahifa, proxy xatosi) `err.message`, u ham bo'lmasa \"Xatolik yuz berdi. Qayta urinib ko'ring.\" fallback"

utils.ts:65-67:
  // Tanada `detail` yo'q (5xx, HTML sahifa, proxy xatosi)
  if (err instanceof AxiosError) return holatXabari(err.response?.status)
  return "Xatolik yuz berdi — qayta urinib ko'ring"

`holatXabari` (utils.ts:24-37) maps 401/403 -> "Sessiya tugadi — qaytadan kiring", 404, 413, 429, 502/503/504 -> "Server yangilanmoqda ...", >=500 -> "Serverda xatolik ...". `err.message` is never returned, and no doc mentions `holatXabari` at all (`grep -rn holatXabari docs/` is empty). The whole point of the helper — never showing the operator "Request failed with status code 500" — is undocumented.

**Tuzatish:** Rewrite the docs/05:72 bullet: describe `holatXabari` (internal, status -> Uzbek message table, used by both extractErrorMessage and detailParts) and correct the final fallback string to "Xatolik yuz berdi — qayta urinib ko'ring".

### `frontend/tsconfig.json:25` — O'LIK KOD

`references` + `tsconfig.node.json` hech qachon qurilmaydi: `npm run build` oddiy `tsc` chaqiradi (`tsc -b` emas), shuning uchun `vite.config.ts` umuman type-check qilinmaydi

**Hujjat:** docs/05-frontend-app.md:36 — "`tsconfig.node.json`: composite project reference — faqat `vite.config.ts` uchun (Node muhitida ishlaydigan config fayl)"

**Dalil:** tsconfig.json:24-29:
```
  "include": ["src"],
  "references": [
    {
      "path": "./tsconfig.node.json"
    }
  ]
```
package.json:9: `"build": "tsc && vite build",` — `-b`/`--build` yo'q; repoda `tsc -b` hech qayerda chaqirilmaydi (`.github/workflows/ci.yml:49` ham `npm run build`).
Empirik tasdiq: `./node_modules/.bin/tsc --listFiles | grep -i vite.config` → bo'sh (dasturda 134 ta `frontend/src` fayli bor, `vite.config.ts` yo'q).
Qo'shimcha: tsconfig.node.json da `composite: true` bor, lekin `noEmit` yo'q va `outDir` ham yo'q — kimdir `tsc -b` chaqirsa `vite.config.js`/`.d.ts`/`*.tsbuildinfo` `frontend/` ga chiqadi, ular esa `frontend/.gitignore` da yo'q.

**Tuzatish:** Yoki `build` ni `tsc -b && vite build` ga o'tkazib `tsconfig.node.json` ga `"strict": true` + `"noEmit": true` qo'shish (va `*.tsbuildinfo` ni gitignore qilish), yoki `references` + `tsconfig.node.json` ni butunlay o'chirib, `vite.config.ts` ni asosiy `include` ga qo'shish.


## infra

### `.dockerignore:4` — BUG

`**/.env` naqshi faqat aynan `.env` nomini tutadi — `.env.server` kabi variantlar build kontekstiga uzatiladi (frontend'ning o'z `.dockerignore`'i esa `.env.*` ni ham yopadi).

**Hujjat:** docs/07-infra-devops.md:487 va :489

**Dalil:** .dockerignore:4 `**/.env` (butun faylda `.env.*` naqshi yo'q) | frontend/.dockerignore: `.env` + `.env.*` — `# dev'ning lokal .env fayllari image'ga va Vite bundle env'iga sizmasin` | repo ildizida hozir `.env.server` fayli turibdi (git status: `?? .env.server`) va u `.gitignore` dagi `.env` / `.env.local` naqshlariga ham tushmaydi | docs/07:487 `Shu bilan secretlar ham image'ga tushmaydi.` va :489 `Frontend'ning o'z konteksti ... va o'z frontend/.dockerignore i bor: node_modules, dist, .env, .env.* (dev'ning lokal .env fayllari image va Vite bundle env'iga sizmasin).`

**Tuzatish:** `.dockerignore` ga `**/.env.*` qatorini qo'shib `!**/.env.example` istisnosini berish (yoki `.env.example` ni baribir kerak emas — backend Dockerfile faqat `backend`/`scripts` ni COPY qiladi), va `.gitignore` ga `.env.*` + `!.env.example` qo'shish.

### `.gitignore:13` — BUG

Only `.env` and `.env.local` are ignored, not `.env.*` — the repo currently has an untracked, un-ignored `.env.server` sitting in the root, one `git add .` away from committing server secrets. The docs claim all ".env fayllar" are ignored.

**Hujjat:** docs/07-infra-devops.md:497 — "**`.env` fayllar** (secretlar repoga tushmaydi)"

**Dalil:** .gitignore:12-14 —
  `# Environment & Virtual Envs`
  `.env`
  `.env.local`
`git check-ignore -v .env.server` -> no output, exit=1 (NOT ignored)
`git status --porcelain` -> `?? .env.server`
By contrast frontend/.dockerignore and frontend's own tooling use the broader form; and .dockerignore:3 uses `**/.env` only, which also misses `.env.server` for the backend build context.

**Tuzatish:** Replace the two lines with `.env` + `.env.*` + `!.env.example` (keeping the example tracked). Mirror the same pattern in `.dockerignore` so `.env.server` never reaches the backend build context either.

### `Makefile:46` — BUG

`make frontend-lint` ("Run frontend linting checks") is not a check — `npm run lint` is `biome check --write --unsafe`, which applies unsafe autofixes to source and cannot fail on anything biome can rewrite.

**Hujjat:** docs/07-infra-devops.md:68 — "| `frontend-lint` / `frontend-build` | `npm run lint` (biome) / `npm run build` |"

**Dalil:** Makefile:44-46 —
  `frontend-lint: ## Run frontend linting checks`
  `\tcd frontend && npm run lint`
frontend/package.json:9 — `"lint": "biome check --write --unsafe --no-errors-on-unmatched --files-ignore-unknown=true ./"`
CI uses the read-only form instead — ci.yml:48 `run: npx biome ci .` — so the Makefile target and CI disagree about what "lint" means.

**Tuzatish:** Point `frontend-lint` at the same command CI uses (`cd frontend && npx biome ci .`), or add a separate `frontend-format` target and reserve `frontend-lint` for the check-only invocation.

### `Makefile:54` — BUG

`make lint` — advertised as "Run linting checks" — silently rewrites files in the developer's working tree and returns 0 for any fixable violation, because `fix = true` is set in backend/pyproject.toml.

**Hujjat:** docs/07-infra-devops.md:67 — "| `lint` / `format` | `ruff check app` + `ruff format --check app` + `mypy app` (`backend/` dan, CI bilan aynan bir xil)"

**Dalil:** Makefile:52-55 —
  `lint: ## Run linting checks (ruff check + format check + mypy, CI bilan bir xil)`
  `\tcd backend && uv run ruff check app && uv run ruff format --check app`
backend/pyproject.toml:58 — `fix = true`
Verified: `cd backend && uv run ruff check app` on a file with an unsorted-import violation printed "Found 1 error (1 fixed, 0 remaining).", exited 0, and the file on disk was modified.

**Tuzatish:** `cd backend && uv run ruff check --no-fix app && uv run ruff format --check app` so the target is a read-only check that matches its own help text.

### `Makefile:59` — BUG

`make format` runs ruff from the repo root, so it reformats files no linter in the project owns — `docs/*.md` python blocks and `bridge-agent/agent.py` (at ruff's default line-length 88, since no ruff config exists outside backend/). The docs document the damage and ask the developer to hand-revert it instead of fixing the target.

**Hujjat:** docs/07-infra-devops.md:408 — "`make format` esa repo **ildizidan** `ruff format .` + `ruff check . --fix` ishlatadi, ya'ni `docs/*.md` dagi qo'lda tekislangan python bloklarini va `bridge-agent/agent.py` ni ... qayta yozadi ... `make format` dan keyin `git diff` ni ko'rib, `backend/` dan tashqaridagi o'zgarishlarni tashlab yuborish kerak"

**Dalil:** Makefile:57-60 —
  `format: ## Run code formatting (ruff)`
  `\tuv --project backend run ruff format .`
  `\tuv --project backend run ruff check . --fix`
Verified with the non-mutating `--check` variant from the repo root:
  `ruff format --check docs/` -> "unformatted: File would be reformatted --> docs/02-backend-core.md:226:28" (collapses the hand-aligned `functions = all_tasks                      # backend/app/tasks dan`)
  `ruff format --check bridge-agent/` -> "unformatted: File would be reformatted --> bridge-agent/agent.py:373:1" plus line-length rewrites at 381, 528, 543
  `ruff check . --no-fix` -> 5 errors, all in bridge-agent/agent.py (EXE001, S110 x2, B023, BLE001) under rules that neither CI nor pre-commit selects.

**Tuzatish:** Scope the target the same way `lint`, CI and the pre-commit hooks are scoped: `cd backend && uv run ruff format app && uv run ruff check app --fix`. Then delete the "inspect git diff afterwards" workaround from docs/07-infra-devops.md:408.

### `caddy/Caddyfile:21` — O'LIK KOD

`header_up X-Real-IP {remote_host}` ikkala reverse_proxy blokida ham o'lik: na backend, na frontend nginx bu headerni o'qiydi.

**Hujjat:** docs/07-infra-devops.md:151 va :154

**Dalil:** caddy/Caddyfile:21 `header_up X-Real-IP {remote_host}` (api bloki) va :52 aynan shu qator (frontend bloki) | `grep -rni "real.ip|real_ip" backend/app frontend/src bridge-agent` — 0 natija | frontend/nginx/index.conf da `real_ip_header`/`set_real_ip_from` yo'q | backend/app/core/setup.py:172-175 faqat `ProxyHeadersMiddleware(trusted_hosts=settings.FORWARDED_ALLOW_IPS)` — u X-Forwarded-For'ni o'qiydi | docs/07:151 `faqat X-Real-IP header_up — Host passthrough va X-Forwarded-For/X-Forwarded-Proto ni Caddy reverse_proxy o'zi default qo'yadi`

**Tuzatish:** Ikkala `header_up X-Real-IP {remote_host}` qatorini o'chirish (shunda `reverse_proxy api:8000` va `reverse_proxy frontend:80` bir qatorlik bo'ladi) va docs/07:151,154 jadval qatorlaridan X-Real-IP eslatmasini olib tashlash.

### `docker-compose.prod.yml:37` — HUJJAT DRIFTI · 2 ta mustaqil agent tasdiqladi

Prod Redis'ning AOF + `noeviction` konfiguratsiyasi (chek navbatining doimiyligi uchun kritik) hech bir hujjatda tasvirlanmagan.

**Hujjat:** docs/07-infra-devops.md:91, docs/01-architecture.md:86

**Dalil:** docker-compose.prod.yml:37-41 `command: > redis-server --appendonly yes --appendfsync everysec --maxmemory-policy noeviction` | docs/07:91 prod redis haqidagi YAGONA qator: `**db** / **redis** credentiallari defaultsiz — .env da majburiy (${POSTGRES_USER} va h.k.).` | docs/01:86 `Cache / queue | Redis 7 (ARQ queue + rate limit + WS pub/sub — uchta lifespan ulanishi...)` | `grep -rn "appendonly|noeviction|appendfsync|AOF|RDB" docs/ README.md` — 0 natija

**Tuzatish:** docs/07 "Production flow" bo'limiga `redis` uchun alohida punkt qo'shish: AOF (`appendfsync everysec`) va `noeviction` nega tanlangani (compose:31-36 dagi izoh matni), hamda lokal composeda bu sozlamalar yo'qligi.

### `scripts/deploy.sh:19` — BUG

`CADDY_HTTP_BIND` is read from `.env` without stripping surrounding quotes, while `SITE_ADDRESS` two lines below is explicitly de-quoted — a quoted bind value produces a malformed curl URL, so smoke fails 30 times and triggers a false auto-rollback of a healthy deploy.

**Hujjat:** docs/07-infra-devops.md:212 — "smoke `SMOKE_URL` ga uradi — default manzil `.env` dagi `CADDY_HTTP_BIND` dan yig'iladi"

**Dalil:** deploy.sh:19 `BIND="$(grep -E '^CADDY_HTTP_BIND=' .env 2>/dev/null | tail -1 | cut -d= -f2- || true)"` — no quote or comment handling. deploy.sh:31-33 for the sibling value does exactly that handling: `SITE="$(grep -E '^SITE_ADDRESS=' .env ...)"`, `SITE="${SITE#\"}"`, `SITE="${SITE%\"}"`. Quoting is an established convention in this very file — .env.example:84 `VITE_API_URL="http://localhost:8000"`. With `CADDY_HTTP_BIND="127.0.0.1:8200"` the `case "$BIND" in *:*)` branch at deploy.sh:21 leaves the quotes in, giving `SMOKE_URL=http://"127.0.0.1:8200"/api/v1/ready`; smoke() (deploy.sh:49-57) then loops 30×5s and returns 1, and deploy.sh:100-107 rolls back a deploy that was fine.

**Tuzatish:** Extract one `env_get` helper used by both values that strips surrounding quotes (and, unlike scripts/backup.sh:27, strips only a leading `#` comment, not every `#`), then use it for `CADDY_HTTP_BIND` and `SITE_ADDRESS` alike.

### `scripts/deploy.sh:31` — HUJJAT DRIFTI

docs/07 describes the smoke URL as always HTTP built from `CADDY_HTTP_BIND`; deploy.sh has a second branch that switches to `https://<SITE_ADDRESS>/api/v1/ready` with `--resolve` when SITE_ADDRESS is a domain.

**Hujjat:** docs/07-infra-devops.md:212 — «scripts/deploy.sh — prod deploy avto-rollback bilan»

**Dalil:** DOC (07:212): "Smoke `SMOKE_URL` ga uradi — default manzil `.env` dagi `CADDY_HTTP_BIND` dan yig'iladi (`http://<bind>/api/v1/ready`; qiymat faqat port bo'lsa oldiga `127.0.0.1:` qo'shiladi, `.env` da yo'q bo'lsa `80`)"
CODE (scripts/deploy.sh:34-43): "case \"$SITE\" in\n    \"\"|:*)  # bo'sh yoki `:80` / `:8443` — oddiy HTTP, yo'naltirish yo'q\n        SMOKE_URL=\"${SMOKE_URL:-http://${BIND}/api/v1/ready}\" ... *)      # domen — TLS orqali, lekin baribir shu mashinada\n        SMOKE_URL=\"${SMOKE_URL:-https://${SITE}/api/v1/ready}\"\n        SMOKE_OPTS=\"--resolve ${SITE}:443:127.0.0.1\""

**Tuzatish:** Add the SITE_ADDRESS branch to docs/07:212: a domain value makes the smoke go over HTTPS pinned to 127.0.0.1 via `--resolve` (because Caddy 308-redirects :80 → :443 in domain mode), plus the `--connect-timeout 5 --max-time 10` guard.

### `scripts/deploy.sh:102` — BUG

The auto-rollback path re-runs `up -d` with the old image TAG but never re-runs `caddy reload`, so a rolled-back stack keeps the new Caddyfile that was loaded on the way in — the rollback restores code but not proxy config.

**Hujjat:** docs/07-infra-devops.md:212 — "`caddy reload` ... Reload qadami majburiy: `up -d` bind-mount qilingan faylning MAZMUNI o'zgarganini bilmaydi"

**Dalil:** Forward path, deploy.sh:78: `if ! $COMPOSE exec -T caddy caddy reload --config /etc/caddy/Caddyfile --adapter caddyfile; then` — with the doc's own justification that `up -d` cannot pick up bind-mount content changes. Rollback path, deploy.sh:100-107: `if [ -n "$STABLE" ]; then ... TAG="$STABLE" $COMPOSE up -d; if smoke; then ...` — no reload, and the on-disk `caddy/Caddyfile` is already the new one (CI rsyncs the whole tree before calling this script, .github/workflows/ci.yml:134-139). If the new Caddyfile is what broke the smoke, the rollback provably cannot fix it and lands in deploy.sh:106 "ROLLBACK SMOKE HAM YIQILDI".

**Tuzatish:** Either add the same `caddy reload` after the rollback `up -d` (with `git`/backup copy of the previous Caddyfile), or move the reload after a successful smoke so a failed deploy never leaves a half-applied proxy config. At minimum, say in the rollback message that Caddyfile/compose/scripts are NOT rolled back.

### `scripts/generate-client.sh:12` — O'LIK KOD

The final line of generate-client.sh formats zero files: `frontend/biome.json` explicitly excludes `src/client`, so biome reports "No files were processed in the specified paths" and the generated client is never formatted.

**Hujjat:** docs/07-infra-devops.md:208 — "oxirida `npx biome format --write ./src/client`"

**Dalil:** scripts/generate-client.sh:12 — `npx biome format --write ./src/client`
frontend/biome.json:8-18 — `"files": { "includes": [ "**", ... "!**/src/client/**/*", ... ] }`
Ran it from frontend/: `npx --no-install biome format ./src/client` ->
  "Checked 0 files in 2ms. No fixes applied."
  "x No files were processed in the specified paths."
  "i These paths were provided but ignored: - ./src/client"

**Tuzatish:** Delete the line (openapi-ts output is already deterministic and the repo treats src/client as generated), or if formatting is genuinely wanted, run it with an explicit override such as `npx biome format --write --files-ignore-unknown=true --config-path=<none> ./src/client`. Then drop the step from docs/07-infra-devops.md:208.

### `scripts/setup.sh:4` — BUG

`setup.sh` and `generate-client.sh` assume the caller's cwd is the repo root, while their two siblings `deploy.sh` and `backup.sh` both normalise cwd first. Run either of the first two directly (they are mode 100755, so `./scripts/setup.sh` from inside `scripts/` is a natural thing to do) and every relative path resolves wrong.

**Hujjat:** docs/07-infra-devops.md:194 — "Barcha `scripts/*.sh` fayllar git'da **executable (mode 100755)** saqlanadi" (i.e. all five are meant to be runnable directly)

**Dalil:** scripts/deploy.sh:12 — `cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."`
scripts/backup.sh:16 — `cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."`
scripts/setup.sh:1-4 — `#!/bin/bash` / `# Exit on error` / `set -e`  (no cd)
  then setup.sh:16 `if [ ! -f .env ]; then` and setup.sh:23 `uv --project backend sync` — both cwd-relative
scripts/generate-client.sh:1-6 — `#! /usr/bin/env bash` / `set -e` / `set -x` / `cd backend`  (no cd to root)
From any cwd but the repo root, setup.sh skips the `.env` copy (no `.env.example` visible) and then dies on `uv --project backend sync`; generate-client.sh dies on `cd backend`.

**Tuzatish:** Add `cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."` right after `set -e` in both scripts, matching deploy.sh:12 and backup.sh:16.

## infra

| Joy | Tur | Nima | Tuzatish |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `.gitignore:8` | O'LIK KOD | The `**/lib/` + `**/lib64/` + `!frontend/src/lib/` trio nets to zero: the only `lib/` directory the repo actually tracks is `frontend/src/lib/`, which the third line immediately re-includes; virtualenv `lib/` dirs are already covered by the | Delete all three lines. `.venv`, `backend/.venv`, `venv/` and `env/` (lines 15-18) already cover every real virtualenv `lib`/`lib64`, and the shadcn exception then needs no rule at all. Update docs/07-infra-devops.md:497 accordingly. |
| `.gitignore:21` | O'LIK KOD | The coverage ignore rules under "# Testing & Linting" are dead: commit 683e47e removed the entire test suite, and no coverage tool exists anywhere in the project. The docs still list `.coverage` as a live artifact. | Drop `.coverage`, `.coverage.*`, `htmlcov/` and rename the section comment to `# Linting cache`; update the docs sentence at 07-infra-devops.md:497 to stop listing `.coverage`. |
| `.pre-commit-config.yaml:23` | TOZALASH | `args: [--fix]` is redundant — `backend/pyproject.toml` already sets `fix = true` globally — and its presence hides the fact that CI's plain `ruff check app` gets fixing behaviour implicitly (see the ci.yml:20 finding). | Remove `fix = true` from `[tool.ruff]` and keep the explicit `args: [--fix]` here — that makes fixing opt-in per call site and lets CI/`make lint` be honest checks without needing `--no-fix`. |
| `Makefile:13` | TOZALASH | The `setup` target recurses with a literal `make` instead of `$(MAKE)`, so the sub-invocation loses the parent's jobserver and command-line flags (`-n`, `-k`, `-j`). | Use `@$(MAKE) install-hooks`, or drop the recursion entirely by declaring `setup: install-hooks` as a prerequisite / inlining `uv --project backend run pre-commit install`. |
| `Makefile:90` | BUG | `make docker-logs` always reads `docker-compose.local.yml`, so after `make docker-prod` it silently omits the `caddy` service — the one component whose logs matter for TLS/proxy problems in production. | Split into `docker-logs` (local file) and `docker-prod-logs` (`docker compose -f docker-compose.prod.yml logs -f`), mirroring the existing `docker-local` / `docker-prod` pair, and list both in docs/07-infra-devops.md:72. |
| `caddy/Caddyfile:10` | TOZALASH | The security-header block ships `X-XSS-Protection`, a header every current browser ignores and that OWASP recommends removing, while `Referrer-Policy` and `Content-Security-Policy` — the two that would actually apply to this app — are absen | Drop `X-XSS-Protection`, add `Referrer-Policy strict-origin-when-cross-origin`, and add a `Content-Security-Policy` starting from `default-src 'self'` plus whatever the Vite bundle needs for inline styles — then document the block in docs/0 |
| `scripts/prestart.sh:13` | O'LIK KOD | The `alembic.ini not found ... skipping migrations` fallback is unreachable from either of the script's two call sites, and if it ever were reached it would boot the API against an unmigrated schema with exit 0. | Drop the guard and run `python -m alembic upgrade head` unconditionally so a missing alembic.ini is a hard start failure instead of a silent skip; simplify docs/07-infra-devops.md:202 to match. |

## Metod

Uch bosqichli audit, 105 ta subagent:

| Bosqich | Agent | Token |
| --- | ---: | ---: |
| backend | 48 | 7 754 947 |
| frontend + infra | 46 | 7 506 630 |
| domen hujjatlari (09/10/11) | 10 | 1 417 614 |
| **jami** | **104** | **16 679 191** |

Xom topilma 1005 → adversarial tekshiruvdan keyin 969 → mexanik darvozadan
keyin 918 (900 to'liq iqtibosli + 18 iqtibossiz). 51 tasi darvozadan o'tmadi:
ular YOLG'ON degani emas — qo'lda tekshirilgan 6 tasining 6 tasi ham haqiqiy
chiqdi, darvoza `...` bilan qisqartirilgan iqtibosni moslay olmagan xolos.

Alohida, modelsiz (skript bilan) tasdiqlangan natijalar — bularning aniqligi
100%, chunki ular bajarilgan:

- `alembic upgrade head` toza bazada muvaffaqiyatli (42 migratsiya, head
  `a7c419e0b3d5`) — 2026-08-18 auditidagi "toza bazada yiqiladi" da'vosi
  endi eskirgan.
- `alembic check` → 6 yetim jadval + 7 yetim ustun; `env.py` dagi
  `LEGACY_TABLES`/`LEGACY_COLUMNS` bilan filtrlanib CI ga ulandi.
- OpenAPI ↔ `docs/03` farqi: 97 endpointdan 95 tasi hujjatlangan; yetishmagani
  aynan `POST /bridge-agents/logs` va `GET /bridge-agents/{agent_id}/logs`.
- `ruff check app` `fix = true` sabab xatoni jimgina tuzatib exit 0 berardi —
  CI lint darvozasi ishlamayotgan edi (`--no-fix` bilan tuzatildi).
- SQLAlchemy autoflush: `db.execute(update(...))` kutayotgan INSERT ni o'zi
  flush qiladi, ya'ni `scanning.py` dagi `except IntegrityError` bloki
  erishib bo'lmaydigan.
